Together, these components provide explicit semantic, geometric, and obstacle-aware cues while preserving the underlying pretrained VLA architecture. We evaluate our framework on pretrained VLA model using the RoboPRO benchmark, including visual perturbation tests, and further validate it on real-world manipulation tasks. MOSAIC improves the success rate by 5.1% in cluttered scenes, and by 9.7% under visual perturbations, with gains of up to 25.0% in the most challenging environment. We will release our code and dataset upon acceptance at the following Github page: this https URL

</details>


### [38] [Runtime Safety and Emergency Stops on a Full-Size Humanoid: Deployment Lessons and a Case for Graduated Force Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15928)
*Max Conway,Yutong Zhang,Allen Devaraj,William Xie,Alessandro Roncone,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作描述了Unitree H1-2人形机器人开源自主栈的安全机制，并通过推倒站立机器人等实验发现急停触发后机器人会失去平衡而摔倒，基于此主张应分阶段授予机器人施力权限并明确责任主体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全尺寸人形机器人被考虑用于拆解电动汽车电池等危险工业作业，其安全性至关重要；现有安全机制在触发急停时可能导致机器人摔倒，在操作员或电池包附近时摔倒可能比触发急停的原因更危险，因此需要重新审视安全设计。

Method: 所有关节指令经过独立于控制软件的安全层，执行关节位置、速度和力矩限制，并在状态估计过期或硬接线急停被按下或断开时停止机器人；实验中将站立机器人推至失去平衡，使用模型预测下半身控制器测试其恢复能力，并观察安全层限制在正常行走中的触发情况。

Result: 模型预测下半身控制器从未成功恢复平衡，每次试验操作员都需按下急停；按下急停会移除平衡控制，机器人随即摔倒；为操作任务设定的安全限制在正常行走中即被超出。

Conclusion: 关节限制只能约束机器人施加的力的大小，而无法判断何时施力是恰当的；借鉴责任研究与儿童青少年发展研究，主张分阶段授予机器人施力权限，并由个人或组织明确承担其使用责任。

Abstract: Full-size humanoid robots are being considered for hazardous industrial work such as disassembling electric-vehicle batteries. We describe the safety mechanisms of an open-source autonomy stack for the Unitree H1-2 humanoid and what deploying them taught us. All joint commands pass through a safety layer that runs separately from the control software. It enforces joint position, velocity, and torque limits, and it stops the robot when a state estimate becomes stale or when the hard-wired emergency stop (e-stop) is pressed or disconnected. We pushed the standing robot until it lost balance. With a model-predictive lower-body controller it never recovered, and the operator had to press the e-stop in every trial. Pressing it removes balance control, and the robot falls. The safety layer's own limits trigger the same stop, and limits chosen for manipulation were exceeded during normal walking. Near an operator or a battery pack, the fall can be more dangerous than the cause of the stop. Joint limits bound how much force a robot can apply, but not when applying it is appropriate. Drawing on research on responsibility and on child and adolescent development, we therefore make a case for granting robots force authority in stages, with a person or organization explicitly responsible for its use.

</details>


### [39] [RUMI: A Robotiq-Matched Universal Manipulation Interface for Robot Learning with Augmented Reality and Haptic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15802)
*Kevin Avdiu,Seongjin Bien,Yannik Blei,Philine Meister,Johannes Hechtl,Philipp Schmitt,Kai Wurm,Georg von Wichert,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出手持式通用操作接口RUMI，匹配Robotiq 2F-85夹爪并集成可换指尖传感器，结合VR增强现实反馈与触觉反馈，支持力感知遥操作与示教数据采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模力感知数据集稀缺，现有手持接口多针对简单线性夹爪，无法匹配常用机器人夹爪的非线性力学特性。

Method: 设计RUMI手持接口，保持目标夹爪几何形状，接触前自然输入、接触后测量力；将跟踪系统集成到VR头显中提供运动学可行性实时AR反馈；引入触觉反馈机制支持仿真与真实机器人的力感知遥操作；用两种指尖模态采集示教数据训练模仿策略。

Result: 用户研究中带实时机器人可视化与位姿初始化的关节限位违规episode为3/50，无AR时为16/50；成功利用两种指尖模态的示教数据训练模仿策略，并评估了触觉接口在仿真中采集力感知遥操作数据集的能力。

Conclusion: RUMI通过夹爪匹配的硬件与AR、触觉反馈，有效减少关节限位违规并支持力感知示教数据采集，为大规模力感知数据集构建提供可行方案。

Abstract: Many manipulation tasks require carefully controlled contact forces for successful execution, yet large-scale, force-aware datasets remain scarce. Handheld interfaces can help by transmitting interaction forces directly through the operator's arm, but most existing designs are tailored to simple linear grippers and do not match the nonlinear mechanics of widely used robotic grippers. We present RUMI, a handheld universal manipulation interface matched to the Robotiq 2F-85 with exchangeable fingertip sensors that preserves the target gripper geometry while enabling natural input before contact and force measurement after contact. By integrating the tracking system into a virtual reality headset, our method provides real-time augmented reality feedback about the kinematic feasibility of motion on the target robot platform. We further introduce a haptic feedback mechanism that supports force-aware teleoperation for simulated and real robots. A user study shows fewer episodes containing joint-limit violations with live robot visualization and pose initialization (3/50) than without augmented reality (16/50). We additionally train imitation policies from demonstrations, collected using two different fingertip modalities enabled by the gripper-matched hardware, and assess the haptic interface's capability for collecting force-aware teleoperation datasets in simulation.

</details>


### [40] [Prediction-Error-Tightened MPC for Socially Compliant Crowd Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15781)
*Alessandro Febbraro,Federico Rollo,Marcello Farina,Luca Bascetta,Navvab Kashiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作扩展了MPC框架，集成障碍物与行人检测、Koopman运动预测及在线预测误差估计以提升拥挤环境导航安全性与舒适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在拥挤环境中自主导航需区分行人与其他障碍物、预测行人未来运动并处理不确定性，同时保持行人心理舒适。

Method: 在碰撞避免机制中集成障碍物与行人检测、基于Koopman的运动预测和在线预测误差估计；聚合邻近障碍物以减少碰撞避免约束数量；通过仿真研究评估预测误差裕度和语义感知聚合的计算收益。

Result: 在300种子闭环对比中，Markov残差收紧将物理接触运行比例从10.0%降至1.33%，平均旅行时间代价0.68秒；人群导航语义消融中，行人感知处理的机器人足迹/个人空间重叠时间占比为2.11%，而通用障碍物处理为22.84%。

Conclusion: 语义感知聚合与预测误差估计能显著降低碰撞风险并减少计算复杂度，同时保持可接受的旅行时间代价。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation of mobile robots in crowded environments, while preserving pedestrians' psychological comfort, requires distinguishing pedestrians from other obstacles, predicting their future motion, and accounting for uncertainty in the predicted trajectories of both pedestrians and the robot. This work extends a previous MPC framework by integrating obstacle and pedestrian detection, Koopman-based motion prediction, and online prediction-error estimation directly within the collision-avoidance mechanism. To reduce collision-avoidance complexity in crowded scenarios, nearby obstacles are aggregated, thereby reducing the number of collision-avoidance constraints. Simulation studies evaluate the prediction-error margins and the computational benefits of semantics-aware aggregation. In a paired 300-seed closed-loop comparison with a moving generic obstacle, Markov residual tightening reduces the fraction of runs with physical contact from $10.0\%$ to $1.33\%$, at a mean travel-time cost of $0.68$ s. In a separate crowd-navigation semantic ablation, the offline robot-footprint/personal-space overlap occupies $2.11\%$ of observed time with pedestrian-aware handling versus $22.84\%$ with generic-obstacle handling.

</details>


### [41] [Robot-Induced Bias in Movement Smoothness Metrics for Rehabilitation: Quantification and Correction in Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15743)
*David Regev,Alex Scherbakov,Eran Beeri Bamani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人机械特性会使运动学康复指标产生偏差，本研究通过闭环人-机器人仿真量化七项指标的偏差，并提出基于机器人动力学与梯度提升树的校正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人机械特性会偏倚运动学康复测量，限制不同康复设备间的比较。

Method: 构建校准至已发表健康与卒中受试者运动学的闭环人-机器人仿真；固定运动控制参数与运动计划，以低阻抗参考量化七项指标的偏差，并用外部重测信度基准归一化；采用Sobol分析识别主要贡献因素，并用梯度提升树结合机器人动力学与观测指标进行校正。

Result: 在设备规格相关子集中，卒中与健康剖面的LDLJ中位偏差分别为0.93与1.79基准单位；库仑摩擦与表观质量为主要贡献因素，偏差在11种稳健性变体中持续存在；校正后LDLJ中位归一化绝对误差由0.77降至0.10，SPARC由0.24降至0.08，四个独立留出参数区域残差≤0.15基准单位；替代控制器下LDLJ误差由1.60降至0.17且无需重训练，但跨指标迁移存在差异。

Conclusion: 仿真结果支持通过机械特性表征与运动依赖校正实现更可比的机器人评估，并值得在患者中进一步验证。

Abstract: Robot mechanics can bias kinematic measures of recovery and limit comparisons across rehabilitation devices. We developed a closed-loop human-robot simulation calibrated to published healthy and stroke group kinematics. Paired runs held motor-control parameters and movement plans fixed to quantify bias in seven metrics relative to a low-impedance reference, normalized using external repeatability benchmarks. Within a subset informed by published device specifications, median log dimensionless jerk (LDLJ) bias was 0.93 and 1.79 benchmark units for stroke and healthy profiles, respectively. Sobol analysis identified Coulomb friction and apparent mass as leading contributors; LDLJ bias persisted across 11 robustness variants. LDLJ bias increased as simulated movements became less fragmented. If recovery similarly reduces fragmentation, this effect could partly mask improvement in measured smoothness. Correction combining identified robot dynamics and observed metrics with gradient-boosted trees reduced median normalized absolute error from 0.77 to 0.10 for LDLJ and from 0.24 to 0.08 for spectral arc length (SPARC) in the device-informed subset of a random 20% hold-out. Median smoothness residuals remained at or below 0.15 benchmark units across four separately held-out parameter regions. Under an alternative controller, LDLJ error decreased from 1.60 to 0.17 without retraining, although transfer varied across metrics. These simulation results support mechanical characterization and movement-dependent correction toward more comparable robotic assessments, motivating validation in patients.

</details>


### [42] [What World Model Does Active Object Search Need? A Study under Imperfect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15689)
*Kyeongryeol Go*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BAMOS研究了在检测器不完美时，主动目标搜索所需的世界模型表示，发现目标信念及其更新机制对搜索性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不完美检测使机器人难以判断未检测到目标的原因（遮挡、视野外或漏检）以及检测是否为误报，因此需要明确世界模型应包含哪些表示来支持可靠搜索。

Method: 提出贝叶斯主动漏检感知目标搜索（BAMOS），在Isaac Sim和HSSD房屋中进行全面消融实验，考察目标信念、占据信念及检测器误差对搜索决策的影响及其更新机制。

Result: 目标信念及其更新机制对搜索性能至关重要，且随着检测错误增多，其贡献通常增大；占据信念等表示的作用相对次要。

Conclusion: 在不确定检测观测下，维护和更新目标信念是实现可靠主动搜索的关键，为具身智能体世界模型设计提供了启示。

Abstract: In active object search, a robot must decide where to look and when to stop. Imperfect detection complicates both decisions: a non-detection may indicate occlusion, an out-of-view target, or a false negative, while a detection may be a false positive. This raises the question of which representations a world model needs to support reliable search under imperfect detection. We introduce Bayesian Active Miss-aware Object Search (BAMOS) to investigate how target belief, occupancy belief, and detector errors affect search decisions. Through comprehensive ablations in Isaac Sim and HSSD houses, we examine the roles of these representations and their update mechanisms across different detector configurations. Notably, target belief and its update mechanisms are critical to search performance, with their contribution generally increasing as detection errors become more prevalent. These findings highlight the importance of maintaining and updating target beliefs to support reliable active search under uncertain detector observations, providing insights into the design of world models for embodied agents. Code is available in this https URL .

</details>


### [43] [GraSLA: A Lightweight Spatial-Language-Action Model with Grasp Hypotheses as Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15659)
*Jibao Yuan,Zixiang Zhao,Wensong Xu,Siqi Lu,Shaofei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GraSLA模型，将6-DoF抓取假设作为几何先验与空间-语言基础模型通过双向交叉注意力融合，实现连续动作预测，在LIBERO上达到99.30%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续动作模仿学习策略因缺乏多样化演示导致行为多样性不足；6-DoF抓取检测虽能提供多样抓取假设，但开环执行难以适应动态环境，故希望将抓取假设的多样性与连续动作策略的自适应性结合。

Method: 构建轻量级空间-语言-动作模型GraSLA：使用空间与语言基础模型分别编码场景几何和任务语义，再通过双向交叉注意力将二者与抓取假设融合，用于连续动作预测。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上取得99.30%的平均成功率。

Conclusion: 以抓取假设作为几何先验并结合空间-语言基础模型，可有效提升语言条件操作策略的行为多样性与有效性。

Abstract: This paper focuses on improving the behavioral diversity and effectiveness of language-conditioned manipulation policies trained through imitation learning. Recently, continuous-action policies have become mainstream in robotic manipulation, but the lack of diverse demonstrations tends to restrict their behavioral diversity. For grasping, a fundamental subtask of manipulation, 6-DoF grasp detection provides diverse grasp hypotheses, but open-loop execution constrains responsiveness in dynamic environments. This motivates us to combine the diversity of grasp hypotheses with the adaptability of continuous-action policies. To this end, we propose GraSLA, a lightweight and effective spatial-language-action model with grasp hypotheses as geometric priors. In particular, we employ spatial and language foundation models to encode scene geometry and task semantics, and integrate them with grasp hypotheses through bidirectional cross-attention for continuous action prediction. On LIBERO, GraSLA achieves an average success rate of 99.30%.

</details>


### [44] [Embedded Sim-to-Real Visual Servoing: Learned Reaching and Force-Guided Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15658)
*Dezhi Sun,Duy Khang Banh,Kenan Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在Jetson AGX Orin上构建嵌入式感知到控制架构，结合视觉定位与力控接触处理，比较PPO策略与力引导解析放置策略的实物性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式感知到控制系统需在有限计算资源下将视觉目标定位与可靠接触处理相结合，现有方法难以兼顾。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin控制Franka Research 3，采用笛卡尔阻抗控制；用RealSense D435i、YOLO11-seg分割、深度反投影和手眼标定获取机器人坐标系目标；对比从仿真迁移的紧凑PPO策略（物体与孔区域到达）和约2 N法向接触加螺旋搜索的力引导笛卡尔放置。

Result: PPO策略各1000个仿真回合成功率87.2%和83.1%；每种策略50次实物试验中，混合学习管线与力引导解析管线完成率分别为70.0%和74.0%；TensorRT平均感知延迟34.55 ms。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种将学习到的自由空间到达与显式接触修正相结合的嵌入式架构。

Abstract: Embedded perception-to-control systems must combine visual targeting with reliable contact handling under tight computational constraints. We present a shared architecture on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin controlling a Franka Research 3 through Cartesian impedance control. RealSense D435i observations, YOLO11-seg segmentation, depth back-projection, and hand--eye calibration provide metric robot-frame targets. We compare two strategies: compact proximal policy optimization (PPO) policies transferred from simulation for object- and hole-region reaching, and force-guided Cartesian placement using approximately 2 N normal contact and spiral search. The PPO policies achieved 87.2% and 83.1% success over 1000 simulation episodes each. Across 50 real-robot trials per strategy, the learned hybrid and force-guided analytical pipelines achieved 70.0% and 74.0% completion, respectively. Mean perception latency with TensorRT was 34.55 ms. These results support an embedded architecture combining learned free-space reaching with explicit contact correction.

</details>


### [45] [Recover, Don't Just Continue: Learning from Feedback for Physical Failure Understanding and Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15651)
*Miao Pan,Kaixiang Yao,Xiaohe Zhou,Xu Wang,Qinfeng Li,Wenqi Zhang,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Embodied-Corrector，通过注入扰动构建失败恢复数据，采用评估优先的SFT和OPD训练，使多模态大模型具备因果失败定位、能力约束适应与经验驱动修正能力，冻结VLA下复合任务成功率提升6.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型被视为具身智能的认知基础，但仅凭指令规划不可靠：执行中动作会因具身形态与环境约束偏离预期，模型需理解失败原因、识别可可靠实现的能力边界并据交互经验修正决策，即因果失败定位、能力约束适应和经验驱动修正，而现有具身基础模型普遍缺乏这些能力。

Method: 在成功轨迹的子任务边界注入扰动、重放受影响子任务，保留可由公开证据观察且当前状态可恢复的失败，构建失败恢复数据；将动作、后果、失败原因与恢复对齐为Judge-Cause-Sub-Fine监督，采用评估优先的SFT先学Judge和Cause再学Sub和Fine。针对自生成前缀导致评估误差传播、响应级奖励过粗的问题，使用OPD将准确前缀下的规划分布内化到自生成前缀下：从自生成前缀生成计划，同时用准确前缀的对比前向传播比较token概率以强化或抑制规划token，部署时移除该分支。

Result: 失败分析与纠正规划能力更强：在冻结VLA时复合任务成功率提升6.1个百分点；在固定GPT-5.5执行时操作与导航任务提升13.9个百分点。

Conclusion: Embodied-Corrector通过失败恢复数据监督与OPD训练，使具身基础模型具备失败定位、能力约束适应与经验驱动修正能力，并在部署中显著提升任务成功率。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models are regarded as the cognitive foundation of embodied intelligence, but planning from instructions alone cannot make them reliable. During execution, actions deviate from intended effects, constrained by the embodiment and environment. An embodied brain must understand why execution deviates, identify what can be reliably realized, and revise decisions using interaction experience. These correspond to causal failure localization, capability-constrained adaptation, and experience-driven revision. Current embodied foundation models largely lack these capabilities. To address this, we introduce Embodied-Corrector. We construct failure-recovery data by injecting perturbations at subtask boundaries of successful trajectories, replaying affected subtasks, and keeping failures that are observable from public evidence and recoverable from the current state. We align actions, consequences, failure causes, and recoveries into Judge-Cause-Sub-Fine supervision. Training uses evaluation-first SFT: the model learns Judge and Cause first, then Sub and Fine conditioned on accurate prefixes. Since deployment uses self-generated prefixes, evaluation errors propagate into recovery planning, and response-level rewards are too coarse to locate affected planning tokens. We therefore use OPD to internalize the planning distribution under accurate prefixes into that under self-generated ones: the model generates plans from self-generated prefixes, while a contrastive forward pass with accurate prefixes compares token probabilities to reinforce or suppress planning tokens. At deployment, this branch is removed. Experiments show stronger failure analysis and corrective planning: with a frozen VLA, composite task success improves by 6.1 percentage points; with fixed GPT-5.5 execution, manipulation and navigation tasks improve by 13.9 percentage points.

</details>


### [46] [Single-View RGB-D Force--Deformation Estimation for Robotic Manipulation of Elasto-Plastic Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15650)
*Dezhi Sun,Marijn van Hilten,Kenan Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文利用标准机器人、单目RGB-D相机和内在力估计研究力-变形关系，为考虑变形的操作建立测量基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续变形使弹塑性操作复杂化，因为物理交互与空间分布的形状变化耦合，需要实用的力-变形测量方法。

Method: 7自由度Franka Emika FR3执行法向压痕，Intel RealSense L515捕捉接触前后表面；点云处理定位变形并估计位移体积；在九个力水平上共27次测量；用四个水平上的52对力-体积数据训练紧凑MLP。

Result: MLP在留出集上RMSE为1.857 N、R²=0.440；16 N时二次模型误差更低，19和23.5 N时MLP更低；每次4 N的八步实验从第二次压痕起展示累积变形定位。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了使用内在力估计和单视图几何进行变形感知操作的实用测量基础。

Abstract: Persistent deformation complicates elasto-plastic manipulation by coupling physical interaction with spatially distributed shape changes. We investigate the force--deformation relationship using a standard robot, a single eye-in-hand RGB-D camera, and intrinsic force estimates. A 7-DoF Franka Emika FR3 performs normal indentations while an Intel RealSense L515 captures pre- and post-contact surfaces. Point-cloud processing localizes deformation and estimates displaced volume. Force--volume characterization includes 27 measurements over nine force levels. A compact multilayer perceptron (MLP), trained on 52 force--volume pairs at four levels, achieves a held-out RMSE of 1.857 N and $R^2=0.440$. A separate comparison shows lower error for a quadratic model at 16 N and for the MLP at 19 and 23.5 N. An eight-step experiment at 4 N per interaction demonstrates cumulative deformation localization from the second indentation onward. These results establish a practical measurement basis for deformation-aware manipulation using intrinsic force estimates and single-view geometry.

</details>


### [47] [Helping Hand: A Continuum Robotic Grasper for Traction in Single-Channel Endoscopic Submucosal Dissection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15638)
*Bendavid Maimon,Thompson Wong,Aedan Mangan,Sukjun Kim,Michael C. Yip,Tania K. Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双段连续体机器人抓持器Helping Hand，与电刀共用单通道实现ESD中的组织牵引与切割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESD微创但操作难度高，医生难以广泛采用，需解决组织牵引与切割工具占用工作通道的问题。

Method: 设计Helping Hand双段连续体抓持器，底部开槽使电刀从同一3.7 mm通道伸出，评估工作空间及不同初始构型下的牵引力，并在离体鸡腿中演示完整流程。

Result: Helping Hand可在不同初始构型下提供牵引力，并在单一通道内完成组织隔离与切除的完整流程演示。

Conclusion: Helping Hand有潜力改善ESD手术的可操作性与推广。

Abstract: Endoscopic submucosal dissection (ESD) is a minimally invasive approach for removing large polyps from the colon and offers many benefits compared to other surgical approaches. However, ESD is technically very challenging to perform, leading to limited adoption by physicians. We propose the Helping Hand, a two-segment continuum robotic grasper that, together with an electrosurgical knife, enables tissue traction and cutting within a single 3.7 mm working channel. We achieve this functionality by creating a slot at the base of the Helping Hand that lets the electrosurgical knife extend out, enabling countertraction and cutting through one instrument lumen. We evaluate the Helping Hand's workspace and its ability to provide traction forces from different starting configurations. Finally, we demonstrate the full workflow by isolating and removing tissue in an ex vivo chicken thigh, illustrating the potential for the Helping Hand to help improve ESD procedures.

</details>


### [48] [EMS-Nav: Embodied Multi-Scale Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15589)
*Zenghui Yang,Xinan Wang,Danyang Li,Zhe Xu,Zheng Yang,Jingao Xu,Qiang Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMS-Nav是一种受生物启发的多尺度导航框架，以区域为基本单元组织感知、空间理解与导航，在保持高性能的同时降低计算与存储开销，支持资源受限机器人的在线端侧部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业巡检机器人需跨越从区域到物体的多空间尺度到达目标，必须将目标与周围环境关联以理解任务并规划路径；现有方法依赖丰富的环境表示实现多尺度导航，但计算和存储成本高，在无线覆盖不完整、只能依赖艇载计算的场景下难以部署。

Method: 提出EMS-Nav框架，核心创新是将感知、空间理解和导航围绕区域组织，使局部空间成为环境理解与计算的基本单元；将物体及其关系归入功能上有意义的空间，并构建从局部区域到更大环境的层级结构，从而支持高效地图构建以及不同尺度语言目标的解释与执行。

Result: 在多尺度导航实验中，EMS-Nav在物体、房间、区域和特定实例四类目标上均取得最高成功率，优于最先进的开词汇建图与导航方法，首次尝试总体成功率比最强基线提升9.39个百分点，同时降低建图开销和语言模型输入规模；真实室内环境的本地部署进一步验证了其端侧具身导航的适用性。

Conclusion: 将区域确立为感知、空间理解和语言引导行动的组织基础，EMS-Nav实现了多尺度理解与计算效率的结合，能够在资源受限机器人上支持多样化语言目标。

Abstract: Industrial inspection robots must reach work areas and specific equipment. These destinations span spatial scales from regions to objects, so robots must relate each target to its surroundings to interpret the task and determine where to go. Existing methods support multi-scale navigation through rich environment representations, but at substantial computational and storage cost. These costs limit deployment where wireless coverage is incomplete and robots must rely on onboard computation to keep navigating. We introduce EMS-Nav, a biologically inspired multi-scale navigation framework for online, on-device operation. Its core innovation is to organize perception, spatial understanding, and navigation around regions, making local spaces the basic units of both environmental understanding and computation. EMS-Nav groups objects and their relationships into functionally meaningful spaces and builds a hierarchy from local areas to the broader environment. This organization supports efficient map construction alongside the interpretation and execution of language goals at different scales. In multi-scale navigation experiments, EMS-Nav achieves the highest success rates across all four target categories: objects, rooms, regions, and specific instances. It outperforms state-of-the-art open-vocabulary mapping and navigation methods, improving overall first-attempt success by 9.39 percentage points over the strongest baseline while reducing mapping overhead and language-model input size. Local deployment in real indoor environments further demonstrates its applicability to on-device embodied navigation. By establishing regions as the organizing basis for perception, spatial understanding, and language-guided action, EMS-Nav provides a system that combines multi-scale understanding with computational efficiency to support diverse language goals on resource-constrained robots.

</details>


### [49] [Generalized Inverse Kinematics via Conditional Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15565)
*Kaitlyn Zhang,Tomás Lozano-Pérez,Leslie Pack Kaelbling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GenGenIK是一种生成式逆运动学方法，支持移动基座、多末端执行器、场景感知和机器人行为偏好，在求解率和运行时间上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆运动学将每个查询视为孤立的位姿到配置问题，但规划器通常需要有效实现的分布，以考虑未来可达性以及避碰和稳定性等偏好。

Method: 提出GenGenIK生成式建模方法，在统一接口下支持移动基座、多末端执行器和目标、场景感知以及对机器人行为的偏好。

Result: 实验表明，GenGenIK在双臂举升和翻转等任务上，求解率和运行时间均优于现有方法，同时允许对采样分布进行灵活控制，并在部分场景观测下保持有效。

Conclusion: GenGenIK通过生成式建模支持多样化逆运动学需求，提升了求解性能并增强了对场景部分观测的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Inverse kinematics (IK) is a foundational primitive in robotics, mapping the task-space objectives expressed by higher-level planners to robot configurations that realize them. Traditional IK methods generally treat each query as an isolated pose-to-configuration problem, but planners often need a distribution over valid realizations that accounts for future reachability and preferences such as collision avoidance and stability. We introduce GenGenIK, a generative modeling approach that supports mobile bases, multiple end-effectors and targets, scene awareness, and preferences over robot behavior within a common interface. Experiments show that GenGenIK outperforms existing methods in both solve rate and runtime across tasks such as bimanual lift and flip-up, while allowing flexible control over the sampled distribution and remaining effective under partial scene observations. Website: this https URL .

</details>


### [50] [Reading the Table: Sim-to-Real Game State Detection for the Chef's Hat Card Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15524)
*Connor Gäde,Matthias Kerzel,Hauke Bünning,Jan-Gerrit Habekost,Mariana Barros,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出全自动合成数据采集管道，通过3D坐标重投影自动标注和域随机化缩小现实差距，微调检测与分割模型，在真实NICO人形机器人上实现卡牌到游戏区域分配F1分数0.906，为机器人自主玩桌游奠定感知基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桌游是研究人机交互的理想场景，但机器人需精确检测高度相似的游戏对象（如不同卡牌设计），而真实世界标注专用训练数据成本高昂。

Method: 开发全自动合成数据采集管道：利用已知3D坐标重投影自动生成标签，并采用域随机化弥合现实差距；采集25413个样本微调目标检测和分割模型，将两者结合把检测到的卡牌分配到游戏相关区域并推断游戏状态。

Result: 在真实NICO人形机器人上评估，卡牌到区域分配的F1分数达到0.906。

Conclusion: 该管道为机器人自主与人类玩家玩Chef's Hat游戏提供了感知基础，验证了合成数据管道在桌游场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Tabletop games offer a compelling scenario to study the interaction between artificial and human agents, combining social interaction with game state tracking and physical manipulation. To assess the game state, a robot needs to precisely detect closely related game objects, such as different card designs, requiring dedicated training data that is costly to annotate in the real world. Instead, we develop a fully automated synthetic data collection pipeline, using re-projection of known 3D coordinates to generate labels automatically and domain randomization to bridge the reality gap. This approach is used to collect a dataset of 25413 samples to finetune an object detection and a segmentation model, which are combined to assign detected cards to game-relevant zones and infer the game state. When evaluated on a real NICO humanoid robot, our pipeline achieves an F1-score of 0.906 for card-to-zone assignment, thus providing the perceptual foundation for a robot that can autonomously play Chef's Hat against human players.

</details>


### [51] [ActionCodec2: A Streaming Action Tokenizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15490)
*Zibin Dong,Yicheng Liu,Shiduo Zhang,Tianyuan Yuan,Baijun Ye,Jinqiu Wang,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActionCodec2 是一种非神经的流式动作分词器，按容差增量释放可执行动作，在仿真与真实机器人上优于对比分词器，并支持闭环状态反馈与 VLM 智能体复用宏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归 VLA 模型的动作分词器把运动压缩成必须整体解码后才能执行的动作块，无法在动作块内及时反馈状态，也难以作为 VLM 智能体的直接控制接口。

Method: 编码器将每一步相对解码器重建状态进行量化，使所选运动基元修正残差而非累积误差；最小基元集被建模为几何覆盖问题，给出平移的精确最小值与旋转的常数因子紧界；再用 Set-BPE 跨坐标和相邻步无损合并共现基元符号，不依赖固定坐标顺序；并在 24 种具身、2 万小时数据上拟合通用版本。

Result: 通用版本在该语料 15 Hz 末端执行器运动上平均每秒 8.8 个 token；相同 VLA 骨干下在三个仿真基准和零样本真机评估中优于对比分词器；流式表示使策略能在动作块内引入状态反馈，在随机动力学、外部扰动和移动物体下明显优于开环执行；同一 token 可作为 VLM 智能体的直接控制接口，智能体构建的宏减少控制决策与思考 token 用量并可迁移到留出任务。

Conclusion: 相对状态量化加无损符号合并的流式分词可在容差内增量释放可执行动作，兼顾重建精度与 token 效率，并同时支持闭环控制与智能体宏复用。

Abstract: Most action tokenizers for autoregressive vision language action (VLA) models compress motion into chunks that must be decoded in full before their actions become executable. We introduce ActionCodec2, a non-neural streaming tokenizer that releases actions incrementally while keeping trajectory reconstruction error within a prescribed tolerance. Its encoder quantizes each step relative to the decoder's reconstructed state, so the selected motion primitive corrects residual error rather than allowing errors from independently quantized increments to accumulate. Finding the smallest such primitive set is a geometric covering problem; we derive the exact minimum for translation and constant-factor-tight bounds for rotation. Set-BPE then losslessly merges co-occurring primitive symbols across coordinates and neighboring steps, without a fixed coordinate ordering. A universal version fitted on 20k hours of data from 24 embodiments averages 8.8 tokens per second for 15 Hz end-effector motion on this corpus. With the same VLA backbone, ActionCodec2 outperforms the compared tokenizers on three simulation benchmarks and in zero-shot real-robot evaluation. Its streaming representation allows policies to incorporate state feedback within an action chunk, substantially improving performance over open-loop execution under randomized dynamics, external disturbances, and moving objects. The same tokens also can serve as a direct control interface for VLM agents, which compose successful action sequences into reusable macros. These agent-built macros reduce control decisions and thinking-token usage across attempts and transfer to a held-out task. More results are available at this https URL

</details>


### [52] [FloCOS: Predicting Compact Forward Occupancy Sets of Surrounding Vehicles via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15488)
*Chuansheng Dong,Yinxiao Zhan,Zongdian Li,Peng Hou,Jin Wang,Jiayu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FloCOS用条件流匹配预测紧凑且高覆盖的前向占用集，提升轨迹规划安全性与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前向占用预测方法要么低估真实占用导致碰撞风险，要么过度保守限制可行空间造成规划失败。

Method: 将前向占用集表示为随时间变化的半空间凸多边形并集，用时变基系数参数化；用条件流匹配学习系数分布以控制凸分量演化，并以覆盖损失和并集面积损失训练；预测的非凸并集直接嵌入基于超平面的避碰约束用于下游轨迹优化。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上，FloCOS生成的占用集紧凑且覆盖率高，交互完成率优于对比基线；真实车辆实验验证其支持交互场景下的无碰撞规划。

Conclusion: FloCOS能在保证高占用覆盖的同时保持占用集紧凑，有效支撑安全且可行的交互式轨迹规划。

Abstract: Existing methods for predicting surrounding vehicles' forward occupancy may under-approximate the ground-truth occupancy, exposing the ego vehicle to collision risk, or produce overly conservative regions that unnecessarily restrict feasible space and cause planning failures. To address these issues, we propose FloCOS, a conditional flow-matching framework that predicts compact forward occupancy sets with high occupancy coverage. FloCOS represents the forward occupancy set as a time-varying union of half-space convex polygons parameterized by temporal basis coefficients. Conditional flow matching captures the conditional distribution of these coefficients to govern the temporal evolution of the convex components, trained under coverage and union-area losses that penalize missed occupancy and excess area. For downstream trajectory optimization, the predicted nonconvex union is directly incorporated into hyperplane-based collision-avoidance constraints. Evaluations on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset show that FloCOS generates compact forward occupancy sets with high coverage and achieves a higher interaction completion rate than evaluated baselines. Real-world vehicle experiments further confirm its ability to support collision-free planning in interactive scenarios.

</details>


### [53] [SPARC: RoboCup Standard Platform for Autonomous Rescue Competition with Initial Datasets, Software, and Simulation Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15443)
*Yuezhong Wang,Bichi Zhang,Zhongli Yin,Pingcong Li,Raimund Edlinger,Sören Schwertfeger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARC 是基于 Unitree A2-W 标准平台的新型自主救援机器人竞赛，论文介绍其赛事、基准环境、开源数据集、导航软件栈与仿真资产，旨在降低自主救援机器人研究门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主救援机器人研究常受限于定制硬件开发与维护的高门槛，许多研究团队缺乏相关经验，难以专注于人工智能算法本身。

Method: 构建以 Unitree A2-W 为通用平台的 SPARC 竞赛，提供标准基准环境；发布 ICRA 2026 腿足机器人挑战赛与 RoboCup Rescue 2026 期间采集的首批开放数据集；开源自主导航软件栈；提供对应 RoboCup Rescue 测试环境的初始仿真资产。

Result: 形成涵盖竞赛平台、基准环境、开放数据集、开源导航软件栈和仿真资产的完整资源体系，支持自主导航、感知、操作与具身 AI 研究。

Conclusion: SPARC 通过标准硬件与共享资源降低自主救援机器人领域的进入门槛，促进可复现研究与协同开发。

Abstract: The RoboCup Standard Platform for Autonomous Rescue Competition (SPARC) is a new competition under development of the RoboCup Rescue Robot League that uses the Unitree A2-W as a common robot platform. SPARC aims to make research on artificial intelligence for autonomous rescue robots accessible to a broader research community, including groups without expertise in developing and maintaining custom robot hardware. By providing a capable standard platform and established benchmark environments, researchers can focus on autonomous navigation, perception, manipulation, and embodied AI for challenging disaster-response scenarios. This paper introduces the SPARC competition, its benchmark environment, and the Unitree A2-W standard platform. Furthermore, we present the first open SPARC datasets collected during the IEEE ICRA 2026 Legged Robot Challenges and RoboCup Rescue 2026, release an open-source software stack for autonomous navigation, and provide initial simulation assets representing RoboCup Rescue test environments. Together, these resources aim to lower the barrier to entry for autonomous rescue robotics and facilitate reproducible research and collaborative development on a common hardware platform.

</details>


### [54] [Actuation and Control of Passive Tendon-Driven Soft Extensions Through Rigid Manipulator Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15386)
*Ignacio Cuiral-Zueco,Gonzalo López-Nicolás,Youcef Mezouar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出通过机械臂关节运动驱动被动肌腱软延伸，并设计控制器在末端执行器约束下调节软体尖端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机械臂可达性和精度高但在狭小空间易损伤结构，软体机器人接触柔顺但可达范围有限，刚软结合系统常需专用驱动硬件。

Method: 利用锚定在上游连杆的肌腱外部跨度随机械臂运动变化来改变张力并使软延伸变形；建立微分模型分离刚性运输与软变形，结合解析臂与肌腱运动学及局部辨识的跨度-变形映射；推导末端约束下肌腱传递独立变形方向数的局部上界及尖端误差指数收敛条件；采用带控制屏障函数的二次规划施加障碍物间隙、支点限制和模型有效性条件。

Result: 仿真和真实实验在自由、位置约束和支点约束的末端执行器运动下实现了尖端和形状调节。

Conclusion: 通过机械臂关节运动被动驱动软延伸可在保持末端执行器约束的同时实现尖端与形状控制，无需专用驱动硬件。

Abstract: Industrial manipulators offer reach and precision but can damage nearby structures in confined spaces. Soft robots provide compliant contact but typically have limited reach. Rigid-soft systems combine their advantages, although they often require dedicated actuation and control hardware. We propose actuating a passive tendon-driven soft extension through manipulator joint motion. As the robot moves, the exterior spans of tendons anchored to upstream links change, altering tendon tension and deforming the extension. Inspection through narrow apertures, for example, constrains the motion of the end effector (EE). We develop a controller that regulates the soft tip while preserving such EE constraints. A differential model separates rigid transport from soft deformation, combining analytic arm and tendon kinematics with a locally identified span-to-deformation map. We also derive a local upper bound on the number of independent deformation directions transmitted by the tendons under the EE constraint, and conditions for the exponential convergence of the tip error. A quadratic program with control barrier functions imposes obstacle clearance, fulcrum limits, and model-validity conditions. Simulations and real experiments show tip and shape regulation under free, position-constrained, and fulcrum-constrained EE motion.

</details>


### [55] [Low-Cost Mobile Bimanual Manipulation for Long-Horizon Toilet Cleaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15381)
*Sven Schreiber,Bernard Burdiek,Lasse Huber-Saffer,Yung-Hui Li,Donglin Chen,Shang-Ching Liu,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 构建低成本可复现的移动双臂平台（两台SO-101机械臂+TurtleBot 4），以长程清洁马桶为案例采集250条真实演示，在统一具身与任务条件下评测多种VLA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作上潜力显著，但在易获取的移动双臂平台上的表现尚未被充分探索，需要验证低成本硬件上长程移动双臂操作的可行性。

Method: 集成两台LeRobot SO-101机械臂、TurtleBot 4与LeRobot-ROS 2软件栈搭建低成本可复现平台；采集250条真实世界演示；在相同具身与任务条件下评测π0、π0.5、SmolVLA和GR00T，并额外评测引入SARM任务进度信号与短期MEM观测历史的变体。

Result: π0表现最佳，严格成功率60%、宽松成功率70%，有限人工干预下可提升至90%；而进度与记忆增强变体相对各自基线均未带来提升。

Conclusion: 结果证明了在易获取硬件上开展长程移动双臂操作的可行性，同时揭示了可靠部署VLA模型仍面临的挑战。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising capabilities for robot manipulation, but their performance on accessible mobile bimanual platforms remains underexplored. We present a low-cost and reproducible mobile bimanual platform integrating two LeRobot SO-101 manipulators with a TurtleBot 4 and a LeRobot-ROS 2 software stack. Using long-horizon toilet cleaning as a case study, we collect 250 real-world demonstrations and evaluate $\pi_0$, $\pi_{0.5}$, SmolVLA, and GR00T under the same embodiment and task conditions. We further evaluate variants using SARM-derived task-progress signals and short-term MEM observation history. Among the evaluated models, $\pi_0$ performs best, achieving 60% strict and 70% relaxed autonomous success, increasing to 90% with limited human intervention. In contrast, the evaluated progress- and memory-augmented variants do not improve their corresponding baselines. Our results demonstrate the feasibility of long-horizon mobile bimanual manipulation on accessible hardware while highlighting remaining challenges in reliable VLA deployment. ### [56] [Bind-WAM: Binding-Uncertainty-Guided Selective Future Imagination in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15368)
*Chenhao Qiu,Shuyi Chen,Songen Gu,Shufeng Nan,Tianyu Wang,Siyu Lin,Yifan Li,Bingwen Zhu,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bind-WAM利用语言-物体绑定不确定性作为选择性未来想象的触发信号，在观测不支持稳定动作生成时才启用联合未来-动作预测，从而降低计算开销并提升机器人策略效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型每步重规划都生成未来视觉状态代价高昂且有时不必要，核心问题是机器人策略应在何时想象未来。

Method: 提出Bind-WAM，引入语言-物体绑定不确定性：离线用完整演示和动作上下文标注任务相关物体并检测参考失配、不可见和身份漂移，生成伪标签训练轻量路由器从当前观测和语言指令预测不确定性；推理时共享WAM策略依据预测不确定性及其相邻重规划步间变化选择性触发未来想象。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，绑定不确定性可作为强且可解释的选择性未来想象信号。

Conclusion: Bind-WAM确立了语言-物体绑定不确定性作为选择性未来想象的有效、可解释触发机制，实现按需未来生成。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) can improve robot learning by jointly predicting future visual states and actions, but generating a visual future at every replanning step is expensive and sometimes unnecessary. This raises a critical question: when should a robot policy imagine the future? In this paper, we propose Bind-WAM, a binding-uncertainty-guided WAM for selective future imagination. Bind-WAM introduces language-object binding uncertainty, selectively invoking joint future-action generation when the current observation does not support stable task-relevant action generation. An offline labeling pipeline uses complete demonstrations and action context to identify task-relevant objects and detect reference mismatch, invisibility, and identity drift. The resulting pseudo-labels supervise a lightweight router that predicts binding uncertainty from the current observation and language instruction. At inference, a shared WAM policy invokes selective future imagination according to the predicted uncertainty and its change between consecutive replanning steps. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that Bind-WAM can establish binding uncertainty as a strong, interpretable signal for selective future imagination.

</details>


### [57] [Interactive Perception of Granular Materials via Robotic Scooping and Force-Torque Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15339)
*Samuli Hynninen,Mohammad Amman,Meri Mäkelä,Anirvan Dutta,Ville Kyrki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Latent Filter++交互感知框架，从舀取过程中的力-扭矩测量直接推断颗粒材料属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒材料交互动力学复杂多变，缺乏物理属性知识时难以可靠操作，需要一种能直接从交互中学习材料属性的方法。

Method: 将仿生频率-时间滤波器组表示与因果潜在动力学模型结合，随交互观测逐步更新表示，避免手工信号预处理，学习具有物理意义的潜在表示以估计粒径和堆积密度。

Result: 在26种颗粒材料上评估，可泛化到训练集外材料，并能用少量额外交互样本实现属性映射的少样本适配；推断属性与运输中不洒出的最大加速度相关。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效从舀取力-扭矩信号推断颗粒材料属性，且推断属性对下游操作任务具有实际相关性。

Abstract: Granular materials exhibit complex and highly variable interaction dynamics, making their reliable manipulation challenging without knowledge of their physical properties. We present an interactive perception framework that infers granular material properties directly from force-torque measurements acquired during scooping. The proposed Latent Filter++ combines a bio-inspired frequency-temporal filter-bank representation with a causal latent dynamical model, allowing the representation to evolve as interaction observations become available while avoiding hand-crafted signal preprocessing. The learned latent representation captures physically meaningful variation in granular materials and supports estimation of properties including grain size and bulk density. Evaluated on 26 diverse granular materials, the approach generalizes to materials excluded from training and enables few-shot adaptation of property-specific mappings using a small number of additional interaction samples. Finally, we demonstrate the relevance of the inferred properties to downstream manipulation by examining their correlation with the maximum acceleration the materials can withstand without spilling from the spoon during transport.

</details>


### [58] [BiPOP: Partial-Order Planning for Long-Horizon Bimanual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15317)
*Kun Chu,Xufeng Zhao,Jan-Gerrit Habekost,Cornelius Weber,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiPOP结合大语言模型与时间部分有序规划，解决双臂长程操作中的时空协调瓶颈，在仿真与真实机器人上均优于现有规划方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作的核心瓶颈不仅在于单臂灵巧技能，更在于长时间跨任务中双臂在空间与时间耦合约束下的协调；现有工作偏重技能获取，忽视双臂行为本身的结构，即多数任务由可复用的单臂技能与少量同步双臂技能组成。

Method: 提出BiPOP框架：构建显式时空模型，按手臂可达性划分工作空间，刻画臂间依赖、手臂分配、同步需求及安全并行机会；由LLM将自然语言指令翻译为PDDL规范，再交由时间规划器执行约束与调度，生成符号层一致且可并行高效执行的计划。

Result: 在不同复杂度的仿真长程任务上，BiPOP在成功率、规划时间和计划效率上优于强推理模型的开环规划与ReAct式闭环规划；完整流程在真实双臂机器人NICOL上端到端运行；失败分析表明仅用LLM的规划器主要违反时空协调约束。

Conclusion: 将时空协调约束交由符号规划器处理是必要且有效的，LLM负责语义翻译、规划器负责约束满足的分工可显著提升双臂长程操作性能。

Abstract: Bimanual robotic manipulation is versatile, yet its central challenge lies not only in executing dexterous motion with each hand, but also in coordinating the two arms for long-horizon manipulation under coupled spatial and temporal constraints. Prior work has largely focused on endowing robots with human-level manipulation skills, paying less attention to the structure of bimanual behaviour itself: many practical tasks are a coordinated composition of reusable unimanual skills and a small set of synchronous bimanual skills. This makes high-level spatio-temporal coordination, rather than skill acquisition alone, a key bottleneck for efficient bimanual execution. We propose BiPOP, a framework that couples Large Language Models (LLMs) with Temporal Partial-Order Planning for long-horizon bimanual manipulation. At its core is an explicit spatio-temporal model that partitions the workspace by arm reachability and captures inter-arm dependencies, arm assignment, synchronization requirements, and opportunities for safe concurrent execution. Building on this model, an LLM translates natural-language instructions into a formal PDDL specification, while a temporal planner enforces the constraints and schedules actions, yielding plans that are logically consistent at the symbolic level and efficient through parallel execution. On simulated long-horizon tasks of varying complexity, BiPOP outperforms open-loop planning with strong reasoning models and closed-loop ReAct-style planning in success rate, planning time, and plan efficiency, and the full pipeline runs end to end on the real bimanual robot NICOL. A failure analysis further shows that LLM-only planners mainly violate spatial and temporal coordination constraints, which motivates delegating these constraints to a symbolic planner. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [59] [APG-Planner: Learning Vision-Based High-Speed Trajectory Planning via Analytic Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15296)
*Jinwoo Lee,Geunsik Bae,Changseung Kim,Jiwoo Kim,Woojae Shin,Hyondong Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于解析策略梯度（APG）的视觉高速轨迹规划框架，训练出APG-Planner，在仿真和真实飞行中性能优于基线，并扩展到地面机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无模型策略梯度方法依赖样本估计，梯度噪声大、训练效率低，难以满足视觉高速导航对平滑无碰撞轨迹的实时需求。

Method: 利用微分平坦性从规划轨迹解析重建车辆状态，无需显式低层动力学模型即可实现可微的轨迹级状态转移；通过迭代轨迹规划与可微状态转移构建可微的回合展开，从回合级损失解析计算策略梯度。

Result: 单GPU训练约30分钟后，APG-Planner在仿真中性能与现有基线相当或更优，真实飞行实验速度达11 m/s；在滑移转向地面机器人上验证了框架对四旋翼之外微分平坦机器人系统的适用性。

Conclusion: 基于解析策略梯度的可微轨迹规划框架能有效提升训练效率与策略性能，并具备跨平台推广能力。

Abstract: Vision-based high-speed navigation requires a trajectory planner that can reactively generate smooth, collision-free trajectories from onboard visual observations. Deep reinforcement learning offers a promising approach to vision-based trajectory planning, as it enables the optimization of sequential decision-making policies in complex environments. However, model-free policy gradient methods rely on sample-based policy gradient estimation, which yields noisy gradient estimates and inefficient training. To this end, we propose a framework for learning trajectory planning policies using the analytic policy gradient (APG) method and use it to train APG-Planner, a vision-based high-speed trajectory planner. The proposed framework leverages differential flatness to analytically reconstruct vehicle states from planned trajectories, thereby enabling differentiable trajectory-level state transitions without requiring an explicit low-level dynamics model. By iteratively performing trajectory planning and differentiable state transitions, we construct a differentiable episode rollout and analytically compute policy gradients from episode-level losses, improving both training efficiency and policy performance. After approximately 30 minutes of training on a single GPU, APG-Planner demonstrates comparable or superior performance to existing baseline methods in simulation and achieves speeds of up to 11\,m/s in real-world flight experiments. We further validate the proposed framework on a skid-steer ground robot, demonstrating its applicability to differentially flat robotic systems beyond quadrotors.

</details>


### [60] [Autonomous Trail Racing with a Bicycle Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15280)
*Fabian Jenelten,Deepana Ishtaweera,Pablo Garcia,Liana Margolese,Igor Bogoslavskyi,Luca Buonocore,Jan Migliazza,Aleksandrs Ecins,Annika Wollschlaeger,Dario Bellicoso,Gabriel Nelson,Abel Gawel,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过将越野小径编码为地形的语义层，实现了自主高速山地自行车导航，并在实车和仿真中验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 山地自行车越野需要多种运动技能和复杂的场景感知，将这种能力迁移到自主两轮机器人上仍是一个巨大挑战，现有控制层次结构存在局限性。

Method: 提出统一学习框架，将局部导航和运动控制作为联合优化问题，避免传统控制层次中的扭转或姿态命令接口；感知上结合冻结视觉基础模型和轻量双头解码器，预测逐像素地形语义和置信度，并与LiDAR测距融合生成30Hz的自我中心语义高程图，在NVIDIA AGX Orin上实时运行。

Result: 在UMV自行车机器人上，自主完成一公里非结构化山地自行车道全程，速度超过5 m/s，平均3.26 m/s；仿真中策略失败率比分层基线低一个数量级，且速度更快。

Conclusion: 将小径编码为地形语义层是实现自主高速越野导航的有效方法，统一框架可同时产生竞速和敏捷跑酷行为，仅通过地形语义和回合长度塑造。

Abstract: Navigating off-road trails on a mountain bike requires diverse locomotion skills, including aerial maneuvers, cornering, and slip recovery, paired with sophisticated perception for scene understanding. Translating these capabilities to autonomous, two-wheeled robots remains a formidable challenge. We show that autonomous, high-speed trail navigation can be achieved through a single idea: encoding the trail as a semantic layer of the terrain. In simulation, this layer embeds the navigation task into the training environment; during deployment, it is reconstructed onboard from camera and LiDAR data. First, we introduce a unified learning framework that treats local navigation and locomotion as a joint optimization problem, bypassing the twist or pose command interface of classical control hierarchies. With fixed reward weights, the same framework produces both trail racing and agile parkour, shaped only through terrain semantics and episode length. Second, our perception pipeline combines a frozen vision foundation model with a lightweight dual-head decoder that predicts per-pixel terrain semantics and confidence scores, which are fused with LiDAR range measurements into an egocentric semantic elevation map at 30 Hz onboard an NVIDIA AGX Orin. We validate our framework on the Ultra Mobility Vehicle (UMV), a bicycle robot that autonomously raced across all sections of a kilometer-long, unstructured mountain bike trail at speeds exceeding 5 m/s and averaging 3.26 m/s. In simulation, our policy fails up to an order of magnitude less often than hierarchical baselines while driving faster.

</details>


### [61] [DroneDiffusion: Uncertainty-Guided Diffusion Path Planner for UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15277)
*Ivan Valuev,Iana Zhura,Valerii Serpiva,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DroneDiffusion：感知感知扩散规划器，结合可靠性图条件与免训练CHOMP引导生成无人机3D轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镜头眩光、过曝和反光表面会导致感知退化，现有规划器未显式建模并规避此类失效区域。

Method: 用视觉-语言感知安全估计器生成像素对齐可靠性图，作为去噪扩散概率模型的条件（连同RGB图像、目标点、初速度）生成自我中心3D轨迹；推理时在3D ESDF上施加免训练CHOMP引导实现避障；全仿真训练，真机零样本部署。

Result: 可靠性条件将轨迹平均感知可靠性从几何基线的0.63提升至0.71；引导使学习先验碰撞率从14.7%降至7.8%。

Conclusion: 感知安全条件与几何引导互补，将传感器可靠性作为一等信号可减少进入不可靠区域，同时保持有竞争力的导航性能。

Abstract: We present DroneDiffusion, a perception-aware diffusion planner for local 3-D UAV trajectory generation that explicitly models and avoids failure zones caused by lens flare, overexposure, and reflective surfaces. A vision-language perceptual safety estimator produces a pixel-aligned reliability map that highlights regions of degraded sensing. A denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM) is conditioned on this map together with the onboard RGB image, goal point, and initial velocity to generate egocentric 3-D trajectories. To enforce obstacle avoidance at inference time without retraining, we apply training-free CHOMP guidance over a 3-D Euclidean signed distance field. The model is trained entirely in simulation and deployed zero-shot on a real drone. Reliability conditioning raises the mean perceptual reliability along generated trajectories to 0.71, compared with 0.63 for a geometric baseline, while guidance halves the collision rate of the learned prior from 14.7% to 7.8%. Perceptual safety conditioning and geometric guidance are therefore complementary: treating sensor reliability as a first-class signal reduces incursions into unreliable regions while maintaining competitive navigation performance.

</details>


### [62] [Are We Ready for Snow-tonomous Driving? Challenges Toward Robust Automated Driving in Extreme Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15217)
*Eren Erdal Aksoy,Abu Mohammed Raisuddin,Thien-Thu Ngo,Eren Aydemir,Osman Tahir Ekşi,Paula Litkey,Petri Manninen,Heikki Hyyti,Jyri Maanpää,Tarmo Kivioja,Tuomas Herranen,Väisänen Sofie,Niemi Kalle,Isabelle Morvan,Pierre Duthon,Ian Marsh,Martin Sanfridson,Henrik Toss,Thanh Bui,Yuri Poledna,Maikol Funk Drechsler,Mikko Tarkiainen,Matti Kutila,Topi Miekkala,Stefan Parzefall,Valentina Donzella,Pak Hung Chan,Sogol Kharrazi,Mario Ceccarelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于欧盟Horizon Europe项目ROADVIEW四年的大规模开发与实车演示，总结了在雪、雨、雾等恶劣天气下实现鲁棒自动驾驶所面临的十大关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年自动驾驶感知与控制研究主要集中于晴朗天气场景，雨雪雾等恶劣天气研究不足，导致下游感知与控制任务性能显著下降，成为自动驾驶的重大瓶颈。

Method: 依托欧盟资助的Horizon Europe项目ROADVIEW，在多个地点、多种车辆平台上开展为期四年的大规模开发与真实环境演示，系统梳理并归纳阻碍天气鲁棒自动驾驶发展的关键挑战。

Result: 识别出十项关键挑战，包括数据稀缺、域偏移、多模态感知局限、控制鲁棒性不足以及验证缺口等，并总结了相关经验教训。

Conclusion: 论文为可靠的雪天自动驾驶指明未来研究方向与标准化需求，强调需在数据、感知、控制与验证等方面协同推进以提升恶劣天气下的自动驾驶鲁棒性。

Abstract: Automated driving has gained remarkable momentum over the past decade, with both industry and academia investing substantial resources in developing perception and control solutions to advance vehicle autonomy. However, these efforts have largely focused on clear-weather scenarios, leaving harsh conditions, such as snow, rain, and fog, comparatively understudied. As a result, inclement weather remains one of the most significant hurdles for autonomous vehicles, often leading to substantial performance degradation in downstream perception and control tasks. In this paper, we present insights from the EU-funded Horizon Europe project ROADVIEW, which aims to develop robust, weather-aware perception and control modules for connected and automated vehicles operating under severe conditions, including snow, rain, and fog. Drawing on four years of large-scale development and real-world demonstrations across multiple vehicle platforms in different locations, we identify ten key challenges that hinder the development of weather-resilient autonomy, including data scarcity, domain shifts, limitations in multimodal perception, control robustness, and validation gaps, among others. We summarize these challenges and lessons learned toward enabling reliable snow-autonomous driving, highlighting critical directions for future research and standardization.

</details>


### [63] [ARTS: An Open-Source, Reproducible Three-Arm Platform for Autonomy Research in Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15135)
*Jiawei Ge,Chunwoo Kim,Antony Goldenberg,Douglas McDonough,Justin Liao,Bo Ning,Sylvia Ladstaetter,Katya Van Anderlecht,Hao Ding,Laura Connolly,Ethan Kilmer,Francis Fan,Shouyue Hu,Tyler Salvador,Brendan Burkhart,Pit Henrich,Richard Cha,Tito Porras,Mathias Unberath,Kevin Cleary,Axel Krieger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARTS是一个完全由可采购部件构建的开源三臂微创手术自主研究平台，成本约13.7万美元，性能可与dVRK Classic媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术自主研究平台依赖捐赠的临床硬件，而基于工业臂的开源替代方案又各自缺失手术链的某些环节，因此需要一个任何实验室都能搭建的开放平台。

Method: 采用两个UR5e臂持腕式da Vinci X/Xi器械、一个UR5臂持双FLIR相机的立体内窥镜，配备医生控制台（触觉手柄、脚踏开关、立体观看器）及独立于软件的硬件急停；ROS 2软件锁定依赖、统一脚本安装，并硬件计时记录立体图像。

Result: 各臂以控制器全速率指令（UR5e达500 Hz），双相机曝光差在12微秒内，手部运动延迟58 ms，内窥镜图像到显示器延迟68 ms；ISO 9283位姿重复性0.14-0.25 mm，与dVRK Classic相当，光学跟踪测得远心运动平均误差0.34-0.37 mm；3名专家和3名新手完成FLS钉转移任务，1名专家完成缝合。

Conclusion: ARTS是一个可复现、性能可靠的开源三臂平台，为微创手术自主研究提供了可负担的开放替代方案，硬件、固件与软件均已开源。

Abstract: Research on surgical autonomy needs open platforms that any laboratory can build, yet the most widely shared platform depends on donated clinical hardware, and open alternatives on industrial arms each lack part of the surgical chain. We present the Autonomous Robotic Translational System (ARTS), an open-source three-arm platform for autonomy research in minimally invasive surgery, built entirely from purchasable parts for about USD 137,000. Two UR5e arms hold wristed da Vinci X/Xi instruments, a UR5 arm holds a stereo endoscope with two FLIR cameras, the surgeon console provides haptic handles, footswitches and a stereo viewer, and a hardware stop independent of the software lets a supervisor freeze all three arms in place. The ROS 2 software pins every dependency, installs from the same scripts on bare metal or in containers, and records stereo images with hardware-measured timing. On the real arms, ARTS commands each arm at its controller's full rate (e.g., 500 Hz on the UR5e), the two cameras expose within 12 microseconds of each other, hand motion reaches the arm in 58 ms, and the endoscope image reaches the monitor in 68 ms. The ISO 9283 pose repeatability is 0.14-0.25 mm, comparable to a da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) Classic arm on the same protocol, and an optical tracker measured a mean remote-center-of-motion error of 0.34-0.37 mm. Three expert and three novice operators performed the Fundamentals of Laparoscopic Surgery (FLS) peg transfer through a laparoscopic trainer box, and one of the experts sutured on a suture pad. The hardware designs, firmware and software are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [64] [PolyMorphWBC: Whole-Body Control from End-Effector Trajectories for Quadrupedal Mobile Manipulation across Arm Morphologies with Extended Reach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15117)
*Heng Li,Yang Li,Yifan Duan,Ren Fang,Matao Duan,Wenhao Yu,Lu Zhang,Jianmin Ji,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PolyMorphWBC是一种分层控制器，仅以末端执行器轨迹为任务指令，即可协调四足移动机械臂的臂-基座运动，支持四种6自由度臂形态及超出固定基座可达范围的延伸轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足移动机械臂需协调臂与基座以扩展操作空间，但短时域末端轨迹执行困难：控制器须根据所装臂的形态决定基座何时、如何移动。

Method: 提出PolyMorphWBC分层控制器：轨迹条件协调器基于预览的末端目标决定基座运动；臂特定逆运动学跟踪当前目标；冻结的运动后端执行运动；通过URDF导出的形态条件与在线残余力反馈实现跨臂复用；在程序生成的臂与结构化轨迹上训练。

Result: 在IsaacLab的未见轨迹与臂形态上完成率93.95%，位置误差2.30cm、姿态误差4.81°；在MuJoCo对比UMI-on-Legs，四条轨迹上末端位置误差降低69–72%、姿态误差降低91–97%；在Go2–ARX5实物系统上，使用pi_0生成的末端轨迹实现语言条件延伸抓放，无需VLA生成基座指令。

Conclusion: PolyMorphWBC能以末端轨迹作为唯一任务级指令，跨多种臂形态生成协调的臂-基座运动，并支持超出固定基座可达范围的延伸操作，在仿真与实物上验证了有效性与跨臂复用能力。

Abstract: Quadrupedal mobile manipulators can extend their manipulation workspace by coordinating the mounted arm with the mobile base. At the task level, short-horizon end-effector (EE) trajectories provide an interface for specifying the desired manipulation motion without prescribing how it should be realized by the arm and base. However, executing such trajectories remains challenging because the controller must determine when and how the base should move, and this decision depends on the mounted-arm morphology. We therefore present PolyMorphWBC, a hierarchical controller that takes an EE trajectory as the sole task-level motion command and generates coordinated arm--base motions across four families of 6-DoF mounted arms, with extended-reach trajectories beyond the initial fixed-base reach. Within PolyMorphWBC, a trajectory-conditioned coordinator determines the base motion from previewed EE targets, while arm-specific Inverse Kinematics (IK) tracks the current target, and a frozen locomotion backend executes the resulting motion. Cross-arm reuse is enabled by a Unified Robot Description Format (URDF)-derived morphology conditioning, together with online residual-wrench feedback. We train PolyMorphWBC on procedurally generated arms and structured trajectories within and beyond fixed-base reach. In IsaacLab, it achieves 93.95% completion with 2.30cm position error and 4.81° orientation error on held-out trajectories and arm morphologies. In MuJoCo evaluation against UMI-on-Legs, PolyMorphWBC reduces EE position error by 69--72% and orientation error by 91--97% across four within- and extended-reach trajectories. On a physical Go2--ARX5 system, we demonstrate language-conditioned extended-reach pick-and-place using EE trajectories from pi_0 without vision-language-action (VLA)-generated base commands.

</details>


### [65] [Elastra: A Simulation Framework for Elastic Bodies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15112)
*Youran Sun,Chugang Yi,Xingyu Ren,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Elastra仿真框架，通过垂直运动单元与弹性和阻尼建模弹性表面变形与回弹，验证后用于训练人形机器人在床垫和蹦床上站立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人与床垫、蹦床等弹性表面交互时，载荷变化导致表面变形和回弹，进而改变后续接触位置与力，现有仿真难以准确建模此类交互以支持控制策略训练与评估。

Method: Elastra将表面表示为仅作垂直运动的单元，用弹性连接和阻尼模拟变形与恢复；接触力驱动表面运动，更新后的表面几何用于计算后续接触力；与独立实现及真实床垫载荷曲线对比验证；以HoST策略和ProtoMotions运动跟踪器为原始控制器，通过强化学习训练残差策略修正关节目标，用于Unitree G1人形机器人站立任务。

Result: 仿真经独立实现和真实床垫载荷曲线系统性验证；在床垫上HoST成功率由44.1%提升至72.5%，蹦床上由55%提升至89%；ProtoMotions在训练未遇到的初始姿态下成功率由19.0%提升至45.0%。

Conclusion: Elastra能有效建模机器人与弹性表面的动态交互，所训练的残差策略显著提升人形机器人在床垫和蹦床上站立的成功率与泛化能力。

Abstract: When robots interact with elastic surfaces such as mattresses and trampolines, changes in loading cause the surfaces to deform and rebound, altering subsequent contact locations and forces. We present Elastra, a simulation framework for modeling this interaction and supporting the training and evaluation of robot control policies. Elastra represents each surface using elements that move only vertically, with elastic connections and damping to model deformation and recovery. Contact forces drive surface motion, and the updated surface geometry is used to compute subsequent contact forces. We systematically validate our simulations against an independent implementation and a real mattress load curve. We further use Elastra to train the Unitree G1 humanoid to stand up on mattresses and trampolines. Using the pretrained HoST policy and ProtoMotions motion tracker as original controllers, we train residual policies through reinforcement learning to add corrections to their joint targets. Adding these residual policies increases the success rate with HoST from 44.1\% to 72.5\%, and from 55\% to 89\% on the trampoline. With ProtoMotions, the success rate increases from 19.0\% to 45.0\% for initial poses not encountered during training. The code and video are available on this https URL .

</details>


### [66] [Decide Before You Move: Diffusion-Generated Search Reference for Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation in Semi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15093)
*Yuxuan Feng,Fujing Xie,Sören Schwertfeger,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PreNav 是一个面向半静态室内环境的开放词汇目标导航框架，它仅在运动前用 LLM 和扩散模型生成一次全局搜索参考，再在检测到目标后在线接近，从而避免逐步调用视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于先验地图的方法在物体移动后失效，而在线方法每几步就需昂贵地调用视觉语言模型；作者假设墙壁和大件家具不动，可利用目标与家具的共现先验来决定搜索位置。

Method: PreNav 先用 LLM 将目标与家具的共现关系转化为空间先验，再以条件扩散模型结合持久结构生成带带状区域的有序中心线；机器人沿该参考搜索，检测到目标后在线接近。

Result: 在 Gibson ObjectNav 基准上，PreNav 在相同结构先验下优于强在线基线；生成器仅在房间级 ScanNet++ 场景上训练，未微调即用于楼层级 Gibson。真实环境物体移位和开放词汇查询实验进一步验证了方法。

Conclusion: PreNav 通过一次性生成全局搜索参考，有效应对半静态环境中的物体移动和开放词汇查询，无需逐步调用视觉语言模型即可实现高效目标导航。

Abstract: Open-world navigation requires a robot to locate a user-specified target in an indoor scene under open-vocabulary queries. Realistic indoor environments are semi-static: walls and furniture largely persist while smaller objects move, relocate, or vanish between visits. Prior-map systems answer queries from object poses recorded at reconstruction time, which expire as soon as objects move, whereas online methods re-derive where to look from current observations every few steps, often through expensive calls to a vision--language model. Assuming that walls and large furniture do not move, we keep their map to plan paths, but use a target--furniture co-occurrence prior to decide where to search. We present PreNav, an ObjectNav framework on semi-static environments. Rather than invoking a vision-language model at each step, PreNav generates a single global search reference before the robot moves: an LLM converts target--furniture co-occurrence into a spatial prior, a conditional diffusion model turns that prior and the persistent structure into an ordered centerline with a band around it, and the robot approaches the target once it is detected online. Experiments on a Gibson ObjectNav benchmark show that PreNav outperforms a strong online baseline under matched structural priors, using a generator trained only on room-level ScanNet++ scenes and applied to floor-level Gibson without fine-tuning. Real-world experiments under object relocation and open-vocabulary queries further validate the approach.

</details>


### [67] [CrowdMAPS: Crowdsourcing Multiple Actions per State for Real-World Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15078)
*Yilong Song,Harshvardhan Chandirasekar,Karthik Desingh,Chen Zhu-Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CrowdMAPS通过网页众包为真实机器人数据集增加每个状态的多个有效动作（MAPS），在五个真实操作任务中将每个状态的动作数从1增加到10，使关键姿态策略和轨迹策略的平均成功率分别提高35.6和33.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人模仿学习受限于物理演示采集成本高，且每个访问状态通常只有一个动作标签，数据监督稀疏。

Method: 提出基于网页的3D众包界面，在标准数据采集后或期间将访问状态分发给远程非专家标注者，由其指定替代有效动作，无需物理机器人硬件，从而沿“宽度”维度扩展数据集。

Result: 在五个真实操作任务中，MAPS宽度从1增至10，关键姿态策略平均成功率提升35.6个百分点，轨迹策略提升33.0个百分点，样本效率提高。

Conclusion: 软件介导的人工标注可增强物理机器人数据以提供更密集监督，CrowdMAPS可作为真实机器人数据采集的可扩展数字层。

Abstract: Real-world robot imitation learning is often limited by the cost of collecting physical demonstrations, which typically yield only one action label per visited state. We present CrowdMAPS, a web-based crowdsourcing paradigm for augmenting real-robot datasets with Multiple Actions per State (MAPS). After or during standard robot data collection, CrowdMAPS distributes visited robot states to remote annotators through a web-based 3D interface, where they specify alternative valid actions without requiring access to physical robot hardware. This expands robot datasets along a complementary "width" dimension--by increasing the number of actions per visited state--rather than only by collecting more trajectories. We evaluate CrowdMAPS using keyframe-centric goal-pose annotations supplied by non-expert annotators and train imitation policies on the resulting MAPS data. Across five real-world manipulation tasks, increasing MAPS width from one to ten actions per state improves sample efficiency and increases average success rates by 35.6 percentage points for keypose policies and 33.0 percentage points for trajectory policies. These results show that software-mediated human annotations can augment physical robot data to afford denser supervision, establishing CrowdMAPS as a scalable digital layer for real-robot data collection.

</details>


### [68] [MiniPose: Miniature-Based Real Data Collection for 6D Pose Estimation of Large Industrial Pipe Segments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15066)
*Hyunjun Kim,Youngjin Seo,Sungwon Seo,Jiyou Shin,Kunwoo Kim,Chanyong Park,Hyungpil Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiniPose提出一种基于缩微模型的真实RGB-D数据采集流程，通过逆缩放变换将缩微深度图映射为全尺寸物体的几何一致表示，以少量缩微真实数据与合成数据混合训练，缓解大型工业物体6-DOF位姿估计中的数据采集负担和仿真到现实差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型重型工业物体（如船厂大型管道系统、石化装置结构件）的6-DOF位姿估计研究不足；反复移动大型物体以采集姿态多样的真实训练数据在物理和空间上代价高昂，而合成数据虽可扩展却难以忠实复现真实RGB-D观测，尤其是真实深度传感器的结构化测量误差。

Method: 制作缩微复刻件，用真实RGB-D相机采集多姿态RGB-D数据；RGB图像直接使用，深度图通过简单逆缩放变换转换为全尺寸物体的几何一致表示；以PVN3D和RDPN6D两种不同形式的RGB-D位姿估计器进行验证，将每类管道最多1000张缩微图像（不足训练数据10%）与10000张合成图像混合训练，并与纯合成训练及全尺寸真实数据训练进行比较。

Result: 对两种估计器，混合缩微数据后各管道类型的平均ADD-S AUC均优于纯合成训练；与全尺寸真实数据训练的比较表明缩微数据可作为难以进行全尺寸采集场景下的替代方案。

Conclusion: MiniPose通过缩微实物采集真实外观与传感器测量特性，能以少量数据有效缩小仿真到现实差距，并在全尺寸采集困难时作为代理数据，降低大型工业物体位姿估计的数据采集负担。

Abstract: 6-DOF pose estimation of large and heavy industrial objects, such as large piping systems in shipyards and structural components in petrochemical plants, remains relatively underexplored in robotic perception. Repeatedly repositioning large objects is physically and spatially demanding, making it difficult to acquire real training data with sufficient pose diversity. Synthetic data offer a scalable alternative, but faithfully reproducing real RGB-D observations, particularly the structured measurement errors of real depth sensors, remains difficult. We propose MiniPose, a practical pipeline for miniature-based real RGB-D data collection that addresses both the data-acquisition burden and the sim-to-real gap. We fabricate a scaled-down replica and capture RGB-D data of it in diverse poses using a real RGB-D camera. RGB images are used directly, while depth maps are converted to a geometrically consistent representation of the full-scale object via a simple inverse scaling transformation. This allows training to incorporate real appearance and sensor measurement characteristics that are difficult to reproduce using synthetic data alone. To examine whether the benefit depends on a particular network, we evaluate two RGB-D pose estimators with different formulations, PVN3D and RDPN6D. Mixing at most 1,000 miniature images per pipe type, less than 10\% of the training data, with 10,000 synthetic images improves the mean ADD-S AUC across pipe types over synthetic-only training for both estimators. A comparison with training on full-scale real data further suggests that miniature data can serve as a proxy where full-scale collection is difficult, reducing the data-collection burden for large industrial objects.

</details>


### [69] [Does Your World Model Know Actions Have Consequences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15048)
*Ayush Gaggar,Jared Berry,Todd D. Murphey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出具身世界模型（WMs）现有评价指标只关注输出质量（如重建清晰度或任务成功率），无法评估模型对反事实动作后果的建模保真度；提出通过敏感性分析检查模型对动作扰动的强制响应，并强调硬件部署需要新的评估框架来权衡状态误差与敏感性预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WMs评价指标聚焦重建清晰度和任务成功率，不能评估模型预测反事实动作后果的保真度，而这对硬件部署至关重要。

Method: 采用敏感性分析，通过检查WMs对动作扰动的强制响应来审计其预测反事实动作后果的能力，并提出需要新的评估框架。

Result: 现有指标无法衡量反事实动作后果的建模保真度；敏感性分析可作为审计手段；硬件部署需权衡状态误差与敏感性预测准确性。

Conclusion: 需要建立新的评估框架，以分析状态误差与准确敏感性预测之间的权衡，从而评估WMs对反事实动作后果的建模保真度。

Abstract: Embodied world models (WMs) are implicit learning systems that predict the next future latent state directly from observations and actions. Unique from other end-to-end learning systems, the dynamics predictor of WMs can simulate the environment's response to alternative actions without physical deployment nor pre-specifying states of the dynamical system. Current metrics focus on the quality of model outputs, e.g., clarity of reconstructions or task success, but fail to assess the fidelity of modeling consequences of counterfactual actions. Inspecting the forced response to action perturbations through sensitivity analysis is one way to audit how well WMs can predict the consequences of counterfactual actions. Deploying WMs on hardware will require new evaluation frameworks to analyze the tradeoffs between state error and accurate sensitivity prediction.

</details>


### [70] [Genie Sim Benchmark: Real-to-Sim Fidelity and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15020)
*Di Yang,Da Huang,Yang Lv,Fan Yang,Yi Lu,Zheyuan Xing,Linting Guo,Jiaen Shen,Wenjun Sun,Maoqing Yao,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文评估Genie Sim Benchmark，通过22个机器人-任务对和系统辨识研究，表明任务特定模拟评估能同时反映任务结果与物理响应一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要验证仿真基准能否在任务层面和物理响应层面可信地替代真实机器人评估。

Method: 采用配对真实-仿真任务（22个机器人-任务对，16个任务名，微调pi0.5策略），结合G2 crsB长时闭环位置与关节力矩一致性实验、五个物体案例的接触转移研究，以及独立遥操作轨迹（32.6503秒）下的运动控制器指令整形与位置伺服评估；并用原生PhysX适配复现三个交互任务。

Result: 任务得分相关系数0.9686，MAE 0.0405，RMSE 0.0520，仿真-真实差异-0.110至+0.080；每关节位置RMSE 0.00614-0.03522 rad，电流导出力矩RMSE 0.73545-4.00099 N·m；PhysX适配以案例相关增益复现三个交互任务。

Conclusion: 联合评估任务结果与物理响应一致性可支持任务特定的仿真评估，Genie Sim Benchmark具备作为真实机器人评估补充的潜力。

Abstract: We evaluate Genie Sim Benchmark through paired real-simulation tasks and complementary system-identification studies. Across 22 robot-task pairs spanning 16 task names, task-specific pi0.5 policies fine-tuned on real-robot data achieve a descriptive Score correlation of 0.9686, MAE 0.0405, RMSE 0.0520, with simulation-real differences from -0.110 to +0.080. G2 crsB experiments assess long-horizon closed-loop position and joint-side torque agreement, while five representative object cases examine identification and cross-engine contact transfer. A separate 32.6503 s teleoperation trajectory, excluded from mechanical identification and servo-gain search, evaluates external position targets through simulated motion-controller command shaping and a calibrated position servo. Across both arms, per-joint position RMSE spans 0.00614-0.03522 rad and current-derived torque RMSE spans 0.73545-4.00099 N m. Native PhysX adaptations recover three recorded interaction tasks, with case-dependent alignment gains. These results support task-specific simulation evaluation through joint assessment of task outcomes and physical-response consistency.

</details>


### [71] [Fast Inference-Time Policy Refinement via Action-Affine World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15011)
*Run Wang,Yilin Mo,Liang Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动作仿射世界模型A²WM，离线学习策略对动作扰动的局部线性响应，在线用凸二次规划快速修正模仿策略的执行误差，成功率高且开销仅3.8毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿策略能完成复杂操作，但小执行误差会累积导致失败；现有修正方法需额外在线策略学习或昂贵的在线规划。

Method: 利用预训练策略作为标称行为，离线从动作扰动的策略 rollout 中学习局部动作敏感性，构建动作仿射世界模型：给定交互历史和策略提议，直接预测整个规划时域的任务相关几何为标称轨迹加对修正的线性响应；结合二次任务误差惩罚与线性修正约束，转化为凸二次规划（QP），在线仅需一次模型评估和一次QP求解，策略与模型权重均冻结。

Result: 在四个仿真操作任务中，该框架在评估基线中取得最高成功率，每控制步平均修正开销仅3.8毫秒（不含基策略采样）。

Conclusion: 将策略修正聚焦于学习局部动作响应并以凸QP求解，可高效、低开销地提升模仿策略的鲁棒性与成功率。

Abstract: Imitation policies can perform complex manipulation, yet small execution errors can accumulate and cause failure. Refinement can correct these errors, but existing approaches often require additional on-policy learning or costly online planning. Our key insight is that a pretrained policy already supplies a useful nominal behavior: refinement can focus on predicting how small action corrections change its outcome. We therefore learn this local response in a form that is cheap to optimize. Our \textit{action-affine world model} (A\textsuperscript{2}WM) learns local action sensitivities offline from action-perturbed policy rollouts. Given interaction history and a policy proposal, it predicts task-relevant geometry as a nominal trajectory plus a linear response to corrections. It predicts the full planning horizon directly, avoiding the propagation of intermediate errors through recursive model evaluations. Quadratic penalties on predicted task errors and linear correction constraints then yield a cheap convex quadratic program (QP). Online refinement requires one model evaluation and one QP solve, while both policy and model weights remain frozen. Across four simulated manipulation tasks, our framework achieves the highest success rate among evaluated baselines, adding only 3.8 ms of mean refinement overhead per control step, excluding base-policy sampling.

</details>


### [72] [ILA: A Transferable Interface-Level Adapter for Compliant Humanoid Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14985)
*ZhuoChang Cai,Zihao Yan,Jinxuan Zhu,Yanjie Li,Hao Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ILA是一种接口级适配器，无需专用交互传感器即可实现人形机器人的柔顺运动跟踪，可即插即用地增强预训练跟踪器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接跟踪参考运动在受限环境或人机物理交互中可能不安全，因此需要柔顺运动跟踪，但现有方法依赖专用传感器或需重新训练策略。

Method: 提出ILA适配器，从机器人观测历史中调整运动参考以预测物理交互引起的参考修正，完全在仿真中训练，可作为接口级模块直接接入不同预训练运动跟踪器而无需重训练。

Result: 在三个运动跟踪器上评估，引导-目标误差相比原始策略降低63.6%–75.4%；匹配仿真和硬件实验表明其提升对人类引导的响应性同时保持标称跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明柔顺引导可作为可复用、无传感器的扩展模块用于预训练人形运动跟踪器。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking is fundamental to reproducing human motions on humanoid robots and serves as an important tool for teleoperation and motion data collection. However, directly tracking reference motions can be unsafe in constrained environments or during physical human--robot interaction, motivating the need for compliant motion tracking. We present ILA, an Interface-Level Adapter for compliant humanoid motion tracking without dedicated interaction sensors. ILA adapts motion references from the robot's observation history, allowing the robot to respond to physical guidance while preserving the capabilities of a pretrained motion tracker. As an interface-level module, ILA can be directly plugged into different pretrained motion trackers without retraining either the adapter or the tracking policies. ILA is trained entirely in simulation to predict reference corrections induced by physical interaction. We evaluate ILA with three humanoid motion trackers and achieve a 63.6--75.4% reduction in guidance-target error compared with their original policies. Matched simulation studies and hardware experiments further show that ILA improves responsiveness to human guidance while preserving nominal motion-tracking performance. These results demonstrate compliant guidance as a reusable, sensor-free extension to pretrained humanoid motion trackers.

</details>


### [73] [From a Single RGB Image to Robot Execution: Metric Scene Alignment and Contact-Guided Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14981)
*Zhenxuan Ding,Yu Huang,Xinmiao Wang,Chenghao Sun,Ziyang Dong,Wenhao Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一流程从单张RGB图像重建场景并生成人形机器人交互动作参考，通过重定向前后两阶段几何一致性细化，在Unitree G1上实现89%仿真和70%实物成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人形交互动作生成扩展到新环境受限于需预建3D场景资产；从单张RGB图像重建场景可突破此限制，但动作生成与迁移过程中的几何一致性难以保证，且人机形态差异会导致支撑误差。

Method: 构建统一流程：从单张RGB图像重建场景几何，基于重建场景生成人体交互动作，再重定向到人形机器人；在形态迁移前后加入两个互补细化阶段——重定向前用关节度量交互细化（JMIR）在保持交互结构的同时对齐人体动作与配准度量场景，重定向后用接触引导残差重定向（CGRR）修正机器人特定支撑误差。

Result: 相比GRAIL，细化时间从480秒降至87秒，加速5.5倍；完整流程在仿真中成功率达89%，在Unitree G1上50次实物试验中达70%。

Conclusion: 该方法能从单张RGB图像生成与重建场景一致、可供多个预训练人形跟踪器直接执行而无需场景特定策略微调的人形动作参考，验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Extending humanoid interaction generation to new environments remains limited by the need for prebuilt 3D scene assets. Reconstructing scenes directly from single RGB images offers a way to overcome this limitation, but introduces a key challenge in maintaining geometric consistency throughout motion generation and transfer. Visually plausible human motions may be misaligned with the reconstructed scene, while retargeting can introduce additional support errors due to differences between human and robot embodiments. We present a unified pipeline that transforms a single RGB image into a humanoid motion reference. It reconstructs scene geometry from the input image, generates a human interaction conditioned on the reconstructed scene, and retargets the motion to a humanoid robot. To maintain geometric consistency throughout this process, we incorporate two complementary refinement stages before and after embodiment transfer. Before retargeting, Joint Metric Interaction Refinement (JMIR) refines human motion alignment with the registered metric scene while preserving the generated interaction structure. Following retargeting, Contact Guided Residual Retargeting (CGRR) corrects robot specific support errors to restore the intended support relationships. Together, these stages produce motion references that are consistent with the reconstructed scene and can be followed by multiple pretrained humanoid trackers without scene specific policy fine tuning. Compared with GRAIL, our method reduces refinement time from 480 s to 87 s, yielding a speedup of 5.5 times. The complete pipeline achieves a success rate of 89% in simulation and 70% across 50 physical trials on the Unitree G1, demonstrating its effectiveness in generating executable humanoid motion references from single RGB images.

</details>


### [74] [Scaling VLA Serving Under Robotic Execution Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14969)
*Yue Zhang,Zicong Ye,Lufang Chen,Dance Yang,Mingyang Li,Zhenwei Duan,Michael Zhao,Ziye Deng,Starrick Liu,Ruizhi Chengze,YongHua Chen,SongAo Han,Yu Sun,Ran Yu,Xiaofan Li,Ruyi Gan,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并部署了XInfer——面向大规模机器人集群的VLA模型推理服务系统，通过联合协调异构VLA计算与机器人执行的动态响应需求，在保障时敏反馈的前提下提升GPU效率，使有效机器人容量达到现有最优系统的1.26–3.75倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中已有数百台由VLA模型驱动的机器人在真实场景运行，大规模部署迫切需要高效的集群推理服务，以降低推理基础设施成本。

Method: XInfer联合协调异构VLA计算与机器人执行不断变化的响应需求：利用计算并行性并管理干扰，在保护时敏机器人反馈的同时提升GPU利用率；基于生产负载在三种VLA模型上进行评估。

Result: 在物理执行约束下，XInfer的有效机器人容量（ERC）达到现有最优系统的1.26–3.75倍，实现已公开。

Conclusion: XInfer作为生产部署的VLA服务系统，能够显著提升大规模机器人部署的推理吞吐与GPU效率，同时保障实时控制需求。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose robotic manipulation. In our production setting, hundreds of VLA-powered robots are already operating in real-world environments, creating an immediate need for efficient fleet serving. We present XInfer, a production-deployed VLA serving system designed to reduce the inference infrastructure cost of large-scale robotic deployments. XInfer jointly coordinates heterogeneous VLA computation with the evolving response requirements of robotic execution. By exploiting computational parallelism while managing interference, XInfer improves GPU efficiency while protecting time-critical robotic feedback. We evaluate XInfer across three VLA models using production-derived workloads, achieving 1.26-3.75 times the effective robot capacity (ERC) of state-of-the-art systems under physical-execution constraints. Our implementation is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [75] [Adapting Monocular Depth Estimation in Novel Scenes through Disparity Refinement and Robot Ego-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14958)
*Dimitrios Chatziparaschis,Georgios Georgakis,Adnan I. Ansar,Shehryar Khattak,Konstantinos Karydis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EM-ViT，一种在线自适应的单目深度估计模型，利用机器人运行中采集的稀疏LiDAR点和自运动信息，将基础模型的相对深度输入快速校准为度量深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目深度估计基础模型多在海量数据上离线训练，采用仿射不变深度表示，在新型非结构化场景中泛化能力不明确；同时LiDAR和双目相机等传感器成本高，需要更经济且能在线适应的感知方案。

Method: 受双目相机参数化启发，将深度预测分解为密集视差细化（恢复场景相对结构）和有效基线标量预测（确定缺失的度量尺度）；在线学习过程中利用稀疏LiDAR点和可选的机器人自运动信息快速训练轻量级Vision Transformer，并引入内部梯度匹配损失以实时保持输入视差的细粒度语义结构。

Result: 在KITTI等公开序列上验证，该在线学习流程相比现有单目深度估计基础模型能更快适应新场景，且精度更高。

Conclusion: EM-ViT能够在线将基础模型先验的相对深度自适应为度量深度，以低成本方式提升机器人在未知环境中的稠密远程深度感知能力。
