Abstract: The geometric understanding of a scene is a crucial component for safe and efficient robotic operations in novel and unstructured environments. The recent emergence of foundation models for Monocular Depth Estimation (MDE) has provided an opportunity to enrich robotic perception capabilities with dense long-range depth predictions, replacing expensive onboard hardware (e.g., LiDARs and stereo cameras). However, these offline models are primarily trained on affine-invariant depth representations, and their generalization to novel scenes remains unclear. In this work we present EM-ViT, a monocular depth estimation model that learns to adapt online relative depth inputs from a foundation model prior to the metric scale with observations collected during robotic operations. Motivated by the stereo camera parameterization, our method decomposes depth prediction via dense disparity refinement to recover the scene's relative structure and an effective baseline scalar prediction to determine the missing metric scale. The learning process leverages sparse LiDAR points and, optionally, the robot's Ego-Motion (EM) to rapidly train a lightweight Vision Transformer (ViT), while an internal gradient matching loss preserves the fine-grained semantic structure of the input disparity in real-time. We demonstrate the efficacy of our method on publicly available sequences, such as KITTI, and show that our online learning pipeline can adapt to novel scenes faster and with higher accuracy than existing MDE foundation models. The code and supplementary materials will be publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [76] [The Posterior-Prior Epistemic Gap: When World Model Imagination Fails to Carry Task-Relevant Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14955)
*Jaerock Kwon,Elahe Delavari,Donghyun Kim,Haewoon Nam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在CARLA驾驶模拟器中对循环状态空间世界模型与主动推理规划的单种子案例研究，发现后验-先验表征差距：想象推演状态逐渐丢失障碍物信息，且规划器集成认知项无法察觉该损失；任务条件加权有效自由能可使同一世界模型实现车道保持与避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的规划器默认转移模型能忠实传播任务相关信号，但这一性质很少被直接测量，需要检验想象推演是否真的保留任务相关信息。

Method: 在CARLA模拟器中对带主动推理规划的循环状态空间模型世界模型进行单种子案例研究，用同一监督障碍探测探针分别作用于后验（观测滤波）状态与想象先验状态，比较AUC与logit分离度以定位信息瓶颈；同时评估集成分歧作为障碍分类器的AUC，并引入任务条件化的有效自由能分量加权。

Result: 障碍探针在后验状态上AUC为0.85，但在想象先验状态上随步数单调衰减（15步内0.85→0.71，logit分离度下降57%）；无特权运行时坐标时避障成功率降至0%；集成分歧作为障碍分类器AUC仅0.52（随机水平）。任务条件化有效自由能加权实现车道保持（路线完成率81.2±1.8%）与避障（28/28，使用特权坐标），运行时权重调制支持避障后回中。

Conclusion: 同一探针下的衰减将信息瓶颈归因于转移模型而非表征，构成规划时未被标记的想象失败（未知的未知）；任务条件化有效自由能加权可在同一世界模型上改善车道保持与避障表现。

Abstract: Model-based planners that roll out imagined futures assume the transition model faithfully propagates task-relevant signals---a property rarely measured directly. In a single-seed case study of a Recurrent State Space Model world model with active-inference planning in the CARLA driving simulator, we identify a \textbf{posterior-prior gap}---posterior (observation-filtered) states retain obstacle information that imagined rollout states progressively lose: a supervised obstacle probe achieves AUC 0.85 on posterior states, but the same probe applied to imagined prior states shows monotonic decay (AUC 0.85$\rightarrow$0.71 over 15 steps; logit separation drops 57\%). Because the probe is identical, the decay isolates the transition model---not the representation---as the information bottleneck. Obstacle avoidance drops to 0\% without privileged runtime coordinates, and the planner's own ensemble-based epistemic term does not flag the loss---ensemble disagreement yields AUC 0.52 as an obstacle classifier (chance level), making the failure an unflagged imagination failure---an unknown unknown at planning time. As a secondary contribution, we show that \textbf{task-conditioned weighting of Effective Free Energy components} over the same world model yields lane keeping ($81.2 \pm 1.8$\% route completion) and obstacle avoidance (28/28 avoided, \emph{using privileged coordinates}) in the CARLA simulator, with runtime weight modulation enabling post-evasion centering.

</details>


### [77] [DIVAL: Diffeomorphic Vector-Field Alignment for Nonholonomic Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14918)
*Yike Qiao,Xiaodong He,An Zhuo,Zhiyong Sun,Weimin Bao,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DIVAL方法，通过微分同胚变形重塑名义向量场使其与机器人初始朝向一致，从而消除非完整机器人初始朝向不匹配导致的碰撞和控制不连续问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于向量场的非完整机器人运动规划分两步：先设计名义向量场，再控制机器人跟随。但机器人无法瞬时对齐向量场方向，初始朝向与场方向存在不匹配，可能导致碰撞。

Method: 对工作空间施加局部微分同胚变形以重塑名义向量场，使重塑后的向量场在初始位置与机器人朝向同向，并在变形区域外与名义向量场一致；由此消除初始朝向不匹配，使机器人全程保持朝向对齐。

Result: 数值仿真中，与原始向量场跟踪基线相比，该方法在所有试验中实现无碰撞收敛并避免控制不连续；地面车辆硬件实验在不同目标和初始条件下、存在真实扰动时也实现无碰撞收敛。

Conclusion: DIVAL通过消除初始朝向不匹配，使非完整机器人保持与重塑向量场的朝向对齐，进而保留名义向量场的避障、目标收敛和平滑控制等性质，仿真与硬件实验均验证其有效性。

Abstract: Conventional vector-field-based motion planners for nonholonomic robots consist of two steps: first designing a nominal vector field and then controlling the robot to follow it to approach the target. However, a nonholonomic robot cannot follow the vector field instantaneously and may collide with obstacles, due to the inevitable mismatch between the robot's initial heading and the vector field's direction. This paper proposes a diffeomorphic vector-field alignment (DIVAL) method, which aligns the vector field's direction with the robot's initial heading so the robot can follow the vector field from the start. Specifically, the nominal vector field is locally reshaped by applying a diffeomorphic deformation to the workspace, such that the reshaped vector field has the same direction as the robot's heading at the initial position and coincides with the nominal vector field outside the deformed region. Since DIVAL eliminates the initial heading mismatch, the nonholonomic robot can maintain heading alignment with the reshaped vector field throughout the motion. This heading alignment further ensures that the robot preserves the nominal vector field's properties, including obstacle avoidance, convergence to the target, and smooth control signals. Numerical simulations provide vector-field-tracking baselines where the nominal vector fields are reshaped with DIVAL. Compared to the original baselines, the proposed method achieves collision-free convergence in all trials and avoids control discontinuities. Hardware experiments on a ground vehicle further demonstrate collision-free convergence across different targets and initial conditions under real-world disturbances.

</details>


### [78] [Can I join you? Extended Social Force Based Model with Predictive Control and Barrier Functions for Humanoid Group Repositioning Amidst Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14853)
*Chuxuan Zhang,Niloofar Jahanshahi,Yasaman Etesam,Mo Chen,Angelica Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合社会力模型、模型预测控制与控制屏障函数的框架，提升人形机器人群体在动态干扰下的社会适宜重定位安全性与平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会力方法在复杂环境中障碍处理有限且安全性不足，而人形机器人群体的社会适宜重定位行为研究较少。

Method: 以模型预测控制引导传统社会力模型进行前瞻规划，并用控制屏障函数约束以保证机器人群体重定位的安全性。

Result: 仿真消融实验与在线用户研究表明，该方法在复杂环境中产生更平滑、更安全的重定位行为，同时保持社会适宜性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效应对群体形成扰动，实现安全且社会化的群体重定位行为。

Abstract: As social and service robots increasingly share interaction spaces in public, they may form groups while coordinating tasks or interacting with humans. Initial group formation alone is insufficient in dynamic settings, because it can be disrupted when a person or another agent joins, leaves, or passes through the group. Although group dynamics and repositioning have been widely studied in swarm robotics and virtual-agent settings, little attention has been given to socially appropriate repositioning behaviors in groups of humanoid robots. Existing social-force-based approaches provide a useful foundation for group behavior, but they have limited obstacle handling, and can produce unsafe behavior as environmental complexity increases. We propose a novel framework that modulates traditional Social Force, guided by Model Predictive Control (MPC) to plan ahead, bounded by Control Barrier Functions (CBF) to increase safety for robot groups repositioning behaviors. This approach aims to produce safer and socially aware group repositioning behaviors under various group formation disturbance. We evaluate the method in simulation through an ablation study and an online user study. The results show that our proposed method can produce smoother and safer repositioning behaviors in complex environments while preserving social appropriateness.

</details>


### [79] [Learning Low-Latency Action Verification for Frozen Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14834)
*Wen-Han Hsieh,Ken Jiankun Zheng,Chia-Chien Chen,David M. Chan,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HDH 是一个轻量级框架，利用冻结 VLA 模型自身部署的后见知识训练紧凑动作验证器，在推理时从候选动作中选择得分最高者执行，从而提升机器人操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在通用机器人操作中表现良好，但采样得到的动作序列可能无效；而用计算昂贵的验证器评估备选动作会在每次策略查询时增加延迟。

Method: HDH 分三阶段：首先部署策略并收集其自主 rollout；然后训练一个紧凑的动作验证器，为每个动作块预测任务成功分数；最后冻结的 VLA 生成候选动作，验证器选择得分最高的候选执行。

Result: 在四个 LIBERO-10 任务上平均成功率达 82.3%；在三个真实世界操作任务上，相比基线策略平均成功率提升 22.6%。

Conclusion: 利用冻结 VLA 自身部署的后见知识训练轻量验证器，可在不增加显著推理开销的情况下有效筛选动作候选，显著提升 VLA 策略的操作成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have made significant progress in general-purpose robotic manipulation. However, sampled action sequences can be ineffective, while evaluating alternatives with computationally expensive verifiers adds latency at each policy query. In this work, we propose HDH, a lightweight framework that uses hindsight from a frozen VLA's own deployments to improve action verification. The framework has three stages. First, we deploy the policy and collect its autonomous rollouts. We then train a compact action verifier to assign each action chunk a predicted task-success score. Finally, the frozen VLA generates action candidates, and the verifier selects the candidate with the highest score for execution. Across four LIBERO-10 tasks, HDH achieves an average success rate of 82.3\% and on three real-world manipulation tasks, HDH improves average success over the baseline policy by 22.6\%.

</details>


### [80] [Open-Source Transtibial Prosthetic Bypass Preserving Joint Alignment: Gait Learning and Kinematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14762)
*Apolline de Vaulchier,Pablo Nocito,Yann Corbel,Nathanaël Jarrassé*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种开源的经胫骨假肢旁路装置，保留部分矢状面膝关节活动且无需对侧鞋垫或假肢侧向偏移，六名健全受试者经10分钟适应后可在跑步机上行走，步态模式在短期学习后趋于一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经胫骨假肢旁路装置存在对侧鞋垫或假肢侧向偏移等限制，且其对步态的影响缺乏系统记录，需要一种更自然、无额外约束的旁路方案以支持假肢器械的早期测试。

Method: 设计并实现开源经胫骨假肢旁路，保留部分矢状面膝关节活动；六名熟悉假肢的健全受试者适应约10分钟后，在跑步机上分别佩戴和不佩戴旁路行走，使用运动捕捉记录关节运动学并用OpenSim提取数据。

Result: 所有受试者均能在无扶手支持下行走，但旁路侧单支撑期较短（占步态周期27% vs. 45%）；旁路侧踝关节运动学与经胫骨假肢使用者报道一致，摆动期膝关节峰值屈曲从61°降至36°（p<0.001），健侧髋关节活动范围从35°增至51°（p<0.01）。

Conclusion: 该旁路装置虽不能完全复现假肢使用者的步态，但仅经10分钟学习即可获得一致的步态模式，为下肢假肢研究社区提供了便于开展健全受试者初步实验的有用工具。

Abstract: Prosthetic bypasses allow able-bodied individuals to walk with a prosthesis, enabling safe early-stage testing of new prosthetic devices before clinical trials. However, existing transtibial bypasses impose constraints, such as a contralateral shoe lift or a lateral offset of the prosthesis, and their effects on gait remain poorly documented. We present an open-source transtibial prosthetic bypass that preserves part of the sagittal knee mobility without these constraints. Six able-bodied participants familiarized themselves with the bypass for approximately 10~min, then walked on a treadmill with and without it, and joint kinematics were recorded with motion capture and then extracted with OpenSim. All participants were able to walk without handrail support, although single stance remained shorter on the bypass side (27% vs. 45% of the gait cycle). Bypass-side ankle kinematics were consistent with those reported for transtibial prosthesis users, whereas peak knee flexion during swing decreased from 61° to 36° (p<0.001) and hip range of the sound leg increased from 35° to 51° (p<0.01). Although gait with the bypass does not fully replicate that of prosthesis users, it yielded consistent gait patterns after only 10~min of learning. This bypass therefore provides a useful tool for the lower-limb prosthetics research community, facilitating preliminary experiments with able-bodied participants.

</details>


### [81] [SunLightBots: Turning sunlight into self-sustained, high-power motion in soft robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14729)
*Yoo Jin Lee,Melanie Torres Crespo,Zhongxiao Deng,Andrew Tejada-Vega,Rebecca Kramer-Bottiglio,Wenzhong Yan,Yimin Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SunLightBots 是亚克级、太阳光驱动的无缆软体机器人，通过自复位突跳机制实现约1个太阳光强下的自主运动，峰值质量比机械功率达89 W/kg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人可利用活性材料将环境刺激转化为运动，但环境能量采集功率低，而快速高机械输出动作需要高功率；亚克级尺度下载荷能力有限，难以集成常规电源与控制硬件，因此难以兼顾能量自主与持续自调节循环驱动。

Method: 提出自复位突跳（SRS）机制，将液晶弹性体（LCE）与突跳失稳结构结合：以低功率积累太阳能，瞬间释放为高速高功率运动，并通过光热-机械反馈复位；协同优化LCE驱动温度、光热转换、驱动应力与结构能垒，使系统在约1 Sun（100 mW/cm²）下工作。

Result: 优化LCE在约60°C时产生0.1 MPa驱动应力；系统在约1 Sun下实现89 W/kg峰值质量比机械功率；SunLightBots实现持续游泳、爬行和重复脉冲跳跃，1.5 Suns下游泳者每周期前进超过三个体长。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条材料-力学路径，用于构建完全由环境供能的自主软体机器人，解决了低功率能量采集与高功率机械输出之间的匹配难题。

Abstract: Soft robots built from active materials can directly convert environmental stimuli into motion, offering a route to energy autonomy and embodied control. However, environmental energy is generally harvested at low power, whereas rapid, mechanically demanding actions require high mechanical output power. Reconciling this mismatch while sustaining self-regulated cyclic actuation is challenging, particularly at the subgram scale, where limited payload capacity constrains the integration of conventional power and control hardware. Here, we present SunLightBots, a class of subgram, sunlight-powered, untethered soft robots that achieve autonomous locomotion under continuous illumination near 1 Sun (100 mW/cm2). Their operation is enabled by a self-resetting snapping (SRS) mechanism that integrates liquid crystal elastomers (LCEs) with snap-through instability to accumulate solar energy supplied at low power, release it as rapid, high-power motion, and reset through photothermal-mechanical feedback. Through coordinated optimization of the LCE actuation temperature, photothermal conversion, actuation stress, and structural energy barrier, the system operates near 1 Sun and achieves a peak mass-specific mechanical power of 89 W/kg. At this irradiance, the optimized LCE generates an actuation stress of 0.1 MPa at approximately 60 deg C. SunLightBots demonstrate sustained swimming, crawling, and repeated impulsive jumping. Under 1.5 Suns, the swimmer advances more than three body lengths per cycle. This work provides a materials-mechanics route toward autonomous soft robots powered entirely by the environment.

</details>


### [82] [Terrain-Aware Thompson Sampling for Multi-Drone Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14685)
*Riana Gagnon Souleiman,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化地形感知的多无人机Thompson Sampling算法，用于搜索与救援中定位失踪人员，并通过真实案例仿真验证其高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索与救援中，地形和植被遮挡会影响检测性能，且需在不确定区域探索与高概率区域利用之间取得平衡；现有覆盖规划或概率主动搜索方法在复杂地形下效率不足。

Method: 利用历史SAR案例数据建立失踪人员概率分布（位移环、高程、流域、扩散角、轨迹偏移），每格采用高斯后验表示，通过贝叶斯推断更新均值和方差；无人机团队以Thompson Sampling从后验采样选择候选动作，并以分数森林覆盖率量化观测不确定性。

Result: 基于YOSAR真实案例数据，在1.5和3.8平方公里区域、5/10/15/20架无人机、50 km/hr条件下仿真；1.5平方公里区域成功率约87%，耗时60至247秒；3.8平方公里区域成功率约75%至91%，耗时161至466秒。

Conclusion: 所提去中心化地形感知多无人机Thompson Sampling算法在不同规模和无人机配置下均能快速、高成功率地定位失踪人员，优于覆盖规划器和概率主动搜索方法。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a decentralized terrain-aware multi-drone Thompson Sampling algorithm tailored for search and rescue (SAR) applications. A probability distribution of locating a lost person is informed by terrain data, using established SAR models (displacement ring, elevation, watershed, dispersion angle, and track offset) derived from historical SAR case data on lost person behavior. Exploration is conducted by a team of drones using Thompson Sampling, where candidate actions are selected by drawing samples from this probability distribution, represented by a per-cell Gaussian posterior whose mean and variance are updated through Bayesian inference. This approach balances exploration of uncertain regions with exploitation of high-probability areas to locate a lost person. Observation uncertainty is incorporated through fractional forest coverage, which measures the percentage of an observation occluded by tree coverage and accounts for reduced detection performance in densely vegetated areas. Simulations using real SAR case data (YOSAR) evaluate the proposed method against a coverage planner and a probabilistic active search method in search regions of 1.5 and 3.8 square kilometers, with 5, 10, 15, and 20 agents flying at 50 km/hr. We demonstrate that our method achieves a success rate of approximately 87% within 60 to 247 seconds in the 1.5 square kilometer region, and success rates of approximately 75% to 91% within 161 to 466 seconds in the 3.8 square kilometer region, across different agent configurations.

</details>


### [83] [SALA: Semantically Aligned Latent Actions Enable Dual-Axis Scaling of Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17110)
*Jiachun Jin,Zetong Zhou,Haonan Wang,Xiao Yang,Jun Zhu,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SALA通过语言描述建立人类与机器人潜在动作的语义对应关系，无需配对数据即可构建共享潜在动作空间，用于具身基础模型训练，并在预训练与后训练阶段均展现出可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型仅通过视频重建学习，导致表征按视觉外观而非底层动作聚类，难以对齐人类与机器人的动作；同时，具身基础模型依赖昂贵机器人交互数据，而自我中心人类视频是有益补充。

Method: 提出SALA，利用无配对人类与机器人视频片段的语言描述估计语义相似度，将其转化为软邻域结构，引导人类潜在动作向语义相关的机器人潜在动作靠拢；同时通过机器人动作重建将潜在空间锚定于控制，从而形成共享潜在动作空间，为在人类与机器人视频上训练具身基础模型提供统一监督信号。

Result: 潜在动作监督沿两个维度扩展：预训练中增加通用人类视频量可一致降低下游任务的机器人动作预测误差；后训练中增加任务特定人类视频量可显著提升真实机器人任务成功率。

Conclusion: 通过语义对应而非仅视觉重建来学习潜在动作，可有效桥接人类与机器人动作表征，使人类视频成为可扩展的机器人策略监督来源。

Abstract: Embodied foundation models hinge on diverse robot interaction data, which are costly to collect. Egocentric human videos offer a complementary source of experience, and latent action models (LAMs) provide a popular means of leveraging these videos for policy supervision. Compared with world-action models (WAMs), LAMs can offer greater training efficiency and potentially lower inference costs by avoiding explicit pixel prediction during policy learning and execution. However, most existing LAMs learn latent actions solely through video reconstruction, which can cause their representations to cluster by visual appearance rather than by the underlying actions. As a result, they struggle to align human actions with their robot counterparts. To bridge the gap, we introduce SALA, which emphasizes establishing the semantic correspondence for latent actions. Without requiring paired data, we use the language descriptions of unpaired human and robot clips to estimate semantic similarities and supervise cross-embodiment correspondence. Specifically, we translate these similarities into soft neighborhood structures that guide human latent actions toward semantically related robot latents, while robot action reconstruction grounds the latent space in control. The resulting shared latent action space provides a unified supervision signal for training an embodied foundation model on both human and robot videos. Notably, latent action supervision enables scaling along two axes: (1) pre-training on increasing amounts of general human video consistently reduces robot action prediction error on downstream tasks, and (2) post-training on increasing amounts of task-specific human video substantially improves real-world robot task success rates.

</details>


### [84] [EgoX: Egocentric Cross-Embodiment Manipulation with Embodiment Dreaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16728)
*Yaru Niu,Binghong Chen,Changwei Yao,Shangtao Li,Zhenlong Fang,Revanth Krishna Senthilkumaran,Shuai Zhou,Yuemin Mao,Kalie Ching,Po-Ju Chao,Yudong Luo,Peiqi Yu,Heng Zhang,Hao Zhang,Bingqing Chen,Chen Qiu,H. Eric Tseng,Ye Yuan,Umar Iqbal,Changliu Liu,Jonathan Francis,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoX 是一个以自我为中心的跨实体学习框架，通过语义对应的观测与动作模态组织异构演示，预训练带实体梦境的模块化跨实体 Transformer（MXT+），再在目标实体上微调，在 13 个任务-实体对上显著提升任务进度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用操作策略可利用人类、真实机器人和仿真智能体的互补经验，但形态、感知与控制接口差异使联合演示学习困难。

Method: EgoX 以语义对应的观测和动作模态组织异构演示；每个实体为每种观测模态分配专用分词器、为每种动作模态分配专用专家，共享 Transformer 主干；引入仅训练时使用的实体梦境目标，预测未来视觉与本体状态的潜在表示；先混合预训练 MXT+，再在目标实体演示上微调。

Result: 在覆盖 5 个任务、6 种机器人实体（5 个物理机器人、1 个仿真人形）的 13 个任务-实体对上，跨实体预训练将平均归一化任务进度从零训练的 0.63 提升至 0.80；分布外条件下从 0.53 提升至 0.77；消融显示更广预训练混合、模块化接口和实体梦境均有收益，梦境使不同实体的潜在几何更相似。

Conclusion: EgoX 证明通过语义对齐的模块化接口与实体梦境目标，可有效利用异构跨实体演示，显著提升通用操作策略性能，尤其在分布外场景。

Abstract: Generalist manipulation policies can benefit from the complementary experience of humans, real robots, and simulated agents. However, differences in morphology, sensing, and control interfaces make it difficult to learn effectively from their combined demonstrations. We introduce EgoX, an egocentric cross-embodiment learning framework that organizes heterogeneous demonstrations through semantically corresponding observation and action modalities. EgoX pretrains a Modularized Cross-Embodiment Transformer with Embodiment Dreaming (MXT+) on this mixture and fine-tunes it on target-embodiment demonstrations. Within each embodiment, MXT+ assigns a dedicated tokenizer to each observation modality and a dedicated expert to each action modality, with a Transformer trunk shared across embodiments. A training-only embodiment-dreaming objective predicts latent representations of future visual and proprioceptive states, encouraging the shared trunk to model each embodiment's dynamics. Across 13 task-embodiment pairs spanning five tasks and six robot embodiments, including five physical robots and one simulated humanoid, cross-embodiment pretraining raises MXT+'s average normalized task progress from 0.63 to 0.80 over training from scratch. The gains are particularly pronounced under out-of-distribution conditions, where average task progress increases from 0.53 to 0.77. Ablations demonstrate the benefits of broader pretraining mixtures, modular interfaces, and embodiment dreaming, with dreaming producing more similar latent geometry across embodiments. Project page: this http URL .

</details>


### [85] [Rethinking Actions: Continuity-Aware Priors for World-Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16645)
*Shaobo Yang,Zichen Ai,Ziyang Ma,Qian Yang,Bin Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LG-DKGP利用专家演示中的时间结构学习动作延续先验，结合神经均值预测与高斯过程残差，在视觉世界模型规划中显著提升多任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家演示的动作具有时间连续性，但现有视觉世界模型规划多采用通用平滑先验，未充分利用演示动作的时间结构；作者探究更贴合专家连续性的动作提议能否改进规划。

Method: 提出局部门控深度核高斯过程先验（LG-DKGP）：融合近期与较长程的动作历史，预测神经延续并学习时间高斯过程残差；用训练得到的探索尺度在修正均值附近初始化搜索，由冻结的世界模型评估目标进展；训练先拟合神经均值，再用专家未来动作联合优化先验与GP残差。

Result: 在五个模拟任务（含原生目标与片段级目标达成）上宏平均成功率达87.07%，对比INTACT的71.03%、PRISM的61.66%和LeWM的58.66%；组件干预与相对专家的动作结构比较支持演示信息驱动的延续，并揭示视觉条件、历史长度与规划视野的任务依赖效应。

Conclusion: 动作提议中融入专家演示的时间结构可有效提升视觉世界模型规划性能，且各组件与设计选择的效果随任务而异。

Abstract: Actions in expert demonstrations exhibit temporal structure. An ordered-versus-shuffled analysis across five environments confirms this dependence. We ask whether action proposals that better reflect expert continuity can improve visual world-model planning. Rather than imposing generic smoothness, we learn continuations from the temporal structure of demonstrated actions. Our locality-gated deep-kernel Gaussian-process prior, LG-DKGP, combines recent and longer-range executed action history, predicts a neural continuation, and learns a temporal Gaussian-process (GP) residual. A training-derived exploration scale initializes search around the corrected mean; a frozen world model evaluates progress toward the goal. Training first fits the neural mean, then jointly optimizes the prior with the GP residual using expert future actions. The resulting planner achieves 87.07\% macro success across five simulated tasks spanning native and fragment-level goal attainment, compared with 71.03\% for INTACT, 61.66\% for PRISM, and 58.66\% for LeWM. Component interventions and expert-relative action-structure comparisons support demonstration-informed continuations and reveal task-dependent effects of visual conditioning, history length, and planning horizon.

</details>


### [86] [BlinkMimic: Training a Humanoid Motion Tracker in Minutes on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16422)
*Fuyi Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BlinkMimic将全身运动跟踪策略训练加速到单张RTX 4090上3.4分钟训练全部40个LAFAN1动作，达到归一化回报0.82且无失败片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（SuperMimic、InstantTrack）训练一个覆盖动作库的跟踪策略仍需数小时GPU时间，作者希望进一步缩短训练时间。

Method: 在SuperMimic（通过并行仿真学习的世界模型反向传播）和InstantTrack（Muon更新、参考动作周围的残差策略、平均部署策略）基础上，将训练时间分解为单次迭代成本、拟合动作所需迭代数、消除最后失败所需迭代数，并新增三个组件：训练进展后递减的步长、由部署条件下部署策略失败触发的重置、整个迭代的GPU图捕获。

Result: 在仿真Unitree G1上，单张RTX 4090训练全部40个LAFAN1片段仅需3.4分钟（三次连续检查点归一化回报≥0.82且无失败片段，三个随机种子均值）；三种子留一消融实验标明各组件影响的训练时间项，并通过梯度、更新规则和失败案例分析解释其效果。

Conclusion: BlinkMimic是一套可显著加速全身运动跟踪策略训练的配方，各新增组件分别作用于训练时间的不同组成部分，消融与机理分析验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Whole-body motion tracking lets a humanoid robot follow reference motions, but training a tracking policy for a library of motions has usually taken hours of GPU time. SuperMimic showed that the policy can instead be trained by backpropagating through a world model learned from parallel simulation, and InstantTrack added Muon updates, a residual policy around the reference and an averaged deployed policy. We build on both and present BlinkMimic, a recipe that makes this training faster. On a simulated Unitree G1, BlinkMimic trains one policy for all 40 LAFAN1 clips in 3.4 minutes on a single RTX 4090, measured until three consecutive checkpoints reach a normalized return of at least 0.82 with no failed clip (mean of three seeds). We split training time into the cost of an iteration, the iterations needed to fit the motions, and the iterations needed to remove the last failures, and assign each component, inherited or new, to one of these terms. The new components are a step size that decreases once training has progressed, resets directed by failures of the deployed policy under deployment conditions, and GPU graph capture of the whole iteration. A leave-one-out ablation with three seeds per component shows which term each component affects, and further measurements of the gradient, the update rule and the failure cases explain these effects. Code and per-seed results are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [87] [What Can a VLA Learn from the Agent That Calls It? Task-Level Experience Distillation for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16246)
*Jinze Tao,Yuxiao Han,Kangkang Dong,Yancong Wei,Xiaojun Zhu,Bokui Chen,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: H2E将导航智能体会话蒸馏为VLA训练数据，使VLA在测试时无需LLM规划器即可独立导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导航智能体每回合都需调用LLM规划器，带来额外成本与延迟；作者希望将成功经验蒸馏进VLA而非规划器，让VLA测试时单独运行。

Method: 提出任务级经验蒸馏方法H2E：把智能体会话中每个动作在原始任务指令下转为VLA动作，以到达位姿为目标；对偏离目标的步骤屏蔽损失；将距目标1米内的访问状态标为停止；再用低秩适配更新VLA。

Result: 在REVERIE-CE验证未见过集全部3189个回合上，H2E将8B VLA的成功率从35.0%提升至44.9%。

Conclusion: H2E能让VLA学会到达目标时停止，并找到基座模型从未到达的目标，从而在测试时无需LLM规划器独立完成导航。

Abstract: Navigation agents use a large language model (LLM) planner to call a vision-language-action (VLA) model. Each episode needs the LLM, adding cost and delay. We distill successful agent experience into the VLA instead of distilling the planner. The VLA then navigates alone at test time. Agent sessions are not VLA demonstrations. They include motions driven by planner tools and sub-instructions, along with detours. We present H2E, a task-level experience distillation method that converts these sessions into VLA training data. Every motion becomes a VLA action under the original task instruction, with the resulting poses as targets. H2E masks the loss on steps that move away from the goal. It labels every visited state within 1 m of the goal as a stop. We then update the VLA with low-rank adaptation. The VLA learns to stop at the goal and find goals the base model never reaches. On all 3,189 REVERIE-CE val-unseen episodes, H2E raises the success rate of an 8B VLA from 35.0% to 44.9%.

</details>


### [88] [DexRemo: A Force-Sensing Hand Platform and Task Suite for Contact-Rich Removal Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15981)
*Liman Wang,Hanyang Zhong,Sun Zhaole,Wenting Cao,Babar Jamil,Andy Tyrrell,Shan Luo,Jihong Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexRemo：三指高力机械手与力传感任务套件，使用ACT策略融合腕部坐标系指尖力实现移除操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移除操作（分离粘附、嵌入、污染或机械约束目标）依赖部分可观测的接触状态演化，视觉和运动学信息不足。

Method: 提出DexRemo三指机械手（高力驱动、模块化指尖、三轴指尖力传感）和九场景真实机器人任务套件；将指尖力表达在共享腕部中心坐标系；基于Action Chunking Transformer融合多视角视觉、本体感知、指尖位姿和力观测，从演示预测闭环动作块。

Result: 六个核心基准任务和三个扩展场景评估中，学习策略取得一致的移除性能；消融显示腕部坐标系力（尤其结合指尖位姿）相比局部坐标系力和无力基线提升了稳定性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 共享腕部坐标系的指尖力表示与指尖位姿结合能有效改善多指移除操作策略的稳定性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Many manipulation tasks require separating adhered, embedded, contaminated, or mechanically constrained targets. We study this setting as removal manipulation, where progress depends on evolving contact states that are only partially observable from vision and kinematics. We introduce DexRemo, a three-finger robotic hand with high-force actuation, modular fingertips, and three-axis fingertip force sensing, together with a nine-scenario real-robot task suite spanning adhesive detachment, surface cleaning and self-cleaning, and occluded retrieval. To make multi-finger force observations easier to fuse, we express fingertip forces in a shared wrist-centered frame, giving force components consistent geometric meaning across fingers and time. Using an Action Chunking Transformer backbone, policies fuse multi-view vision, proprioception, fingertip pose, and force observations to predict closed-loop action chunks from demonstrations. Evaluation uses six core benchmark tasks for controlled fingertip-input and force-frame ablations, plus three extension scenarios for additional held-out coverage. Across these evaluations, the learned policies achieve consistent removal performance, and ablations show that wrist-frame forces, especially with fingertip pose, improve stability and generalization over local-frame force inputs and no-force baselines. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [89] [What Makes a VLA Reactive? A Study of Execution Strategies for Dynamic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15491)
*Zhuoyue Li,Zhendong Han,Pankhuri Vanjani,Wenting Li,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型预测未来动作块，但物体移动时动作块在执行前会过时。本文研究使预训练VLA具有反应性的因素，发现最佳预测时域约1秒，执行时域取决于检查点，异步执行下短时域更有帮助，保持轨迹连续的方法优于仅降低延迟的方法，且反应性同样取决于执行流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型预测未来动作块，但在动态环境中物体移动会导致动作块在执行前过时，因此需要理解如何使预训练VLA对动态场景具有反应性。

Method: 在两个动态仿真基准和三个真实机器人任务上使用π0.5模型，变化预测与执行时域，比较同步与异步推理，测试更快传递动作或保持连续动作块的方法，并通过运动线索和潜空间强化学习自适应策略。

Result: 最佳预测时域约1秒，最佳执行时域取决于检查点；短时域在异步执行下更有帮助；保持轨迹连续的方法优于仅降低延迟的方法；自适应策略修复了针对性失败，但在传送带任务上改变执行时域获得同等收益。

Conclusion: 反应性既取决于策略也取决于执行流水线，因此两者应一起评估。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions, and when objects move, those chunks go stale before they are executed. We study what makes a pretrained VLA reactive, using \pihalf{} on two dynamic simulation benchmarks and three real-robot tasks. We vary the prediction and execution horizons, compare synchronous and asynchronous inference, test methods that deliver actions sooner or keep successive chunks continuous, and adapt the policy with motion cues and latent-space reinforcement learning. The best prediction horizon is about one second, and the best execution horizon depends on the checkpoint. Short horizons help more with asynchronous execution, and methods that keep the trajectory continuous outperform those that only reduce latency. Adapting the policy repairs targeted failures, but on the conveyor task changing the execution horizon gained as much. Reactivity depends on the execution pipeline as much as on the policy, so both should be evaluated together.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [AstroMemBench: A Benchmark for Memory Systems in Embodied Spacecraft Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16590)
*You Wu,Guhong Zhang,Hongwei Li,Xinyue Hong,Yuan Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AstroMemBench基准与Scenario-Challenge-Capability设计方法，用16项记忆子挑战、140个模板、2240个实例评估航天器具身智能体的记忆能力，并对比六种记忆系统配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期航天器自主运行要求具身智能体在通信延迟、时间紧迫、环境不确定、复杂物理交互及功耗算力受限条件下利用运行历史，现有工作缺乏可执行、可复现的记忆能力评测手段。

Method: 采用Scenario-Challenge-Capability设计方法将需求映射为可执行记忆测试；覆盖行星表面作业、在轨服务和深空探测三类共9个任务的16项记忆子挑战，构建140个模板和2240个实例；使用确定性环境按任务完成、状态变化、安全性和资源使用对动作打分；开展9种条件下的消融实验，并比较六种配置。

Result: 消融表明基准对智能体历史信息恢复与使用精度及记忆系统设计差异敏感；Mem0、Chain-of-Memory、LightMem与RAG在同一会话内均优于原生Pi Agent，但三种记忆系统相对RAG无明显优势；会话重启后Mem0比RAG高12.25分；收益主要体现在指令保持和结果复用，而非持续状态跟踪与能量预留。

Conclusion: AstroMemBench可支撑具身航天器智能体记忆系统的设计、开发与选型，其1.0版本与评测代码已公开。

Abstract: Long-term spacecraft autonomy requires embodied agents to use operational history under communication delays, time-critical operations, environmental uncertainty, complex physical interactions, power and computational constraints, and safety and reliability requirements. We introduce AstroMemBench and a Scenario-Challenge-Capability design method that maps these requirements to executable memory tests. The benchmark covers 16 memory subchallenges through 140 templates and 2240 instances across nine tasks in planetary surface operations, on-orbit servicing, and deep-space exploration. A deterministic environment scores actions for task completion, state changes, safety, and resource use. Ablations under nine conditions show that the benchmark is sensitive to how accurately agents recover and use historical information and to differences in memory-system design. Main experiments compare six configurations. Mem0, Chain-of-Memory, LightMem, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improve same-session mean scores over native Pi Agent, with no clear advantage of the three memory systems over RAG. After a session restart, Mem0 outperforms RAG by 12.25 points. Benefits favor instruction retention and result reuse over ongoing state tracking and energy reservation. AstroMemBench supports the design, development, and selection of memory systems for embodied spacecraft agents. AstroMemBench 1.0 and its evaluation code are publicly available: this https URL .

</details>


### [91] [HeMeR: Heterogeneous Memory Reconciliation for Embodied Agents via Structured KV Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16487)
*Saehun Chun,Wonje Choi,Jinwoo Jang,Taeyoon Kwack,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HeMeR通过记忆上下文更新间的KV缓存对账机制，仅重算受影响部分并复用未变内容的KV状态，在真实机器人部署中实现83.20%任务成功率并将每步推理时间从2.94秒降至1.87秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构记忆模块通过独立接口访问，每次记忆上下文更新都被当作全新输入送入基础模型，导致即使大部分记忆内容未变也必须完整重算KV缓存，无法选择性复用。

Method: 提出推理框架HeMeR，跨动作决策跟踪陈述性、程序性与工作记忆内容，保留未变内容的KV状态，仅对受记忆内容更新影响的KV状态执行对账式重算。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Habitat及真实机器人部署上评估；真实场景中任务成功率达83.20%，相比MemRL每步推理时间由2.94秒降至1.87秒。

Conclusion: HeMeR在记忆内容随交互演化时实现了高效且稳定的推理，并保持KV状态兼容，适用于需协同使用异构记忆内容的具身智能任务。

Abstract: Embodied agents make decisions by combining information from heterogeneous memory modules, including declarative memory for explicit knowledge, procedural memory for learned routines, and working memory for current observations. During interaction, the memory contents required for decision making change with the agent's state, goal, and task progress, while many contents remain shared across consecutive decisions. However, existing memory modules are typically accessed through separate interfaces, so each updated memory context is often processed as a new input to the foundation model. This prevents selective key-value (KV) cache reuse for unchanged memory contents and forces full KV cache recomputation even when much of the memory context remains valid. We introduce HeMeR, an inference framework that performs KV reconciliation between dynamically updated memory contexts and reusable KV states. HeMeR tracks declarative, procedural, and working memory contents across action decisions, preserves KV states for unchanged contents, and reconciles only the KV states affected by memory-content updates. This enables efficient and stable inference as memory contents evolve during interaction. We evaluate HeMeR on ALFWorld, Habitat, and real-world robotic deployments requiring coordinated use of heterogeneous memory contents. In the real-world setting, HeMeR achieves 83.20% task success and reduces per-step inference time from 2.94 to 1.87 seconds compared with MemRL, while preserving compatible KV states as task progress updates the memory context.

</details>


### [92] [Marginal-Preserving Coupled Diffusion Composition for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15585)
*Hengrui Zhang,Qiyu Wang,Yuhu Cheng,Brooks Paige,Xuesong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练推理方法MCDC，通过构造块间正相关的耦合高斯初始化，在保持各块边际标准高斯分布的同时协调相邻轨迹块的模式选择，提升组合扩散规划器的全局一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态环境下，独立初始化的轨迹块可能去噪到不兼容的重叠模式，导致拼接后计划局部可行但全局不一致；现有补救方法需在推理时增加搜索、引导、剪枝或图结构，计算成本高或依赖任务专用机制。

Method: MCDC为预训练组合扩散规划器设计免训练推理方法：不独立采样各块噪声，而是构造耦合高斯初始化，使各块边际严格保持标准高斯分布（满足预训练去噪器要求），同时相邻块噪声分量正相关。

Result: 证明了边际保持的精确性，并在简单多模态重叠模型下证明正耦合提高兼容模式选择概率；在OGBench长时程拼接任务上，MCDC一致优于独立组合扩散采样，推理时间几乎不变，且相比基于搜索的组合方法取得更优的成功率-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 图结构化块间噪声依赖可作为轻量协调机制，在不增加推理成本的前提下提升组合轨迹规划的全局一致性。

Abstract: Compositional diffusion planners extend short-horizon trajectory models to long-horizon goal-conditioned planning by generating and stitching multiple local trajectory chunks. However, in multimodal environments, local plausibility is not sufficient for global consistency: independently initialized chunks may denoise toward incompatible overlap modes, producing plans that are individually feasible but incoherent after stitching. Existing remedies typically add search, guidance, pruning, or graph-based structure at inference time, increasing computational cost or relying on task-specific machinery. We instead investigate graph-structured dependence among chunk-wise initial diffusion noises as a lightweight coordination mechanism for compositional trajectory planning. We propose \textbf{M}arginal-Preserving \textbf{C}oupled \textbf{D}iffusion \textbf{C}omposition (MCDC), a training-free inference method for pretrained compositional diffusion planners. Instead of sampling chunk noises independently, MCDC constructs coupled Gaussian initializations whose per-chunk marginals remain exactly standard Gaussian, as required by the pretrained denoiser, while neighboring chunks receive positively correlated noise components. This preserves the standard-Gaussian initialization marginal of each chunk while biasing adjacent chunks toward compatible overlap-mode choices. We prove exact marginal preservation and show, under a simple multimodal overlap model, that positive coupling increases the probability of compatible mode selection. On OGBench long-horizon stitch tasks, MCDC consistently improves over independent compositional diffusion sampling while retaining nearly the same inference time, and achieves a favorable success--cost trade-off compared with search-based composition methods.

</details>


### [93] [Towards Robust Vision-Language Reasoning: A Multi-Stage Optimization Pipeline with Masked Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15238)
*Shunming Liu,Pei Huang,Yajing Sun,Huaxiao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向标准视觉语言模型的多阶段优化流程，将离散扩散生成用于自动驾驶，无需辅助动作专家或专用MoE Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散生成通过迭代细化整体token序列去噪，具备全局规划与渐进校正能力，适合自动驾驶；但从通用场景迁移到驾驶领域在训练与推理上存在显著域间差距。

Method: 提出涵盖后训练与推理的多阶段优化流程：监督微调阶段优化规划输入表示以抑制任务响应中的幻觉；强化学习阶段引入指标引导的多样性过滤以识别更优学习起点并提升策略探索；推理阶段提出动态自动驾驶解码（DAD），按场景复杂度自适应调整解码模式；据此构建Masked Diffusion Drive（MD-Drive）。

Result: MD-Drive较未优化模型性能提升29.7%；在NAVSIM闭环基准及nuScenes、DriveCoT开环数据集上，优于同架构自回归VLM基线，并相较不同架构系统在性能与计算成本间取得更优平衡。

Conclusion: 离散扩散训练与推理可使标准VLM架构在自动驾驶中超越同架构自回归基线，并获得更优的性能-计算成本权衡。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion generation is emerging as a competitive alternative to autoregressive generation by denoising the entire token sequence through iterative refinement. Its global planning and progressive correction capabilities are particularly well suited to autonomous driving. However, transferring this generation paradigm from general-purpose settings to driving introduces substantial domain gaps in both training and inference. To unlock its potential, we propose a multi-stage optimization pipeline for standard vision-language model architectures, without auxiliary action experts or specialized mixture-of-experts Transformer, spanning post-training and inference. During supervised fine-tuning, we optimize the representation of planning inputs to suppress hallucinations in task responses. During reinforcement learning, we introduce metric-guided diversity filtering to identify stronger learning starting points and improve policy exploration. At inference time, we propose Dynamic Autonomous Driving Decoding (DAD), which adaptively adjusts decoding modes according to scene complexity. Based on this pipeline, we develop Masked Diffusion Drive (MD-Drive), improving its performance by 29.7\% over the unoptimized model. Experiments on the NAVSIM closed loop benchmark and the nuScenes and DriveCoT open loop datasets show that MD-Drive outperforms autoregressive VLM baselines with the same architecture, while achieving a more favorable balance between performance and computational cost than systems with different architectures through discrete diffusion training and inference.

</details>


### [94] [Can MLLMs Reason over Surround-View Driving Videos? A Multi-View Autonomous Driving QA Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15225)
*Shunming Liu,Jianyi Peng,Cheng Li,Lu Hou,Pei Huang,Yajing Sun,Huaxiao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MVDriveBench基准，系统评估多模态大模型在自动驾驶环视时空理解与推理能力，发现现有模型能力不足且与闭环驾驶性能相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM主要关注前视信息，其环视时空理解与推理能力未知，而真实驾驶常需前视以外的信息。

Method: 构建高信息密度视频问答基准MVDriveBench，含同步环视输入、时空理解与推理链导向评估，设计12类QA任务覆盖从低层视觉感知到高层驾驶决策的完整推理流程，并评测23个开源、闭源及专家MLLM。

Result: 当前MLLM环视时空推理能力仍不足；在AlpaSim中对专家模型进行闭环评估，发现环视时空推理能力与闭环驾驶性能存在相关性。

Conclusion: 环视时空推理对可靠车辆控制至关重要，现有MLLM在此方面仍需提升，MVDriveBench为系统评估提供了基准。

Abstract: Surround-view video understanding in autonomous driving is a crucial capability for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to achieve reliable driving reasoning. Real-world driving often requires information beyond the front-view camera. Existing MLLMs primarily focus on front-view information, while their spatio-temporal understanding and reasoning abilities remain unknown. To address this gap, we propose MVDriveBench, a high-information-density video QA benchmark for autonomous driving, characterized by synchronized surround-view inputs, spatio-temporal understanding, and reasoning-chain-oriented evaluation. We design 12 categories of QA tasks that cover the complete reasoning pipeline from low-level visual perception to high-level driving decision-making, enabling a systematic evaluation of MLLMs surround-view spatio-temporal understanding and reasoning capabilities in complex driving environments. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 23 open-source, closed-source, and expert MLLMs on MVDriveBench. The results show that current MLLMs still exhibit insufficient surround-view spatio-temporal reasoning ability. Meanwhile, closed-loop evaluations of specialist models in AlpaSim reveal a correlation between surround-view spatio-temporal reasoning capabilities and closed-loop driving performance, highlighting the importance of comprehensive multi-view understanding for reliable vehicle control.

</details>


### [95] [Robust Task-Aware State Estimation from Pre-trained Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14781)
*Guy Azran,Sarah Keren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预训练感知模型（如VLM）输出概率构建θ置信最小信念集（MBS_θ）的方法，通过PRaVaSS算法在多项式复杂度内近似状态概率并支持下游决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将感知模型预测确定化为单一最佳猜测，错误时决策不安全或不可行；而维护完整符号状态概率分布又难以处理，需要折中方案。

Method: 利用VLM输出层token分布等感知概率，构建三层信念系统从特征级预测和任务约束近似状态概率，保持精简因子化表示；提出PRaVaSS算法利用该结构构造MBS_θ，无约束情形下具正确性保证和多项式复杂度。

Result: 以VLM为状态估计主干验证了方法的理论性质、随问题规模的可扩展性，以及在任务规划流水线中下游决策的改进。

Conclusion: MBS_θ在最小性与计算可行性间取得平衡，仅考虑最可能状态以增强决策鲁棒性，是一种实用且可扩展的不确定性感知状态估计与决策框架。

Abstract: Agents that perform state estimation using pretrained perception models, e.g., Vision-Language Models (VLMs), face the fundamental challenge that predictions are uncertain. Still, most approaches determinize these predictions into a single best-guess state and make decisions as if it were ground truth. This is brittle because when the guess is wrong, the agent's decisions may be unsafe or infeasible. At the other extreme, maintaining a full probability distribution over symbolic states is often intractable. We propose a middle path, drawing on the underlying probabilities the perception model already produces (e.g., a VLM's output-layer token distributions) to construct a $\theta$-confidence Minimal Belief Set (MBS$_\theta$), a minimal subset of states whose cumulative probability meets a user-specified belief-mass threshold. The minimality of an MBS$_\theta$ ensures that the agent considers only the most likely states, enhancing robustness in downstream decision-making while maintaining computational tractability. Using a novel three-layered belief system, we efficiently approximate individual state probabilities from per-feature predictions and task-level constraints. On this belief construction, we base our MBS$_\theta$ construction, while maintaining only a lean factored representation of the belief. Our algorithm, Probability-Ranked Valid State Search (PRaVaSS), takes advantage of the unique structure of this belief system to construct an MBS$_\theta$ with correctness guarantees and polynomial complexity in the unconstrained case. Using a VLM as the backbone of our state estimator, we validate our method's theoretical properties, scalability with problem size, and improvement of downstream decision-making when used in a task-planning pipeline.

</details>


### [96] [AFTER: A World Model with Adaptive Fast--Slow Timescales for Error-Driven Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15215)
*Haoyi Zhang,Qi Zhang,Yunfan Ren,Yi Bin,Lei Zhu,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AFTER-WM世界模型，通过快慢双时间尺度校正机制实现机器人动力学突变的自适应，在仿真和真实无人机数据上多步预测误差最低且控制回报最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人交互中动力学常发生突然、持续且初始未知的变化（如抓取未知负载），现有世界模型难以同时满足快速响应、稳定区分持续变化与瞬态扰动、以及无新观测时保持适应结果三项要求，导致开环预测误差累积并误导动作。

Method: 受人类大脑预测编码的多时间尺度适应启发，构建AFTER-WM：预测误差驱动一个快速校正（随时间衰减）和一个慢速校正（持续保留），并动态调整预训练专家的组合权重而不修改专家本身，以权衡响应性、稳定性与持续性。

Result: 在五个仿真机器人数据集和真实无人机数据上，AFTER-WM的多步平均误差低于全部十个对比模型，在联合故障控制任务中平均回报最高；仿真中多步平均误差比最强对比模型低16%-34%，在变化后1-4步及仅瞬态扰动下误差最低，第40步误差也最低。

Conclusion: AFTER-WM通过快慢时间尺度误差驱动保持机制有效解决了动力学适应中响应性、稳定性与持续性的权衡，验证了其预测与控制性能优势，代码与数据集将开源。

Abstract: In robotics and embodied AI, a core challenge for world models is adapting to sudden, persistent, yet initially unknown changes in robot dynamics during interaction. For instance, when a robotic arm grasps an object with an unknown payload, the robot dynamics change suddenly. If the world model fails to adapt, its prediction errors accumulate over the open-loop rollout and ultimately mislead the robot's actions. Existing world models, however, struggle to simultaneously satisfy three critical requirements for dynamics adaptation: responsiveness, reacting promptly to sudden changes to curb prediction error accumulation; stability, distinguishing persistent changes from transient disturbances to prevent incorrect updates; and persistence, retaining the adapted dynamics without new observations so that later predictions do not revert to the old dynamics. Inspired by predictive coding in the human brain with multi-timescale adaptation, we propose AFTER-WM, a world model with Adaptive Fast-slow Timescales for Error-driven Retention, whose prediction errors drive a fast correction that fades and a slow correction that persists, while dynamically adjusting the combination weights of pre-trained experts without altering them, to overcome the trade-offs among responsiveness, stability and persistence. On five simulated robot datasets and real UAV data, AFTER-WM has a lower multi-step mean error than all ten compared models and the highest mean return in a joint-failure control task. In simulation, its multistep mean error is 16%-34% below the strongest compared model's and is also the lowest 1-4 steps after a change and under transient disturbances alone, and its step-40 error is the lowest as well, reflecting responsiveness, stability and persistence. All code (method, baselines, experiments) and datasets will be released online.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Epigraph Form Reinforcement Learning-based Safe Trajectory Tracking Control for Connected and Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14903)
*Xiaoke Yang,Jin Chen,Yafei Wang,Qi Zhang,Ming Li,Haiwen Wu,Chengliang Yin,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STTC-EFRL框架，将安全轨迹跟踪建模为epigraph形式min-max优化，提升训练稳定性与跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非学习STT方法模型简化导致精度次优，Lagrangian DRL因乘子增大会放大策略梯度而训练不稳定。

Method: 将STT问题表述为epigraph形式min-max优化，设计基于递减标量的学习机制调整策略梯度下降方向而不增大梯度幅值，并提出高效ray-casting方法计算多面体缩放因子以量化安全性。

Result: 实验含车辆在环测试表明，STTC-EFRL跟踪精度高于非学习基线，训练稳定性优于常用Lagrangian DRL方法，并满足联网环境下的安全要求。

Conclusion: STTC-EFRL能兼顾高跟踪精度、稳定训练与安全性，适用于高度动态环境中的CAV安全轨迹跟踪。

Abstract: Safe trajectory tracking (STT) aims to enable autonomous vehicles to follow a reference trajectory accurately while avoiding collisions. This problem is particularly challenging for Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) in highly dynamic environments. Existing non-learning-based STT methods often use simplified vehicle and interaction models to meet real-time requirements, which may result in suboptimal tracking accuracy. Although Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) can help address this limitation, widely used Lagrangian DRL methods often suffer from unstable training because increasing Lagrange multipliers amplify policy-gradient magnitudes. To address these issues, we propose a novel reinforcement learning framework for STT, called STT Control Epigraph Form RL (STTC-EFRL). In this framework, the STT problem is formulated as an epigraph-form min-max optimization problem. Based on this formulation, we develop a learning mechanism that uses a decreasing scalar related to the epigraph to adjust the policy gradient descent direction without increasing the gradient magnitude, thereby improving training stability. We also develop a computationally efficient ray-casting method to compute a polyhedron scaling factor for safety quantification. Experimental results, including Vehicle-in-the-Loop tests, show that STTC-EFRL achieves higher tracking accuracy than the considered non-learning-based baseline and more stable training than widely used Lagrangian DRL methods, while satisfying safety requirements in connected environments.

</details>


### [98] [SPIRE: Task-agnostic embodied continual learning through autonomous situation inference and causal motor-primitive reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14840)
*Yiyang Jia,Mingyang Lyu,Gongce Cao,Feifei Zhao,Yinqian Sun,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPIRE是一个循环脉冲神经网络系统，通过从动作-结果证据中推断潜在情境并在线学习情境特定运动执行器，实现无需任务标签的具身持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身持续学习要求控制器在没有任务标签和已知任务边界的情况下推断情境变化、保留先前行为并复用运动技能；核心挑战在于决定何时恢复早期行为以及哪些技能可支持新学习。

Method: 提出SPIRE（Situation-guided Primitive Integration for Recurrent Embodiment）循环脉冲系统：从动作-结果证据推断潜在情境，在线学习情境特定运动执行器；成熟执行器成为受保护的源，与目标情境残差结合驱动循环脉冲运动群体。

Result: 在坐标、三关节和隐力控制任务中，SPIRE维持控制、自主推断情境、保留先前行为，并将未见源组合的平均达标交互次数较匹配的新控制器减少约40%；回退兼容源执行器在所有匹配干预中均损害早期习得，证明因果复用；与七种循环持续学习和模块化控制对照方法相比，SPIRE的平均先验学习优势为最强无任务标签替代方法的2.4倍。

Conclusion: 结果证明了在连续具身控制中新学习时运动技能可实现自主恢复与因果复用。

Abstract: Embodied continual learning requires controllers to infer changing situations, retain prior behavior and reuse motor skills without task labels or known boundaries. The challenge is deciding when to recover earlier behavior and which skills can support new learning. We introduce Situation-guided Primitive Integration for Recurrent Embodiment (SPIRE), a recurrent-spiking system that infers latent contexts from action-outcome evidence and learns context-specific motor actors online. Mature actors become protected sources that combine with a target-context residual to drive recurrent-spiking motor populations. Across coordinate, three-joint and hidden-force control, SPIRE maintained control, autonomously inferred situations, retained prior behavior and accelerated acquisition of an unseen source composition, reducing mean interactions to criterion by approximately 40% relative to matched fresh controllers. Rewinding compatible source actors impaired early acquisition in every matched intervention, demonstrating causal reuse. Against seven recurrent continual-learning and modular-control comparators, SPIRE achieved 2.4 times the mean prior-learning advantage of the strongest eligible task-free alternative selected for each paired stream. These results demonstrate autonomous recovery and causal reuse of motor skills for new learning in continuous embodied control.

</details>


### [99] [A Recipe for Physical Action Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16450)
*Shaojiang Wang,Geer Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文以条件分布为目标统一分析机器人学习中的容量、数据与部署决策，提出基于嵌套学习Oracle的风险核算及训练处方，并通过数值实验和206条公开轨迹验证信息设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模型容量选择与演示预算分配需同时考虑数据组成、表示、优化与部署，但这些决策缺乏统一的风险与信息论分析框架。

Method: 构建条件分布目标连接各决策；利用嵌套学习Oracle进行精确风险核算，结合局部信息几何、条件互信息和经验收敛给出条件训练处方；进行已知目标数值检验，并用206条公开PushT轨迹在不拟合策略下评估固定特征的信息设计准则。

Result: 数值检验揭示了容量-数据耦合、表示导致的混合失配，以及部署KL增大而负对数似然下降的案例；在非对称评估目标下，所选分配使准则较目标匹配降低4.5%–6.2%，平衡目标下无收益。

Conclusion: 研究明确了哪些量可支撑训练建议，并指出看似有利的预测指标在特定条件下可能具有不同解释。

Abstract: Choosing robot model capacity and demonstration budgets also requires decisions about data composition, representation, optimization, and deployment. We connect these decisions through a common conditional-distribution target. Nested learning oracles give exact risk accounting; local information geometry, conditional mutual information, and empirical convergence provide conditional training prescriptions. Known-target numerical checks expose capacity-data coupling, representation-induced mixture mismatch, and an example where deployment KL increases while negative log-likelihood decreases. An analysis of 206 public PushT trajectories evaluates fixed-feature information-design criteria without fitting a policy. Under asymmetric evaluation targets, the selected allocations reduce this criterion by 4.5-6.2% relative to target matching; balanced targets show no benefit. These results clarify which quantities can support training recommendations and when an apparently favorable predictive metric has a different interpretation.

</details>


### [100] [Imitating the Imperfect: Offline-to-Online Robust Imitation Learning from Heterogeneous Demonstrators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16020)
*Cheng Pan,Nan Lu,Kai Arulkumaran,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEOS-IL框架，从异构且质量不一的离线演示中联合学习状态-动作最优性评分模型和各演示者的潜在专业水平，并将评分模型融入在线策略优化，以提升模仿学习在低质量混合数据下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习大多假设演示者同质或接近最优，但实际数据常由专业水平未知且各异的演示者产生，呈现高度混合质量，这一不现实假设限制了方法在真实场景中的适用性。

Method: LEOS-IL从无标签的次优离线演示中联合学习（i）状态-动作最优性评分模型和（ii）每个演示者的潜在专业水平；随后将评分模型集成到在线策略优化中，使智能体最大化估计的最优性分数，并用在线 rollout 迭代精炼评分模型以缓解在线适应中的分布偏移；同时给出最优性分数可识别性及条件最优占用与策略恢复的理论保证。

Result: 在连续控制基准上的实验表明，该方法在高度异构和低质量演示场景下持续优于现有基线。

Conclusion: LEOS-IL为从异构不完美演示者学习提供了一种有理论支撑的实用框架，能有效应对混合质量数据并提升在线策略优化的鲁棒性与性能。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) typically relies on large-scale offline demonstrations collected from multiple human or algorithmic demonstrators. However, most existing approaches assume these demonstrators are homogeneous or near-optimal experts, a convenient but unrealistic assumption in real-world applications. In practice, demonstrations are often collected from heterogeneous demonstrators with unknown and varying levels of expertise, resulting in highly mixed-quality data. In this work, we study a challenging yet practical setting of imitation learning from such heterogeneous imperfect demonstrators. We propose Latent Expertise and Optimality Scoring based Imitation Learning (LEOS-IL), a principled framework that jointly learns (i) a state-action optimality scoring model and (ii) latent expertise levels for each demonstrator from unlabeled suboptimal offline demonstrations. The learned scoring model is then integrated into an online policy optimization procedure, where the agent is trained to maximize estimated optimality scores while its online rollouts are iteratively leveraged to refine the scoring model, thereby mitigating distributional shift during online adaptation. We further provide theoretical guarantees for optimality score identification and conditional optimal occupancy and policy recovery under explicit sufficient conditions. Experiments on continuous control benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing baselines across highly heterogeneous and low-quality demonstration regimes.

</details>


### [101] [MOON-RL: Multi-Objective Optimization via Objective Space Navigation with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15227)
*Andrii Artomov,Soheyl Massoudi,Jürg Schiffmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOON-RL将约束多目标优化建模为目标空间中的偏好导向导航，用轻量PPO策略根据目标与约束观测自适应更新设计，并由元算法周期性地重置策略、从非支配解档案重采样起点、调整步长与惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计中存在多目标冲突和严格可行性约束，传统基于种群的优化器评估次数多，且难以在线控制搜索聚焦区域。

Method: 提出基于强化学习的MOON-RL框架：PPO策略接收目标空间和约束相关观测，学习沿用户指定权衡方向更新设计；元算法周期性重置策略、从持续更新的非支配解档案重采样初始设计，并调节步长与惩罚调度。在真实执行器设计问题及收敛困难的约束基准上，与主流进化算法在相同评估预算下对比，并进行消融实验。

Result: 在聚焦搜索场景中，MOON-RL在感兴趣区域取得有竞争力的性能；消融实验表明RL驱动的自适应采样过程和元算法均有贡献；框架也可兼容自适应非RL采样器，而PPO在固定目标、偏好导向的目标空间导航中仍是强选择。

Conclusion: 将强化学习训练重新用作目标空间导航，是构建交互式、偏好引导设计工具的可行基础模块。

Abstract: Many engineering design problems involve multiple conflicting objectives and strict feasibility constraints. They are typically solved with population-based optimizers that require many evaluations and offer limited online control over where the search is focused. We present MOON-RL, a reinforcement-learning-based optimization framework that formulates constrained multi-objective search as preference-directed navigation in the objective space. In MOON-RL, a lightweight PPO policy receives objective-space and constraint-related observations and acts as an instance-specific adaptive search process. It learns how to update the design so that the current solution advances along a user-specified trade-off direction, while a simple meta-algorithm periodically resets the policy, resamples starting designs from a continuously updated archive of non-dominated solutions, and adjusts step-size and penalty schedules. We evaluate MOON-RL on real-world actuator-design problems and additional convergence-hard constrained benchmarks, comparing against established evolutionary algorithms under matched evaluation budgets. In focused search scenarios, MOON-RL achieves competitive performance in the region of interest, and ablation studies show that both the RL-driven adaptive sampling process and the meta-algorithm contribute to this behaviour, suggesting that repurposing RL training as objective-space navigation is a viable building block for interactive, preference-guided design tools. Additional experiments show that the framework also accommodates adaptive non-RL samplers, while PPO remains a strong choice for fixed-target, preference-directed objective-space navigation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Ropedia Xperience 2: A Gold Standard for Scaling Physical AI Environments and Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17060)
*Hao Li,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Hui En Pang,Kairui Hu,Mingze Sun,Runmao Yao,Xinhao Ji,Xiyuan Zheng,Xuanli Chen,Yalun Dai,Yap Kim Hui,Dingwen Zhang,Yuanyuan Gao,Ziang Cao,Cheng Huang Yean,Jackson Peng,Rui Duan,Yaobo Liu,Zetong Wang,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Ropedia Xperience 2数据集，包含1589个米制3D扫描和4230个第一视角任务录制（74项任务、131小时），并展示每条采集可转化为可交互仿真环境、机器人全身轨迹及额外行为，为建立物理AI在环境与行为上的缩放定律奠定数据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型虽能直接从视觉观测控制机器人，但在真实世界长时程、富接触任务上仍脆弱；物理AI的进展依赖示范行为与可交互环境两类互补资源，而当前缺乏对“应扩展何种数据、如何持续产生与复用数据”的认识，需先明确物理AI能力如何随环境与行为规模缩放。

Method: 构建Ropedia Xperience 2数据集：发布1589个米制尺度3D扫描与4230段第一视角录制（74项任务，131小时）；将场景转换为物理可交互仿真环境，将人体运动重定向为全身机器人轨迹，并通过虚拟遥操作与自动化长时程轨迹生成获得额外机器人行为。

Result: 证明每次采集均可被复用于机器人学习：场景变为可交互仿真环境，人体动作转为机器人全身轨迹，且能通过虚拟遥操作与自动轨迹生成扩充机器人行为数据。

Conclusion: 提出将环境与行为的联合采集作为物理AI数据收集的“金标准”，为建立环境与行为维度上的缩放定律提供数据基础。

Abstract: Large foundation models such as GPT-6 Astra and Claude Fable 5.1 have recently been shown to control robots directly from visual observations. They nevertheless remain brittle on long-horizon, contact-rich tasks in real-world environments. Further progress depends on two complementary resources: demonstrations of human behavior that supervise how tasks are performed, and interactive environments in which policies can be rolled out, evaluated, and improved in closed loop. Language models have advanced along well-characterized scaling laws; the corresponding question for physical AI is how capability scales with environments and behaviors, which first requires identifying which data to scale and how to produce and reuse such data continuously. To address this question, we introduce Ropedia Xperience 2, comprising 1,589 released metric-scale 3D scans and a collection of 4,230 egocentric recordings of 74 tasks (131 hours). We further show that each capture can be repurposed for robot learning: scenes are converted into physically interactive simulation environments, human motion is retargeted to whole-body robot trajectories, and additional robot behaviors are obtained through virtual teleoperation and automated long-horizon trajectory generation. We propose this joint capture of environments and behaviors as a gold standard for physical AI data collection, laying a data foundation for establishing scaling laws over environments and behaviors.

</details>


### [103] [Evaluating Video LLMs' Understanding of Corner Cases in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16767)
*Gabriel Manalu,Minh Son Hoang,Hyeontaek Hwang,Jaesung Lee,Hyojin Choi,Tran Tuan Phong,Seon Kwon Kim,Saad Wazir,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DashBench基准，测试25个视频大语言模型在1709个驾驶视频（含碰撞相关及非碰撞罕见长尾场景）上的表现，发现所有模型均远低于安全关键应用阈值，常误判或漏判危险事件并产生幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶场景基准主要针对常规交通条件和通用理解任务，无法评估视频大语言模型在罕见且安全关键的长尾驾驶事件（corner cases）上的表现。

Method: 构建DashBench基准，包含1709个精心筛选的驾驶视频（涵盖碰撞相关长尾案例及罕见非碰撞案例），采用大量提示词和开放式问答，对25个视频大语言模型进行评测。

Result: 所有被评估模型的表现均显著低于安全关键驾驶应用所需阈值，频繁误判或漏判前方危险事件，并在解释中幻觉出不存在的交通参与者或动作。

Conclusion: DashBench结合广泛数据集、严格评估工具和对当前模型局限性的详细诊断，有助于推动更安全、更可解释的自动驾驶系统发展。

Abstract: Natural language explanations promised by modern Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) offer autonomous driving systems a path toward detailed scene description and interpretable decision making on the road. While recent benchmarks have begun assessing these models on driving scenes, they mainly target routine traffic conditions and general comprehension tasks based on existing driving datasets and therefore fail to evaluate model performance on the rarest and most safety-critical driving events, also known as "corner cases". To provide a comprehensive evaluation of Video LLMs on precisely these scenarios, we introduce DashBench, a benchmark testing 25 Video LLMs on 1,709 carefully curated driving videos spanning not only collision-related corner cases but also rare non-collision ones. Through extensive prompts and open-ended question answering, we expose a critical capability gap: all evaluated models perform substantially below the threshold required for safety-critical driving applications, frequently misjudging or missing dangerous events on the road ahead and hallucinating non-existent agents or actions in their explanations. We believe DashBench, combining an extensive dataset with a rigorous evaluation toolkit and a detailed diagnosis of current model limitations, drives progress toward safer, more explainable autonomous driving systems. The code and data will be shared at: this http URL

</details>


### [104] [Fog-FWL: A Large-Scale Full-Waveform LiDAR Dataset for Recovering Fog-Hidden Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16742)
*Kazuma Ikeda,Kotaro Oishi,Ryosei Hara,Ryo Yoshida,Takahiro Kado,Ibuki Fujioka,Taro Beppu,Mariko Isogawa,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了Fog-FWL数据集基础，包含真实雾天全波形LiDAR基准Fog-FWL-Real（7527帧，是现有数据的579倍）和基于物理模拟器生成的大规模合成训练集Fog-FWL-Sim（12000帧），用于从全波形中恢复雾中隐藏物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR在雾天性能严重下降，雾散射产生强后向散射并衰减物体反射，导致弱物体回波在峰值检测中被丢弃；全波形LiDAR保留峰值检测前的信号，但缺乏合适数据阻碍了相关研究。

Method: 构建Fog-FWL-Real真实基准（8场景58序列，含直方图级Object/Fog/Noise标签、10719个3D边界框、三级难度）；开发Fog-FWL-Simulator物理模拟器（集成空间异质雾密度场、含多次散射的时间分辨光传输、光子计数SPAD接收器模型）生成Fog-FWL-Sim合成集；两子集共享传感器格式和标注协议，支持严格sim-to-real评估。

Result: 分析表明最困难条件下仅34.9%的物体光线其回波包含在最强三个峰值中，凸显波形级恢复的必要性；Fog-FWL-Real比唯一现有真实雾天FWL数据大579倍。

Conclusion: Fog-FWL-Real、Fog-FWL-Sim和Fog-FWL-Simulator将公开释放，以促进雾鲁棒LiDAR感知研究。

Abstract: LiDAR is a core sensor for autonomous driving, yet it is highly vulnerable to fog. Fog scattering produces strong backscatter near the sensor and attenuates reflections from objects behind it, so weak object returns are often discarded by the peak detection that converts received signals into point clouds. Full-waveform LiDAR (FWL), which records the received optical signal before peak detection, retains these weak returns, but the lack of suitable data has prevented research on recovering fog-hidden objects from FWL. We present Fog-FWL, a dataset foundation for this task that consists of a real-world benchmark, Fog-FWL-Real, and a large-scale synthetic training set, Fog-FWL-Sim. Fog-FWL-Real contains 7,527 real foggy FWL frames from 8 scenes and 58 sequences, which is 579x larger than the only existing real foggy FWL data, together with histogram-level Object/Fog/Noise labels, 10,719 3D bounding boxes, and three difficulty levels defined by the attenuation of object reflections. Fog-FWL-Sim contains 12,000 frames generated by our physics-based simulator, Fog-FWL-Simulator, which integrates spatially heterogeneous fog density fields, time-resolved light transport including multiple scattering, and a photon-counting SPAD receiver model. The two subsets share the same sensor format and annotation protocol, enabling a strict sim-to-real evaluation in which no real waveforms are used for training. Our analysis shows that, under the hardest condition, object returns are included in the three strongest peaks for only 34.9% of object rays, underscoring the need for waveform-level recovery. Fog-FWL-Real, Fog-FWL-Sim, and the Fog-FWL-Simulator will be publicly released to facilitate research on fog-robust LiDAR perception.

</details>


### [105] [From Foundation VLM to Versatile Driving VLM: A Triplet Incremental Training Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16727)
*Shengtao Wu,Zichao Dong,Weixuan Qiao,Xiaolu Liu,Xin Zhan,Junbo Chen,Jianke Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通用视觉语言模型（VLM）无需重型3D空间先验或架构改造即可适应自动驾驶任务，其关键缺失仅是精确的度量深度；本文提出三元组增量训练框架，以非侵入方式注入3D几何感知且零推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将通用VLM适配自动驾驶通常被认为需要重型3D空间先验或侵入式架构改造来弥补空间 grounding 的不足，但这一假设值得检验；作者发现现成通用VLM在多样驾驶任务上已具备显著适应性，真正瓶颈在于精确的度量深度。

Method: 提出三元组增量训练框架，基于自动构建的（图像、深度、问答对）三元组进行统一三流训练：未冻结的主VLM流适配驾驶任务；辅助深度流通过显式几何约束引导视觉骨干隐式想象3D场景几何；冻结的孪生VLM流采用logit级KL蒸馏防止语义漂移；部署时丢弃辅助深度分支以实现零推理开销。

Result: 在覆盖3D空间感知、驾驶导向语言推理、端到端规划和通用多模态理解四大能力的四个主要基准上评估，性能超越最先进的驾驶VLM，在3D感知上媲美领域专用专家模型，并成功保留原始通用推理能力。

Conclusion: 三元组增量训练为将通用基础模型转化为稳健驾驶智能提供了一种统一、可直接部署的范式，证明精确度量深度是通用VLM适配自动驾驶的主要缺失环节且可通过非侵入方式有效补齐。

Abstract: Adapting general Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to Autonomous Driving (AD) is thought to require heavy 3D spatial priors or intrusive architectural overhauls to compensate for a lack of spatial grounding. In this work, we present a counterintuitive pilot study: off-the-shelf general VLMs already exhibit remarkable adaptability across diverse driving tasks, while precise metric depth remains the primary missing link, a bottleneck readily addressable via onboard sensors and depth foundation models. To this end, we introduce the Triplet Incremental Training Framework, a non-intrusive paradigm that seamlessly instills 3D geometric awareness into a general VLM backbone while fully preserving its open-world capabilities. Driven by automatically constructed (image, depth, QA pair) triplets, our framework operates via a unified tri-stream training pipeline: an unfrozen primary VLM stream to adapt to driving tasks, an auxiliary depth stream that guides the visual backbone to implicitly imagine 3D scene geometry via explicit geometric constraints, and a frozen twin VLM stream employing logit-level KL distillation to prevent semantic drift. Crucially, the auxiliary depth branch is discarded during deployment, ensuring the enhanced geometric awareness comes with zero inference overhead. We evaluate our framework across four major benchmarks targeting four diverse capabilities spanning 3D spatial perception, driving-oriented language reasoning, end-to-end planning, and general multimodal understanding. Our approach outperforms state-of-the-art driving VLMs, rivals domain-specific expert models in 3D perception, and successfully retains its original general reasoning capabilities. This demonstrates that Triplet Incremental Training offers a unified, deployment-ready paradigm for transforming general foundation models into robust driving intelligences.

</details>


### [106] [ReFineR: Single image to dense point cloud registration at pixel level with point splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16583)
*Ugo Insalaco,Jean-Emmanuel Deschaud,Pierre Dellenbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReFineR，一个离线框架，利用可微点泼溅光栅化与平滑梯度方向测度（GOM），通过分析-合成优化迭代细化单张图像与预重建点云之间的相机位姿，实现精细的图像-点云配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机与LiDAR观测互补，但标定后仍存在数像素的残余重投影误差（源于时间不同步、机械振动或点云重建不准确），在中等距离上导致分米级几何偏差，影响密集跨模态监督、纹理映射和语义标签迁移等下游任务。

Method: 构建基于可微点泼溅光栅化与平滑梯度方向测度（GOM）的离线框架，采用分析-合成优化迭代细化相机位姿；并在优化前估计自动点云缩放因子，以单一尺度不变公式实现跨场景的鲁棒细化。

Result: 在来自异构数据源、经人工标定的图像数据集上评估该方法；实验表明可在优化前估计点云缩放因子，并在广泛场景下实现鲁棒的位姿细化。

Conclusion: ReFineR能有效精细配准单张图像与预重建点云，校正残余重投影误差，且尺度不变公式使其适用于多种场景。

Abstract: Cameras and LiDARs (Light Detection and Ranging) provide complementary observations of the same scene: cameras capture rich appearance cues while LiDAR sensors provide accurate geometric measurements. In this work, we address the problem of fine image-to-point cloud registration from a single image and a roughly estimated camera pose. On robotic platforms, residual reprojection errors of several pixels are commonly observed even after calibration due to temporal desynchronization, mechanical vibrations, or inaccuracies in point cloud reconstruction. Such errors can lead to decimeter-level geometric discrepancies at moderate distances and significantly affect multiple downstream tasks, such as dense cross-modal supervision, texture mapping, and semantic label transfer. To address this challenge, we introduce ReFineR: an offline framework to finely register individual images to pre-reconstructed point clouds. ReFineR builds upon differentiable point splatting rasterization and a smooth Gradient Orientation Measure (GOM) to iteratively refine the camera pose through analysis-by-synthesis optimization. We evaluated the method on a curated dataset of manually calibrated images collected from heterogeneous data sources. Furthermore, we show that an automatic point cloud rescale factor can be estimated prior to optimization, enabling robust refinement across a wide range of scenarios using a single scale-invariant formulation. The project page can be found at this https URL

</details>


### [107] [Do the Details Help the Whole? One-Way Transfer from Person Actions to Group Intent in VLMs on JRDB-Social](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16581)
*Chengguang Gan,Zhixi Cai,Yunhao Liang,Hanjun Wei,QingHao Zhang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在JRDB-Social数据集上测试视觉语言模型联合学习个体动作与群体意图是否互相促进，发现联合训练可提升意图识别但不改善动作识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会场景数据集同时标注个体动作和群体意图，通常假设同时训练两个层级能互相帮助，但这一假设缺乏验证。

Method: 以Qwen3.5 4B为主模型，在JRDB-Social上设计八种训练方案（每组三个随机种子），在Qwen3.5 9B和Qwen3-VL 4B上重复核心方案，并用预先固定的判定规则评估；辅以强制前缀、遮挡、成员掩码、线性探针和组件嫁接等分析。

Result: 先写成员动作再写意图使意图宏F1从30.1升至31.0，先写意图达到31.2，而动作F1与仅训练动作的模型相差不超过0.2；所选方案中没有任何联合意图训练的模型提升动作表现；意图增益依赖正确且基于图像的动作——空动作列表或借用同意图其他组的动作会降低意图F1；意图跟随所写动作、更依赖人物信息且部分存在于视觉塔中；将动作作为输入而非目标会损害两个层级。

Conclusion: 联合学习个体动作与群体意图并非对称互利：意图识别可从动作细节中受益，但动作识别不会因意图监督而提升，且增益依赖准确、图像扎根的动作信息。

Abstract: Social scene datasets label people at two levels: what each person is doing and what the group is there for. Training on both levels at once is often assumed to help each of them. We test this assumption for vision-language models (VLMs) on JRDB-Social, treating person actions as the details and group intent as the whole. We fine-tune Qwen3.5 4B under eight training arms with three seeds, repeat the core arms on Qwen3.5 9B and Qwen3-VL 4B, and judge every arm with a verdict rule fixed before testing. Writing each member's actions before the intent raises intent macro-F1 from 30.1 to 31.0, and writing the intent first reaches 31.2, while action F1 stays within 0.2 points of an action-only model. Under the selected recipe, no arm trained with intent improves actions. The gain requires correct, image-grounded actions: empty action lists or actions borrowed from another group with the same intent lower intent F1. Forced-prefix tests, occlusion, member masking, linear probes and component grafting show that the intent follows the written actions, relies more on people and partly resides in the vision tower. Supplying actions as input instead of as targets hurts both levels.

</details>


### [108] [ActWorld-JEPA: Action-Conditioned JEPA World Models Meet Latent Planning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16579)
*Ziying Song,Shengkai Zhang,Caiyan Jia,Lei Yang,Lin Liu,Yuchen Liu,Ziyang Liu,Zirui Li,Gang Wang,Peiliang Wu,Li Wang,Yadan Luo,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActWorld-JEPA将JEPA潜在预测与候选轨迹重排序结合，用于自动驾驶规划选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA能在潜在空间预测未来而无需像素重建，但仅靠潜在预测一致性无法判断候选规划的相对优劣，需要把预测表示与候选选择衔接起来。

Method: 提出ActWorld-JEPA联合嵌入框架：PC-JEPA用全局和逐步轨迹意图引导循环潜在预测，并通过门控交叉注意力融合预测未来与当前场景；R3（Relational Residual Reranking）在规划监督下建模规划与未来表示的一致与差异，对基础规划分数施加有界门控残差修正；推理时仅用观测上下文与候选轨迹。

Result: NAVSIM v1 navtest达94.1 PDMS（最终选择器用NAVTEST聚合反馈校准）；NAVSIM v2 navtest和navhard分别达91.6和42.7 EPDMS；nuScenes上1–6 s平均L2误差1.20 m，预测碰撞率0.76%。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了从JEPA预测表示到由预期后果驱动驾驶决策的有效路径。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) enable future prediction in latent space without pixel reconstruction. However, latent predictive consistency alone does not establish the relative driving quality of candidate plans. We present ActWorld-JEPA, a joint-embedding formulation for candidate-based driving. Where Plan-Conditioned JEPA (PC-JEPA) uses global and step-wise trajectory intentions to guide recurrent latent prediction, then combines the predicted future with the current scene through gated cross-attention; Relational Residual Reranking ($R^3$) models agreement and discrepancy between plan and future representations under planning supervision, yielding bounded, gated residual corrections to the base planning scores. This formulation connects latent prediction to candidate selection using only observed context and proposed trajectories at inference. Benchmark-specific implementations report 94.1 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 navtest, with the final selector calibrated using aggregate NAVTEST feedback, and 91.6 and 42.7 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 navtest and navhard, respectively. The nuScenes evaluation reports a mean $L_2$ error of 1.20 m and a predicted collision rate of 0.76% over 1--6 s. This work provides an effective path from JEPA's predictive representations to driving decisions informed by anticipated consequences.

</details>


### [109] [PhysCapBench: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Physically Grounded Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16424)
*Haofeng Wang,Pengcheng Wen,Ruiqi Li,Yuhao Zhang,Tiange Zhang,Boren Zheng,Xuanyi Liu,Siyu Tao,Danni Yu,Gan Lin,Haocheng Tang,Tianzizhe Guo,Mingyuan Su,Boyuan Chen,Zhongyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysCapBench，首个跨第三人称、自我中心操作和手持夹爪演示三个域的统一物理字幕基准，评估字幕质量并证明物理字幕对重建与策略微调的下游价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频-语言预训练中语义正确的字幕可能遗漏定义过程的关键交互与状态转移，视频中丰富的物理经验无法自动转化为充分的语言监督，缺少衡量标注质量、比较字幕策略和指导数据精炼的标尺。

Method: 构建PhysCapBench基准，包含1,500个视频序列的人工验证标注，覆盖三个域，通过七个物理维度评估准确性与覆盖率；并通过视频重建和操作策略微调检验下游效用。

Result: 物理字幕相较元数据字幕使第三人称视频重建LPIPS降低8.6-9.5%，自我中心视频降低26.9-30.9%；训练指令丰富化同时提升PhysCapBench分数与策略成功率，pi0.5在RoboTwin 2.0子任务上提升7.5个百分点。

Conclusion: PhysCapBench使语言监督中的物理内容可检查、可比较，为跨域评估与改进物理字幕提供统一基础。

Abstract: Physical AI aims to learn transferable physical knowledge from real-world experience to understand, predict, and act in the physical world. Internet videos and embodied demonstrations provide complementary observations of motion, interaction, and state evolution. A foundational data challenge is to measure and preserve this physical information as training supervision. In video-language pretraining, captions express observed processes in language, yet a semantically correct description can omit the interactions and transitions that define those processes. Rich physical experience in video does not automatically translate into sufficient language supervision. This gap calls for a yardstick to assess annotation quality, compare captioning strategies, and guide training-data refinement. Motivated by our data construction for world-model pretraining, we introduce PhysCapBench, to our knowledge the first unified physical-caption benchmark spanning all three domains: third-person scenes, egocentric manipulation, and handheld-gripper demonstrations. It provides human-verified annotations for 1,500 video sequences and evaluates accuracy and coverage through seven physical dimensions. We examine downstream utility through video reconstruction and manipulation-policy fine-tuning. Physical captions reduce reconstruction LPIPS by 8.6-9.5% on third-person videos and 26.9-30.9% on egocentric videos relative to metadata captions. Training-instruction enrichment improves both PhysCapBench scores and policy success, including a 7.5-percentage-point gain for pi0.5 on a RoboTwin 2.0 subtask. PhysCapBench makes the physical content of language supervision inspectable and comparable, providing a cross-domain basis for its evaluation and improvement.

</details>


### [110] [VBot Exo2Ego Policy: Lightweight Data Collection and Exocentric-to-Egocentric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16417)
*Tong Rao,Zhihao Zhang,Yuntian Wang,Siyu Zhou,Hailong Qin,Yinan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VBot Exo2Ego Policy将第三人称视频转换为选定执行者的第一人称观测，以扩展第一人称操作数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称操作数据稀缺，而现实世界第三人称视频丰富，需要一种将第三人称视频转化为第一人称观测的方法来扩充数据。

Method: 提出轻量级数据采集流程和移动应用，支持第一人称、第三人称及双视角同步采集；基于预训练视频扩散Transformer，引入几何引导及语义掩码和手部关键点辅助监督；训练时随机注入相机几何并逐步移除，推理时无需目标相机标定；结合源视频感知推理、可选执行者参考和多镜头适应以识别目标执行者并跟踪操作。

Result: 模型能够将第三人称视频转换为选定执行者的第一人称观测，并在无需目标相机标定的情况下生成高质量的手物交互视频。

Conclusion: 该采集系统和生成模型为跨任务、场景和参与者扩展第一人称操作数据提供了基础。

Abstract: We present VBot Exo2Ego Policy, a video generation model that turns third-person videos into first-person observations of a selected actor, offering a practical way to expand first-person operation data from abundant real-world footage.
