To support this goal, we introduce a lightweight data collection pipeline and mobile app that support first-person recording, third-person recording, and synchronized capture of both views. This setup combines accessible single-view collection with paired RGB and camera geometry for learning cross-view correspondence. Building on a pretrained video diffusion transformer, our model incorporates geometric guidance and auxiliary supervision from semantic masks and hand keypoints to facilitate optimization and improve the fidelity of hand-object interactions. Camera geometry is injected stochastically during training and gradually removed, allowing inference without target-camera calibration. To accommodate the diversity of Internet videos, we combine source-video perception and reasoning, optional actor references, and multi-shot adaptation to identify the intended actor and follow an operation across camera cuts. The collection system and generation model provide a foundation for expanding first-person operation data across tasks, scenes, and participants.

</details>


### [111] [GOOSELoc: A Multimodal Benchmark for Ground-to-Aerial Localization in Unstructured Off-Road Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16162)
*Fabian Deuser,Peter Mortimer,Robin-Nico Kampa,Anna Bößendörfer,Mirko Mählisch,Martin Werner,Norbert Oswald*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GOOSELoc多模态基准，用于非结构化环境（森林、田野、乡村道路）的地对空定位，包含9,356个场景、四季环绕RGB与3D LiDAR点云，并给出融合图像与LiDAR几何的多模态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角定位基准（如VIGOR、cross-view KITTI）以城市和道路场景为主，缺乏越野地形，无法评估GNSS不可靠的非结构化环境下的定位能力。

Method: 构建GOOSELoc基准，覆盖慕尼黑周边森林、田野与乡村道路，配对地理参考航空正射影像、环绕RGB图像和3D LiDAR点云，提供铺装/非铺装标签与季节数据；在该基准上微调四种先进跨视角定位基线，并提出融合环绕图像与LiDAR几何的多模态基线。

Result: 四种先进基线即使经过任务特定微调，在越野环境中性能仍显著下降；所提多模态基线提升了定位性能，为后续工作提供更强参考。

Conclusion: GOOSELoc填补了非结构化环境跨视角定位基准的空白，表明现有方法在越野场景迁移性不足，多模态融合是提升该场景定位性能的有效方向。

Abstract: Cross-view pose estimation estimates a ground camera's geographic pose by matching onboard sensor data with geo-referenced overhead imagery. This is particularly relevant when GNSS is unreliable, for example under forest canopy or in urban canyons. Existing metric cross-view benchmarks, such as VIGOR and cross-view KITTI, are dominated by urban and road scenes with strong human-made structure without off-road terrain. We introduce GOOSELoc, a multimodal benchmark for ground-to-aerial localization in unstructured environments, including forests, fields, and rural tracks. GOOSELoc comprises 9,356 scenes recorded across all four seasons around Munich, Germany, pairing geo-referenced aerial orthophotos with surround-view RGB imagery and 3D LiDAR point clouds. Paved/unpaved labels enable analyzing the impact of terrain structure, while the seasonal recordings allow studying the appearance gap to mostly summer-captured orthophotos. To assess the transferability of existing methods, we adapt and fine-tune four state-of-the-art cross-view localization baselines on GOOSELoc. Despite task-specific fine-tuning, the performance degrades substantially in off-road environments. We therefore propose a multimodal baseline combining surround-view imagery and LiDAR geometry, improving localization performance and providing a stronger reference for future work. Data and baselines are released at this https URL .

</details>


### [112] [Sub-actions in Action: Text-Guided Hand-Role Alignment for Sub-Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16056)
*Sadegh Rahmaniboldaji,Filip Rybansky,Quoc Vuong,Frank Guerin,Andrew Gilbert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BiScopeNet框架增强视频大模型区分第一人称动作中左右手角色及操作对象的能力，并构建BiScopeCap-100K数据集用于监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升视觉语言模型在第一人称视频中区分左右手角色及其操作对象的能力，以支持细粒度子动作识别。

Method: BiScopeNet包含FocusAlign（门控文本引导的注意力模块，对齐视频特征与文本上下文以突出动作相关线索）和MetaLoss（掩码、对象加权的损失，优先关注视觉 grounded 的术语）；不修改原模型的视觉编码器和语言模型；同时构建BiScopeCap-100K大规模视频级数据集，提供密集描述（高层动作、左右手角色、操作对象）及空间元数据。

Result: 在评估的Video-LLM骨干上，BiScopeNet在动作、手部角色和对象识别上均优于对应的监督基线，在BiScopeCap上取得最强综合性能。

Conclusion: BiScopeNet能有效增强现有视频大模型对第一人称子动作中左右手角色和操作对象的识别能力，且无需修改模型结构。

Abstract: Our objective is to enhance VLMs ability to distinguish the roles of the left and right hands in egocentric actions. We introduce sub-action detection, a task that requires models to explicitly recognise the left- and right-hand roles and their manipulated objects within an ongoing high-level action. To address this challenge, we propose BiScopeNet, a framework that augments existing video-LLMs without modifying their vision encoders or language models. BiScopeNet incorporates two components: FocusAlign, a gated text-guided attention-based module that selectively aligns video features with textual context to emphasise action-relevant cues, and MetaLoss, a masked, object-weighted loss that prioritises visually grounded terms. To supervise sub-action detection, we introduce BiScopeCap-100K, a large-scale video-level dataset curated from existing egocentric datasets, providing dense captions that jointly describe high-level actions, left/right-hand roles, and manipulated objects, enriched with spatial metadata for grounding. Across the evaluated Video-LLM backbones, BiScopeNet improves action, hand-role, and object recognition over the corresponding supervised baselines, achieving the strongest aggregate performance among the evaluated configurations on BiScopeCap.

</details>


### [113] [Topology-Aware 4D Gaussian Splatting for Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16012)
*Leonardo Vanni,Stefano Gasperini,Federico Tombari,Chiara Plizzari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TopoEgo基准和Topo-4DGS方法，解决第一人称视频中拓扑变化（切割、合并、开合）的4D动态重建问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D高斯泼溅方法依赖固定表示形变，无法处理日常交互中的拓扑变化（分裂、合并、表面出现/消失），导致严重视觉伪影。

Method: Topo-4DGS通过为交互部件分配独立刚性变换建模部件级运动，并动态激活/停用高斯以表示表面出现、消失或分离，联合建模时变表面支撑。

Result: 在拓扑变化场景（尤其事件后帧）上，Topo-4DGS显著优于现有最先进4DGS方法。

Conclusion: TopoEgo基准与Topo-4DGS方法有效应对第一人称视频中的拓扑变化重建挑战，代码和数据将公开。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic scenes and objects from monocular egocentric video is crucial for embodied AI, yet existing 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) approaches struggle with everyday interactions involving topological changes, such as cutting, assembly, opening, or closing. Since 4DGS methods rely on deforming a fixed representation, events that split, merge, or reveal surfaces cause severe visual artifacts. To address this challenge, we introduce TopoEgo, the first benchmark dedicated to topological changes in egocentric video (covering fission, fusion, open, and close), alongside Topo-4DGS, a topology-aware reconstruction method. Topo4DGS jointly models part-level motion and time-varying surface support by assigning independent rigid transformations to interacting parts and dynamically activating or deactivating Gaussians as surfaces appear, disappear, or separate. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Topo-4DGS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art 4DGS approaches across topological changes, especially on post-event frames. Code and data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [114] [CGOcc: Learning Cross-Gaussian Dependencies for 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15692)
*Zirui Huang,Jiongle He,Yunfan Zeng,Yuanzhi Lu,Guangye Li,Boyan Tang,Weite SU,Pedro V. Sander,Amy Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CGOcc是一种显式建模跨高斯依赖关系的多相机监督框架，用于3D语义占据预测，在nuScenes上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯的语义占据预测中，空间加权混合方法忽略了跨基元依赖关系的显式建模，导致重叠基元的联合解读能力受限，难以利用类别特定的关系证据进行体素分类。

Method: CGOcc融合外观与几何观测来精化高斯状态，以对角加低秩表示编码跨高斯依赖；通过类条件投影提取对角及带符号的跨高斯统计量（无需构建稠密依赖矩阵），并利用这些统计量精化占据类别概率，同时保持学习参考的占据质量与自由概率。

Result: 在nuScenes上的大量实验表明CGOcc达到最先进性能，在高斯重叠度较高时增益更大，边界附近预测有所改善；消融研究验证了协方差感知预测的有效性及其与几何交互的协同作用。

Conclusion: 显式建模跨高斯依赖并采用协方差感知的类条件投影可有效提升基于高斯的3D语义占据预测性能，尤其在高重叠与边界区域表现更优。

Abstract: In Gaussian-based semantic occupancy prediction, overlapping primitives can provide ambiguous class predictions, making their joint interpretation important for voxel classification. However, current methods using spatially weighted mixtures neglect explicit modeling of cross-primitive dependencies, limiting the readout's ability to utilize category-specific relational evidence. Hence, we introduce CGOcc, a supervised multi-camera framework that explicitly models Cross-Gaussian dependencies for 3D semantic Occupancy prediction. CGOcc integrates appearance and geometric observations to refine Gaussian states and encodes cross-Gaussian dependencies in a diagonal-plus-low-rank representation. To incorporate these dependencies into voxel classification, CGOcc uses a class-conditional projection to extract diagonal and signed cross-Gaussian statistics without constructing a dense dependency matrix. The readout then refines occupied-class probabilities using these statistics while preserving a learned reference's occupied mass and free probability. Extensive experiments on nuScenes demonstrate CGOcc achieves state-of-the-art performance, with larger gains at high Gaussian overlap and improved predictions near boundaries. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of covariance-aware prediction and support its synergy with geometric interaction.

</details>


### [115] [Fear of Memorizing Objects: End-to-End Zero-Shot Model-Based Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15459)
*Leon Greiner,Wout Boerdijk,Junwen Huang,Benjamin Busam,Rudolph Triebel,Maximilian Durner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FOMO，一种端到端可训练的基于transformer的零样本模型物体检测架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提案匹配的流水线易受杂乱场景中碎片化提案和遮挡下匹配退化影响，且训练数据集物体多样性有限、缺乏真实密集杂乱。

Method: FOMO将物体模板信息早期注入检测流程，通过可学习潜在token、多视图CAD模板与查询图像特征间的双向注意力生成物体感知图像特征和条件查询，再解码为边界框和置信度预测；并构建Clutter6D大规模合成数据集训练。

Result: 在BOP-Classic-Core上达到54.2 mAP，每张图0.23秒，整体排名第二，与第一名相差3.8个百分点但速度快7倍以上。

Conclusion: FOMO通过早期模板注入和端到端训练有效提升零样本多实例物体检测性能与效率，消融实验验证了架构设计选择。

Abstract: This paper presents FOMO (Fear of Memorizing Objects), an end-to-end trainable transformer-based architecture for zero-shot model-based object detection. Existing approaches typically follow a proposal-and-match pipeline: target-agnostic mask proposals are generated and then matched to template features in a set of loosely coupled stages, making them susceptible to fragmented proposals in clutter and degraded matching under occlusions. FOMO elegantly replaces this paradigm by injecting object template information early in the detection pipeline: Bidirectional attention between learnable latent tokens, multi-view CAD templates, and query image features produces object-aware image features and conditioned queries which are subsequently decoded into bounding-box and confidence predictions. We train FOMO on Clutter6D, a novel large-scale synthetic dataset with over 500k images featuring more than 10k diverse object models rendered in highly cluttered scenes with multiple instances and varying occlusion levels, which addresses the limited object diversity and lack of realistic dense clutter in existing datasets for training zero-shot multi-instance algorithms. In true zero-shot fashion, FOMO achieves 54.2 mAP on BOP-Classic-Core at 0.23 s per image, ranking second overall with 3.8 pp difference to the top method while being more than 7x faster. Throughout comprehensive ablation studies we investigate and verify architectural design choices.

</details>


### [116] [IncR-3DSGG: Incremental Posed RGB Image Based 3D Scene Graph Generation via Privileged Depth Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15429)
*Mrunmai Phatak,Daniel Kienzle,Roman Küble,Jörg Hähner,Rainer Lienhart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IncR-3DSGG，仅用带位姿RGB图像增量构建3D场景图，推理时无需深度传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图生成方法推理时依赖深度传感，用于跨视角实例关联或几何关系计算，限制了部署平台。

Method: 利用参数化关系预测器（PRP）从2D视觉特征和2D几何线索学习估计物体间成对距离与相对角度，仅训练时用3D几何监督；再通过位姿图优化（PGO）跨视角融合，增量恢复度量一致的3D物体位置并构建全局一致3D场景图。

Result: 在3RScan数据集按3DSSG协议评测，平均物体召回52.2、谓词召回30.8，优于MonoSSG的27.0和17.6，且推理不使用深度或重建几何。

Conclusion: 表明仅凭带位姿RGB图像即可预测物体关系与度量 grounded 的3D物体表示。

Abstract: Scene graphs provide a compact, structured representation of environments by capturing objects and their spatial or semantic relationships in a form suitable for embodied agents. Existing 3D scene graph generation methods often rely on depth sensing at inference, either to associate object instances across views via point cloud overlap or to compute inter-object relations from geometric cues, limiting deployment to platforms with depth sensors. We propose IncR-3DSGG, which builds 3D scene graphs incrementally from posed\footnote{RGB images with camera extrinsics estimated by an off-the-shelf visual SLAM system.} RGB images, eliminating the use of depth sensors at inference. Our method leverages a Parametric Relation Predictor (PRP) that learns to estimate pairwise distances and relative angles between objects from 2D visual features and 2D geometric cues, using 3D geometric supervision available only during training. These learned pairwise measurements are fused across views through Pose Graph Optimization (PGO)~\cite{grisetti2010tutorial} to recover metrically grounded 3D object positions and build a globally consistent 3D scene graph incrementally. On the real-world 3RScan~\cite{wald2019rio} dataset, our method attains $52.2$ mean object recall and $30.8$ mean predicate recall under the 3DSSG~\cite{wald2020learning} evaluation protocol, against $27.0$ and $17.6$ for MonoSSG~\cite{wu2023incremental}, while using no depth or reconstructed geometry at inference. This shows that relations and metrically grounded 3D object representations can be predicted from posed RGB images alone.

</details>


### [117] [CARE-Drive: Coupled Alternating Refinement for Ego-trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15360)
*Xiao Ma,Yijun Chen,Zhiquan Hu,Jicheng Zhao,Yuming Li,Chuang Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CARE-Drive，将轨迹精化分解为路点残差修正与位置条件化的航向生成，在NAVSIM v1上取得88.8 PDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA自动驾驶模型的轨迹精化方法通常对整条轨迹做统一精化，忽视了不同物理量（位置与航向）及其相互作用的差异，缺乏针对性的物理专门化建模。

Method: 将轨迹精化分解为两部分：位置专家预测有界的度量路点残差，航向专家在修正路径条件下由相邻角度增量重建车辆朝向；骨干网络通过模仿学习和未来token辅助任务提供预测表示与基础轨迹，并在冻结骨干的情况下，对两个专家采用监督初始化与强化微调交替优化。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上达到88.8 PDMS，相比同一骨干生成未精化的基础轨迹提升2.4个点；消融实验进一步验证了专门化专家与分阶段训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 物理专门化与条件耦合相结合的精化设计能更简单直观地提升轨迹质量，验证了位置残差修正与位置条件化航向生成交替优化的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models bring multimodal understanding and world knowledge to end-to-end autonomous driving. To further improve the quality of predicted trajectories, existing studies introduce candidate refinement, residual denoising, and token correction. However, these methods typically focus on unified refinement of the entire trajectory, paying limited attention to the distinct physical quantities and their interactions. We further investigate physical specialization and coordination in trajectory refinement, centering on position and heading for simpler and more intuitive correction modeling. To this end, we propose CARE-Drive (Coupled Alternating Refinement for Ego-trajectories), which decomposes trajectory refinement into waypoint residual correction and position-conditioned heading generation. Through imitation learning and a future-token auxiliary task, the backbone provides predictive representations and a base trajectory. The position expert predicts bounded metric waypoint residuals, while the heading expert reconstructs vehicle orientation from adjacent angular increments conditioned on the corrected path. With the backbone frozen, the two experts are optimized alternately through supervised initialization and reinforcement fine-tuning. This design simplifies refinement through clear physical specialization while preserving conditional coupling during prediction and optimization. On NAVSIM v1, CARE-Drive achieves 88.8 PDMS, improving by 2.4 points over the unrefined base trajectories generated by the same backbone. Ablation studies further support the effectiveness of specialized experts and staged training.

</details>


### [118] [Driving World Models Move the Camera, Not the Scene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15260)
*Yuqiao Feng,Xiaoyu Ao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有驾驶世界模型虽能按计划移动相机，但场景几乎不随计划变化；ReTime-Compose通过几何方式注入计划，无需训练即可让冻结生成器更真实地响应计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计划条件驾驶世界模型用于比较候选计划，其预测应像真实场景一样随计划变化，但常用控制指标只检查相机是否跟随指令，无法反映场景响应。

Method: 利用记录驾驶数据中未执行计划的真实结果（重定时获得位姿匹配图像与LiDAR，立体设备记录横向偏移视图）作为参考；通过生成器内部干预定位失败源于计划通道；提出ReTime-Compose，将某记录速度rollout的帧传输到计划相机，冻结模型仅补全无帧覆盖区域，以几何方式交付计划且无需训练。

Result: 发布的世界模型场景响应至多约为真实视频的三分之一，轨迹分数无法区分执行与忽略速度指令；ReTime-Compose使冻结生成器在KITTI、nuScenes、Argoverse 2上对0.54 m横向偏移的响应接近真实场景，并在三个生成器中比重采样更好地响应减速计划、在两个生成器中响应加速计划。

Conclusion: 计划应作为几何而非令牌注入，ReTime-Compose无需训练即可显著提升世界模型对计划场景响应的真实性。

Abstract: Plan-conditioned driving world models are used to compare candidate plans, so their forecast should change with the plan as the real scene would, yet widely used control metrics check only whether the camera follows the command. Recorded drives already contain real outcomes of plans that were never driven: retiming a log gives pose-matched images and LiDAR for other speeds, and a stereo rig records a laterally shifted view. Against these references, released world models move their camera but barely move the scene: their scene response, the share of the real change in distance that the forecast reproduces, reaches at most about a third of real video's, and the trajectory score cannot tell an executed speed command from an ignored one. Interventions inside the generator trace the failure to the plan's channel. The forecast geometry follows the context frames; once they carry the planned geometry, plan tokens add little. ReTime-Compose therefore delivers the plan as geometry, without training. It transports the frames of one logged-speed rollout to the planned cameras, and the frozen model completes only what no frame covers. Frozen generators then follow a 0.54 m lateral shift nearly as the real scene does on KITTI, nuScenes and Argoverse 2, and respond more than re-sampling to slow-down plans in three generators and to speed-up plans in two.

</details>


### [119] [Test-Time Selective Adaptation for Cross-Modal UAV Object Tracking with Token Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15059)
*Yun Xiao,Hailong Ye,Zhicheng Zhao,Lei Liu,Amir Hussain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TSAT方法，通过可控token移位辅助任务和时序反向预测辅助任务生成无标注的时空自适应信号，并用可靠性感知更新门控筛选有效信号，实现跨模态无人机目标跟踪的在线测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态无人机目标跟踪存在模态切换、无人机运动和传感器偏移导致的模态外观差异与目标位置突变问题；现有方法依赖离线表示学习，测试阶段缺乏面向定位的自适应能力。

Method: 设计可控token移位辅助任务，在预定义token平移与定位偏移之间建立显式空间对应，生成稳定的无标注空间自适应信号；引入时序反向预测辅助任务利用帧间时序依赖，缓解连续跟踪中的累积定位误差；开发可靠性感知更新门控策略筛选有效自适应信号，稳定在线参数更新。

Result: 在CM-UOT数据集上，TSAT相比现有测试时自适应方法取得最优性能，在频繁模态切换和严重空间位移下表现出更强鲁棒性；跨跟踪框架与数据集的实验进一步验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: TSAT通过无监督测试时选择性自适应与token移位机制，有效应对跨模态无人机跟踪中的外观差异和位置突变，提升在线定位鲁棒性与泛化性。

Abstract: Cross-modal unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) object tracking faces modality-induced appearance discrepancies and abrupt target-position shifts that can arise from modality switching, UAV motion, and inter-sensor offsets. Although existing methods model modality and spatial variations, they primarily rely on offline representation learning and offer limited localization-oriented adaptation during testing. To address these challenges, we propose Test-Time Selective Adaptation with Token Shift (TSAT), which derives spatial and temporal adaptation signals from unlabeled test sequences for online optimization. Specifically, we design a controllable token shift auxiliary task, which imposes explicit spatial correspondence between predefined token translations and resultant localization offsets, generating stable annotation-free spatial adaptation signals. Furthermore, we introduce a temporal backward prediction auxiliary task to exploit frame-wise temporal dependencies, effectively mitigating cumulative localization errors in continuous tracking. To suppress interference from unreliable tracking samples, we develop a reliability-aware update gating strategy to screen valid adaptation signals and stabilize online parameter updates. Experiments on CM-UOT demonstrate that TSAT achieves state-of-the-art performance against existing test-time adaptation methods, exhibiting superior robustness under frequent modality switching and severe spatial displacement. Experiments across tracking frameworks and datasets further validate the effectiveness and generalization capability of TSAT.

</details>


### [120] [RSLiveBench-UAV: A Continually Evolving Vision-Language Benchmark for Aerial Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14953)
*Yue Zhou,Jiawei Liu,Jue Chen,Ziyang Gong,Haojia Wei,Jingxiang Li,Xue Jiang,Zhihang Zhong,Pai Peng,Xue Yang,Yansheng Li,Jonathan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RSLiveBench-UAV是一个持续更新的无人机视觉语言基准，用于评估航空空间智能，包含35,491条问题记录、17项任务，覆盖2D/3D/跨视角感知与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空空间智能（ASI）需要模型理解不同视角与尺度下的物体及空间关系；评估此类能力需要新的物理观测，而持续生成新的航空空间问题必须反复建立实体身份、几何参考和跨视角对应关系，现有基准难以满足。

Method: 提出RSLiveBench-UAV：通过持续采集真实无人机影像更新基准，仅接纳上一版本之后拍摄的图像并延迟公布答案；采用以实体为中心的生产流程，用几何辅助关联链接同一物理车辆的重复观测，复用人工验证属性，并结合视角特定标注与空间参考生成可溯源问题；所有任务使用确定性评分，不依赖模型评判。

Result: 在10个场景、17项任务、35,491条问题记录上评估了30个通用、遥感与具身模型；所有18个受测具身模型在总体得分上均超过3个遥感对照模型，但最强具身模型得分为43.29，低于GPT-6-Astra的59.88；细粒度车辆识别仍具挑战性，性能随高度和相机俯仰角变化；回顾性场景组比较和滚动窗口回放刻画了场景更新下得分模式的一致性。

Conclusion: RSLiveBench-UAV将新鲜物理观测、以实体为中心的答案生产和系统化模型评估相连接，支持对航空空间智能进行持续评估。

Abstract: Aerial spatial intelligence (ASI) requires models to understand objects and spatial relationships across changing viewpoints and scales. Evaluating these abilities as models evolve calls for fresh physical observations, yet producing new aerial spatial questions requires repeatedly establishing entity identities, geometric references, and cross-view correspondences. We introduce \textbf{RSLiveBench-UAV}, a continually evolving vision-language benchmark driven by recurring acquisition of real UAV imagery. Its update protocol admits images captured after the preceding release and delays answer disclosure. An entity-centered production workflow uses geometry-assisted association to link repeated observations of physical vehicles, reuse human-verified attributes, and combine them with view-specific annotations and spatial references for traceable question generation. All tasks use deterministic scoring without model judges. We evaluate 30 general-purpose, remote-sensing, and embodied models on 35,491 question records from ten scenes, covering 17 tasks across 2D perception, 3D perception, cross-view perception, and reasoning. All 18 tested embodied models outperform the three selected remote-sensing comparators in Overall, although the strongest embodied model scores 43.29 versus 59.88 for GPT-6-Astra. Fine-grained vehicle recognition remains challenging, and performance varies across heights and camera pitches. Retrospective scene-group comparisons and rolling-window replay characterize score-pattern consistency under scene renewal. RSLiveBench-UAV connects fresh physical observations, entity-centered answer production, and systematic model evaluation to support continual assessment of ASI. Benchmark will release at this https URL

</details>


### [121] [BirdDreams: Joint Perspective and BEV Video Generation for a Plausible, Reactive Driving World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14815)
*Junyoung Seo,Jaewoo Seo,Seungryong Kim,Laura Leal-Taixé,Xiuming Zhang,Seung Wook Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BirdDreams是一种自回归驾驶世界模型，联合生成透视视频与鸟瞰图（BEV）示意视频，无需高清地图或车辆状态输入即可实现精确的自车轨迹控制与合理的周围车辆运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅从透视视频学习车辆运动困难，因为运动与外观纠缠且车辆会被遮挡；已有工作依赖指定车辆运动的布局作为条件，但需要未来轨迹作为输入。

Method: BirdDreams以自回归方式同时生成透视视频和由场景标注渲染的固定尺度BEV视频；两个视频扩散Transformer通过联合注意力互相传递信息；利用预测BEV的最新帧沿输入自车轨迹平移旋转，作为软条件实现几何自车控制；推理时仅需单张相机图像。

Result: 在PhysicalAI-AV和nuScenes上，BirdDreams比先前驾驶世界模型更忠实地跟随输入自车轨迹，并在无自车动作条件下生成更合理的自车运动；人工评估显示其在生成真实周围车辆行为上优于仅透视基线。

Conclusion: 将BEV布局纳入预测未来并作为几何控制接口，可有效提升驾驶世界模型的轨迹可控性与周围车辆运动真实性，且无需HD地图或车辆状态输入。

Abstract: Driving world models aim to simulate how a scene unfolds under different ego actions. This requires plausible motion of surrounding vehicles as well as precise ego-trajectory control. Learning vehicle motion from perspective video alone is challenging because motion is entangled with appearance and vehicles can be occluded. Prior work therefore conditions video generation on layouts that specify vehicle motion, providing control over surrounding vehicles but requiring their future trajectories as inputs. We introduce BirdDreams, an autoregressive driving world model that jointly generates perspective video and a schematic bird's-eye-view (BEV) video, making scene layout part of the predicted future. The BEV, rendered from scene annotations for training, depicts roads and vehicles as simple shapes at a fixed metric scale, providing a top-down view of their positions and motion with reduced occlusion between vehicles. Perspective video, by contrast, retains scene details that the BEV omits, such as traffic signals and object appearance. Joint attention between two video diffusion transformers, one per stream, lets each stream inform the other during generation. The predicted BEV also provides a geometric interface for ego control: we translate and rotate its latest frame along an input ego trajectory and use the resulting frames as a soft condition that shows how the scene would appear to move if only the ego vehicle moved. At inference, BirdDreams generates both videos from a single camera image without requiring an HD map or vehicle-state inputs. On PhysicalAI-AV and nuScenes, BirdDreams follows input ego trajectories more faithfully than prior driving world models and generates more plausible ego motion without ego-action conditioning. Human evaluation shows that BirdDreams outperforms a perspective-only baseline in generating realistic surrounding-vehicle behavior.

</details>


### [122] [Where to Generate Touch: Do Image Latents Preserve Material Recognition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15834)
*Wanghao Ye,Aarosh Das,Sihan Chen,Yiting Wang,Bowei Tian,Guoheng Sun,Shwai He,Zheyu Shen,Ziyao Wang,Yexiao He,Zhaoyi Liu,Meng Liu,Yuning Zhang,Meng Feng,Ziyi Wang,Yilong Dai,Yifei Dong,Siyuan Peng,Zhenle Duan,Joshua Liu,Lang Xiong,Daeun Jung,Aditya Sarkar,Parastoo Pilevar,Xiaochuan Gong,Yancheng Zhu,Yingying Fan,Qiong Wang,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过比较不同图像模型重建的触觉数据对材料分类器的影响，选择SD-VAE潜空间用于触觉生成，并训练了照片到触觉的生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成触觉可以扩展机器人学习超越已记录接触的范围，而触觉生成器需要合适的潜空间，因此需要系统评估不同预训练图像模型的潜空间对触觉任务的适用性。

Method: 在ObjectFolder Real等数据集上，用真实GelSight按压训练材料分类器，测试其在三种16x离散tokenizer和Stable Diffusion VAE（SD-VAE）编码-解码重建上的性能；并在Touch and Go和DIGIT物体识别（YCB-Slide）上验证；还测试了在重建上重新训练分类器（无新标签）的效果以及物体身份保持能力。

Result: 在ObjectFolder Real上，分类器在三种16x离散tokenizer重建上损失6.5至9.7个百分点，在SD-VAE重建上仅损失0.3个百分点；16x tokenizer在Touch and Go和YCB-Slide上损失更大；在重建上重新训练可缩小离散tokenizer的差距；SD-VAE重建保持物体身份，物体匹配准确率76.7%，对比真实触觉78.9%和MAGVIT-v2的67.0%。

Conclusion: 选择SD-VAE潜空间，并展示了在该潜空间中训练的照片到触觉生成器，代码将在接收后发布。

Abstract: Generated touch could extend robot learning beyond recorded contacts. A tactile generator needs a latent space, and we choose one by testing whether a material classifier trained on real GelSight presses still works on encode-decode reconstructions from pretrained image models. On ObjectFolder Real, the classifier loses 6.5 to 9.7 points on reconstructions from three 16x discrete tokenizers and 0.3 points on those from the Stable Diffusion VAE (SD-VAE). The 16x tokenizers again lose far more on Touch and Go and on DIGIT object recognition (YCB-Slide). Retraining the classifier on reconstructions, with no new labels, narrows the discrete gap. SD-VAE reconstructions also keep object identity, with 76.7% object-matching accuracy against 78.9% for real touch and 67.0% for MAGVIT-v2. We therefore select SD-VAE latents and show a photo-to-touch generator trained in them. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [123] [HeadIO: Body-Frame Heading Decomposition for Neural Inertial Odometry on Head-Mounted Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14895)
*Taeyoung Yeon,Yiquan Li,Chenfeng Gao,Vasco Xu,Karan Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeadIO 提出体坐标系航向分解与双向 Mamba 主干结合的头戴式惯性里程计，实现倾斜不变性和偏航等变性，在多个数据集上以更低计算量达到最优 RTE-5s 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有惯性里程计在世界坐标系下运行，不具备偏航等变性；偏航增强提升鲁棒性但不保证等变性，学习等变层虽可实现但计算成本高；原始体坐标系 IMU 输入虽偏航不变，但头部倾斜会使重力和运动信号在设备轴上重新分布，导致表示具有倾斜变异性。

Method: 提出 HeadIO，将体坐标系航向分解与双向 Mamba 主干网络结合；分解仅利用设备姿态的倾斜分量分离重力对齐分量与水平分量，从而实现倾斜不变性；并在四个头戴式数据集上评估，分析倾斜精度、陀螺仪偏航校正及稀疏外部偏航校正的影响。

Result: 在最大基准 Nymeria 上，RTE-5s 较带学习等变层的世界坐标系方法提升 8.1%（0.260 m 至 0.239 m），FLOPs 降低近 3 倍、参数量减少 8 倍；在所有 Aria-glasses 数据集上 RTE-5s 达到最优；仅 IMU 部署场景下仍保持最优；单系数陀螺仪校正消除 11.6% 偏航误差并提升 RTE-5s 2.5%，每 30 秒一次稀疏外部偏航校正可提升 12.0%。

Conclusion: HeadIO 的分解轻量、可与任意主干组合、适合头戴设备实时部署，主要依赖可从重力信号获得的倾斜精度，并在偏航校正下进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Inertial odometry (IO) using only Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) enables camera-free head tracking in head-mounted devices such as VR headsets, AR glasses, and earbuds. Prior IO methods operate in a gravity-aligned world frame, which is not yaw-equivariant. Yaw augmentation improves robustness but does not guarantee equivariance; learned equivariant layers achieve it, but EqNIO's full pipeline costs nearly 3$\times$ the FLOPs of ours. While raw body-frame IMU inputs are inherently yaw-invariant, head tilt redistributes both gravity and motion signals across device axes, making the representation tilt-variant. We propose HeadIO, which combines a body-frame heading decomposition with a bidirectional Mamba backbone for head-mounted inertial odometry. Our decomposition achieves tilt invariance by separating gravity-aligned and horizontal components using only the tilt component of device orientation. We evaluate on four head-mounted datasets and find that HeadIO improves RTE-5s by 8.1% (0.260 m $\to$ 0.239 m) over world-frame methods with learned equivariant layers on Nymeria, the largest benchmark dataset, at nearly 3$\times$ lower FLOPs and 8$\times$ fewer parameters. HeadIO achieves state-of-the-art performance on our primary metric, RTE-5s, on all Aria-glasses datasets, which natively provide body-frame semantics for our decomposition method. We further evaluate the IMU-only deployment scenario on Nymeria, where HeadIO remains state-of-the-art. We show that our method relies mainly on tilt accuracy, which can be obtained from the gravity signal; for yaw, a single-coefficient gyroscope correction removes 11.6% of the yaw error and improves RTE-5s by 2.5%, and sparse external yaw corrections (for example, one every 30 s) can improve RTE-5s by 12.0%. Our decomposition is lightweight, composes with any backbone, and is suitable for real-time deployment on head-mounted devices.

</details>


### [124] [Prediction or Persistence? What Future-Prediction Scores Miss About World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15082)
*Animesh Maheshwari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 未来预测分数混淆了编码器质量、近期相似性和预测器贡献；通过配对持续性控制分离三者，发现预测器贡献在不同模型间差异巨大，甚至为负，建议报告分数时同时报告持续性基线及其差值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频世界模型常被未来预测分数选作机器人学习组件，但该分数无法单独作为预测证据，因为它混合了编码器质量、近期与过去的相似性以及预测器本身的贡献，需要一种方法来区分这三者。

Method: 从家庭自我中心视频构建27,000个未来检索试验，模型需从同一视频的11个候选（随机概率0.091）中选出真实后续片段，按各自训练准则零样本排序；每个模型配对一个相同的持续性控制（预测器从不调用），通过配对差异隔离预测器贡献。

Result: 在原生视频V-JEPA家族内，分数与贡献分离：V-JEPA 2.1预测器提升+0.291 top-1，而V-JEPA 2在相同试验上仅提升+0.03至+0.04；五个模型中贡献范围从+0.291到负值，负值仅出现在两个动作条件模型（在其训练分布外评估）；持续性单独恢复与预训练一致的编码器排序，表明探针追踪真实信号。

Conclusion: 应同时报告未来预测分数及其持续性基线，两者差值而非分数本身使数字可解释（仅需一次前向传播的必要检查）；下游实用性需通过规划或控制评估另行确立。

Abstract: Pretrained video world models are compared and chosen as components for robot learning largely by future-prediction scores. On its own, such a score cannot be read as evidence of prediction, because it mixes encoder quality, the near future's resemblance to the recent past, and the predictor's own contribution. We separate the three with a matched control. On 27,000 future-retrieval trials from household egocentric video, a model must pick the true continuation of a clip among eleven candidates from the same video (chance 0.091), ranked zero-shot by its own training criterion. Each model is paired with a persistence control, an identical copy whose predictor is never invoked, so the paired difference isolates the predictor. Score and contribution dissociate within the native-video V-JEPA family alone: the predictor adds +0.291 top-1 for V-JEPA 2.1 but +0.03 to +0.04 for V-JEPA 2 on the identical trials. Across five models the contribution ranges from +0.291 to negative, and it is negative only for the two action-conditioned models, which we evaluate outside their training regime. Persistence alone recovers the encoder ordering expected from pretraining, a sign that the probe tracks real signal. We suggest reporting each future-prediction score together with its persistence baseline. The gap between them, not the score, is what makes the number interpretable: a necessary check costing one forward pass. Establishing downstream usefulness is a separate matter, for a planning or control evaluation.

</details>
